```ts
import type { PhotoType } from "@/lib/types";

// The photos the server takes, and how the browser shrinks them (selling spec 2.3). Plain values, safe anywhere,
// the photo field's client code included.

/** The types, known by their signature (lib/photos/signature.ts). The file input asks for the same ones. */
export const PHOTO_TYPES: readonly PhotoType[] = ["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"];
export const PHOTO_ACCEPT = PHOTO_TYPES.join(",");
/** Each photo, as it arrives: 5 MB. */
export const PHOTO_MAX_MB = 5;
export const PHOTO_MAX_BYTES = PHOTO_MAX_MB * 1024 * 1024;
/** Photos per listing, the cover included. */
export const PHOTOS_PER_LISTING = 6;
/** The browser's re-encode: the longest side in pixels, and the WebP (or JPEG) quality. */
export const SHRINK_MAX_SIDE = 1600;
export const SHRINK_QUALITY = 0.8;
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the signature check**

Create `lib/photos/signature.ts`:

```ts
import type { PhotoType } from "@/lib/types";

const startsWith = (bytes: Uint8Array, at: number, expected: readonly number[]) =>
  bytes.length >= at + expected.length && expected.every((byte, index) => bytes[at + index] === byte);

const JPEG = [0xff, 0xd8, 0xff];
const PNG = [0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a];
const RIFF = [0x52, 0x49, 0x46, 0x46];
const WEBP = [0x57, 0x45, 0x42, 0x50];

/**
 * A photo's real type from its first bytes (selling spec 2.3), whatever its name or the browser says it is. Null for
 * anything else: GIF, SVG, HEIC, or a file pretending.
 */
export function photoTypeOf(bytes: Uint8Array): PhotoType | null {
  if (startsWith(bytes, 0, JPEG)) return "image/jpeg";
  if (startsWith(bytes, 0, PNG)) return "image/png";
  if (startsWith(bytes, 0, RIFF) && startsWith(bytes, 8, WEBP)) return "image/webp";
  return null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the stripper**

Create `lib/photos/strip-metadata.ts`:

```ts
import type { PhotoType } from "@/lib/types";

/**
 * A photo without its hidden data (selling spec 2.3, plan decision 11). Location, camera details, times, names and
 * comments sit in their own segments or chunks, which are dropped whole; the image data is copied as it is, so
 * nothing is decoded and no image library is needed (decision 59). The browser re-encodes photos when the app's
 * JavaScript runs, and this still runs on every upload. A file whose structure doesn't add up gives null, and the
 * caller refuses it.
 */
export function stripMetadata(bytes: Uint8Array, type: PhotoType): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> | null {
  switch (type) {
    case "image/jpeg":
      return stripJpeg(bytes);
    case "image/png":
      return stripPng(bytes);
    case "image/webp":
      return stripWebp(bytes);
  }
}

function concat(parts: readonly Uint8Array[]): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> {
  const out = new Uint8Array(parts.reduce((total, part) => total + part.length, 0));
  let at = 0;
  for (const part of parts) {
    out.set(part, at);
    at += part.length;
  }
  return out;
}

const code = (bytes: Uint8Array, at: number) =>
  String.fromCharCode(bytes[at], bytes[at + 1], bytes[at + 2], bytes[at + 3]);
const uint32be = (bytes: Uint8Array, at: number) =>
  ((bytes[at] << 24) | (bytes[at + 1] << 16) | (bytes[at + 2] << 8) | bytes[at + 3]) >>> 0;
const uint32le = (bytes: Uint8Array, at: number) =>
  (bytes[at] | (bytes[at + 1] << 8) | (bytes[at + 2] << 16) | (bytes[at + 3] << 24)) >>> 0;

// --- JPEG ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// SOI (FF D8), then segments: FF (after any number of FF fill bytes), a marker, and for most markers a 2-byte
// big-endian length that counts itself, then the data. After each SOS segment comes the scan's compressed data, in
// which FF appears only as FF 00 or a restart marker (FF D0 to D7); the next other marker ends it. EOI (FF D9) ends
// the image, and anything after it (a phone's appended video, say) is dropped.
// Dropped: APP1 (Exif, XMP and extended XMP), APP13 (Photoshop's, where IPTC keeps a place) and COM (comments).
// Kept: everything else, such as APP0 (JFIF), APP2 (the colour profile), APP14 (Adobe's colour transform), the
// tables, the frame and the scans.

const JPEG_DROPPED = new Set([0xe1, 0xed, 0xfe]);
const isRestart = (marker: number) => marker >= 0xd0 && marker <= 0xd7;

function stripJpeg(bytes: Uint8Array): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> | null {
  if (bytes.length < 4 || bytes[0] !== 0xff || bytes[1] !== 0xd8) return null;
  const parts: Uint8Array[] = [bytes.subarray(0, 2)];
  let at = 2;
  for (;;) {
    if (at >= bytes.length || bytes[at] !== 0xff) return null;
    while (at + 1 < bytes.length && bytes[at + 1] === 0xff) at++;
    if (at + 1 >= bytes.length) return null;
    const marker = bytes[at + 1];
    if (marker === 0xd9) {
      parts.push(bytes.subarray(at, at + 2));
      return concat(parts);
    }
    if (marker === 0x00 || marker === 0xd8) return null;
    if (marker === 0x01 || isRestart(marker)) {
      parts.push(bytes.subarray(at, at + 2));
      at += 2;
      continue;
    }
    if (at + 3 >= bytes.length) return null;
    const end = at + 2 + ((bytes[at + 2] << 8) | bytes[at + 3]);
    if (end < at + 4 || end > bytes.length) return null;
    if (!JPEG_DROPPED.has(marker)) parts.push(bytes.subarray(at, end));
    at = end;
    if (marker !== 0xda) continue;
    // The scan's compressed data, up to the next marker that isn't a stuffed FF 00 or a restart.
    const start = at;
    while (at + 1 < bytes.length && !(bytes[at] === 0xff && bytes[at + 1] !== 0x00 && !isRestart(bytes[at + 1]))) {
      at++;
    }
    if (at + 1 >= bytes.length) return null;
    parts.push(bytes.subarray(start, at));
  }
}

// --- PNG ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// The 8-byte signature, then chunks: a 4-byte big-endian length, a 4-letter type, the data and a CRC. IEND ends the
// image, and anything after it is dropped. Dropped: eXIf (Exif) and the text chunks tEXt, zTXt and iTXt (where XMP
// and comments live). Kept: every other chunk, the image data among them.

const PNG_DROPPED = new Set(["eXIf", "tEXt", "zTXt", "iTXt"]);

function stripPng(bytes: Uint8Array): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> | null {
  const parts: Uint8Array[] = [bytes.subarray(0, 8)];
  let at = 8;
  while (at + 12 <= bytes.length) {
    const end = at + 12 + uint32be(bytes, at);
    if (end > bytes.length) return null;
    const type = code(bytes, at + 4);
    if (!PNG_DROPPED.has(type)) parts.push(bytes.subarray(at, end));
    if (type === "IEND") return concat(parts);
    at = end;
  }
  return null;
}

// --- WebP ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// "RIFF", the size of what follows (4 bytes, little-endian), "WEBP", then chunks: a 4-letter code, a 4-byte
// little-endian size, the data, and a padding byte when the size is odd. Dropped: EXIF and "XMP ", with their flags
// cleared in VP8X's first byte (EXIF 0x08, XMP 0x04), and anything after the RIFF. The RIFF size is worked out again.

const WEBP_DROPPED = new Set(["EXIF", "XMP "]);

function stripWebp(bytes: Uint8Array): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> | null {
  if (bytes.length < 12) return null;
  const end = 8 + uint32le(bytes, 4);
  if (end < 12 || end > bytes.length) return null;
  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
  let at = 12;
  while (at + 8 <= end) {
    const size = uint32le(bytes, at + 4);
    const chunkEnd = at + 8 + size + (size % 2);
    if (chunkEnd > end) return null;
    const chunk = code(bytes, at);
    if (chunk === "VP8X" && size >= 1) {
      const header = bytes.slice(at, chunkEnd);
      header[8] &= ~(0x08 | 0x04);
      chunks.push(header);
    } else if (!WEBP_DROPPED.has(chunk)) {
      chunks.push(bytes.subarray(at, chunkEnd));
    }
    at = chunkEnd;
  }
  if (at !== end) return null;
  const body = concat(chunks);
  const size = body.length + 4;
  const sizeBytes = Uint8Array.of(size & 0xff, (size >>> 8) & 0xff, (size >>> 16) & 0xff, (size >>> 24) & 0xff);
  return concat([bytes.subarray(0, 4), sizeBytes, bytes.subarray(8, 12), body]);
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  checkAbout,
  checkListing,
  checkShopName,
  checkSlug,
  checkTagline,
  cleanLine,
  nameKey,
  nextState,
  parsePrice,
  slugify,
  type ListingInput,
} from "./rules";

const MIDRAND = { categorySlugs: ["tech", "tutoring"], pickupPointIds: ["pp-midrand-library", "pp-midrand-reception"] };
const ONLINE = { categorySlugs: ["tech", "tutoring"], pickupPointIds: [] };

const item: ListingInput = {
  kind: "item",
  title: "  Casio   calculator ",
  categorySlug: "tech",
  condition: "like-new",
  price: "R1 200",
  free: false,
  priceIsFrom: true,
  payMode: "cash",
  swapFor: "ignored",
  description: "Works perfectly.\r\nWith its cover.",
  handover: "pickup",
  pickupPointId: "pp-midrand-library",
};

describe("one line of text", () => {
  it("collapses spaces and line breaks, and drops control characters", () => {
    expect(cleanLine("  Tech \n\t corner\u0000 ")).toBe("Tech corner");
  });
});

describe("shops (selling spec 2.6 and 2.11, plan decision 16)", () => {
  it("compares names without case, accents or punctuation", () => {
    expect(nameKey("Tech Corner!")).toBe("tech corner");
    expect(nameKey("Zoë's  café")).toBe("zoe s cafe");
    expect(nameKey("tech-corner")).toBe(nameKey("TECH CORNER"));
  });

  it("makes a link from a name", () => {
    expect(slugify("Crochet corner")).toBe("crochet-corner");
    expect(slugify("Zoë's Café & Bakes!")).toBe("zoes-cafe-bakes");
    expect(slugify(`${"a".repeat(50)} shop`)).toBe("a".repeat(40));
    expect(slugify("!!!")).toBe("shop");
  });

  it("takes a name of 2 to 60 characters once tidied", () => {
    expect(checkShopName("  Tech   corner ")).toEqual({ value: "Tech corner", error: null });
    expect(checkShopName(" ").error).toBe("name-missing");
    expect(checkShopName("T").error).toBe("name-length");
    expect(checkShopName("Te").error).toBeNull();
    expect(checkShopName("a".repeat(60)).error).toBeNull();
    expect(checkShopName("a".repeat(61)).error).toBe("name-length");
  });

  it("lets the tagline be empty, and up to 80 characters", () => {
    expect(checkTagline("")).toEqual({ value: "", error: null });
    expect(checkTagline("a".repeat(80)).error).toBeNull();
    expect(checkTagline("a".repeat(81)).error).toBe("tagline-length");
  });

  it("keeps the about text's line breaks, up to 600 characters", () => {
    expect(checkAbout("I test everything.\r\nAsk me anything.")).toEqual({
      value: "I test everything.\nAsk me anything.",
      error: null,
    });
    expect(checkAbout(" ").error).toBe("about-missing");
    expect(checkAbout("a".repeat(601)).error).toBe("about-length");
  });

  it("takes a typed link of 3 to 40 lower-case letters, digits and single hyphens", () => {
    expect(checkSlug(" Tech-Corner ")).toEqual({ value: "tech-corner", error: null });
    for (const bad of ["tc", "tech--corner", "-tech", "tech-", "tech corner", "tëch", "a".repeat(41)]) {
      expect(checkSlug(bad).error, bad).toBe("slug-format");
    }
  });
});

describe("prices (plan decision 26)", () => {
  it("reads whole rand however it's written", () => {
    expect(parsePrice("250")).toBe(250);
    expect(parsePrice("R1 200")).toBe(1200);
    expect(parsePrice(" r 1,200 ")).toBe(1200);
    for (const bad of ["", "12.50", "R", "two hundred", "-5"]) expect(parsePrice(bad), bad).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("the listing form's rules (selling spec 2.10, plan decision 26)", () => {
  it("tidies a good item and keeps only what applies to it", () => {
    expect(checkListing(item, MIDRAND)).toEqual({
      value: {
        kind: "item",
        title: "Casio calculator",
        categorySlug: "tech",
        condition: "like-new",
        priceZar: 1200,
        priceIsFrom: false,
        payMode: "cash",
        swapFor: null,
        description: "Works perfectly.\nWith its cover.",
        handover: "pickup",
        pickupPointId: "pp-midrand-library",
      },
      errors: [],
    });
  });

  it("lets a service have a starting price and no condition", () => {
    const service = { ...item, kind: "service", handover: "on-campus-or-online", pickupPointId: "" };
    expect(checkListing(service, MIDRAND).value).toMatchObject({
      condition: null,
      priceIsFrom: true,
      handover: "on-campus-or-online",
      pickupPointId: null,
    });
  });

  it("makes a free listing R0 and cash, and refuses a free swap", () => {
    expect(checkListing({ ...item, free: true, price: "" }, MIDRAND).value).toMatchObject({
      priceZar: 0,
      payMode: "cash",
      priceIsFrom: false,
    });
    expect(checkListing({ ...item, free: true, payMode: "both", swapFor: "a kettle" }, MIDRAND).errors).toEqual([
      "free-swap",
    ]);
  });

  it("gives a swap-only listing no price, and asks what a swap would be for", () => {
    expect(checkListing({ ...item, payMode: "swap", price: "", swapFor: " a kettle " }, MIDRAND).value).toMatchObject({
      priceZar: null,
      payMode: "swap",
      swapFor: "a kettle",
    });
    expect(checkListing({ ...item, payMode: "both", swapFor: "" }, MIDRAND).errors).toEqual(["swap-missing"]);
    expect(checkListing({ ...item, payMode: "both", swapFor: "a".repeat(201) }, MIDRAND).errors).toEqual([
      "swap-length",
    ]);
  });

  it("lists every problem with an empty form, in the form's order", () => {
    const empty: ListingInput = {
      kind: "",
      title: "",
      categorySlug: "",
      condition: "",
      price: "",
      free: false,
      priceIsFrom: false,
      payMode: "",
      swapFor: "",
      description: "",
      handover: "",
      pickupPointId: "",
    };
    expect(checkListing(empty, MIDRAND)).toEqual({
      value: null,
      errors: [
        "kind-missing",
        "title-missing",
        "category-missing",
        "price-missing",
        "pay-missing",
        "description-missing",
        "handover-missing",
      ],
    });
  });

  it("checks the limits and the choices", () => {
    expect(checkListing({ ...item, title: "a".repeat(71) }, MIDRAND).errors).toEqual(["title-length"]);
    expect(checkListing({ ...item, categorySlug: "cars" }, MIDRAND).errors).toEqual(["category-missing"]);
    expect(checkListing({ ...item, condition: "broken" }, MIDRAND).errors).toEqual(["condition-missing"]);
    expect(checkListing({ ...item, price: "0" }, MIDRAND).errors).toEqual(["price-invalid"]);
    expect(checkListing({ ...item, price: "100001" }, MIDRAND).errors).toEqual(["price-high"]);
    expect(checkListing({ ...item, description: "a".repeat(1001) }, MIDRAND).errors).toEqual(["description-length"]);
  });

  it("needs one of the shop's own spots for a pickup, and offers a shop without spots no pickup", () => {
    expect(checkListing({ ...item, pickupPointId: "pp-pretoria-library" }, MIDRAND).errors).toEqual(["pickup-missing"]);
    expect(checkListing(item, ONLINE).errors).toEqual(["handover-missing"]);
    expect(checkListing({ ...item, handover: "online" }, ONLINE).value).toMatchObject({
      handover: "online",
      pickupPointId: null,
    });
  });
});

describe("what Save and publish does (plan decision 3)", () => {
  it("publishes a new listing or a draft, keeps a paused or sold one as it is, and Save as draft always drafts", () => {
    expect(nextState(null, "publish")).toBe("available");
    expect(nextState("draft", "publish")).toBe("available");
    expect(nextState("available", "publish")).toBe("available");
    expect(nextState("paused", "publish")).toBe("paused");
    expect(nextState("sold", "publish")).toBe("sold");
    expect(nextState("available", "draft")).toBe("draft");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { listingFieldIds, listingFieldPrefix, sellingErrors, shopFieldIds } from "./errors";

describe("selling's error words (plan decisions 24 and 27)", () => {
  it("words a listing's errors and links each to its field", () => {
    expect(sellingErrors(["title-missing", "price-high", "pickup-missing", "photo-size"], listingFieldIds("new"))).toEqual([
      { message: "Give your listing a title.", fieldId: "new-title" },
      { message: "Keep the price under R100,000.", fieldId: "new-price" },
      { message: "Choose one of your pickup points.", fieldId: "new-pickup" },
      { message: "Each photo must be 5 MB or less.", fieldId: "new-photos" },
    ]);

    ]);

    ]);

    expect(listingFieldIds("edit-l-001").title).toBe("edit-l-001-title");

});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  deleteListingPath,
  editListingPath,
  myListingsPath,
  onboardingPath,
  parseListingsTab,
  parseStep,
  tabFor,
} from "./paths";

describe("selling's addresses (selling spec 2.2 and 3)", () => {
  it("puts the wizard's step in the link, and reads it back safely", () => {
    expect(onboardingPath(1)).toBe("/sell/onboarding");
    expect(onboardingPath(4)).toBe("/sell/onboarding?step=4");
    expect(parseStep("3")).toBe(3);
    expect(parseStep(["2", "4"])).toBe(2);
    for (const bad of [undefined, "", "0", "6", "2.5", "two", ["9"]]) expect(parseStep(bad)).toBe(1);
  });

  it("reads My listings' tab, and links to each", () => {
    expect(parseListingsTab("drafts")).toBe("drafts");
    expect(parseListingsTab("nope")).toBe("available");
    expect(parseListingsTab(undefined)).toBe("available");
    expect(myListingsPath()).toBe("/sell/listings");
    expect(myListingsPath("sold")).toBe("/sell/listings?tab=sold");
  });

  it("knows which tab each state belongs to", () => {
    expect([tabFor("available"), tabFor("paused"), tabFor("sold"), tabFor("draft")]).toEqual([
      "available",
      "paused",
      "sold",
      "drafts",
    ]);
  });

  it("links to a listing's form and its delete page", () => {
    expect(editListingPath("l-001")).toBe("/sell/listings/l-001/edit");
    expect(deleteListingPath("l-001")).toBe("/sell/listings/l-001/delete");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { applyPhotoMove, parsePhotoMove, photoOrder, resolveToken } from "./photo-order";

const A = "/photos/ph-aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa";
const B = "/photos/ph-bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb";
const C = "/placeholders/listing/tech.svg";

describe("photo moves (selling spec 2.5 and section 4, plan decision 9)", () => {
  it("reads a button's value: the photo, then what to do", () => {
    expect(parsePhotoMove(`${A}:cover`)).toEqual({ token: A, op: "cover" });
    expect(parsePhotoMove("new:2:later")).toEqual({ token: "new:2", op: "later" });
    for (const bad of ["", ":cover", `${A}:jump`, A]) expect(parsePhotoMove(bad), bad).toBeNull();
  });

  it("moves a photo earlier or later, makes it the cover, or removes it", () => {
    const order = [A, B, C];
    expect(applyPhotoMove(order, { token: B, op: "earlier" })).toEqual([B, A, C]);
    expect(applyPhotoMove(order, { token: B, op: "later" })).toEqual([A, C, B]);
    expect(applyPhotoMove(order, { token: C, op: "cover" })).toEqual([C, A, B]);
    expect(applyPhotoMove(order, { token: A, op: "remove" })).toEqual([B, C]);
  });

  it("does nothing past either end, or for a photo that isn't there", () => {
    expect(applyPhotoMove([A, B], { token: A, op: "earlier" })).toEqual([A, B]);
    expect(applyPhotoMove([A, B], { token: B, op: "later" })).toEqual([A, B]);
    expect(applyPhotoMove([A, B], { token: C, op: "cover" })).toEqual([A, B]);
  });
});

describe("the photos' final order (plan decision 9)", () => {
  it("follows the posted order, turning new:<n> into the n-th stored upload", () => {
    expect(photoOrder(["new:1", A, "new:0"], new Set([A]), [B, C])).toEqual({ order: [C, A, B], tooMany: false });
  });

  it("drops what isn't allowed or was refused, adds uploads no token named, and never repeats one", () => {
    const stranger = "/photos/ph-cccccccc-cccc-4ccc-8ccc-cccccccccccc";
    expect(photoOrder([stranger, A, A, "new:0", "new:9"], new Set([A]), [null, B])).toEqual({
      order: [A, B],
      tooMany: false,
    });
  });

  it("keeps 6 at most, and says when there were more", () => {
    const seven = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, n) => `/photos/ph-${String(n).repeat(8)}-0000-4000-8000-000000000000`);
    expect(photoOrder(seven, new Set(seven), [])).toEqual({ order: seven.slice(0, 6), tooMany: true });
  });

  it("turns a token into the address it stands for", () => {
    expect(resolveToken("new:1", [A, B])).toBe(B);
    expect(resolveToken("new:5", [A])).toBeNull();
    expect(resolveToken(C, [A])).toBe(C);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { EnquiryCounts } from "@/lib/types";
import { badgeProgress, replyRate } from "./badges";

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-14T08:00:00.000Z");
const OLD_ACCOUNT = "2026-03-02T09:15:00.000Z";

const counts = (enquiries: number, due: number, repliedInTime: number, salesConfirmed: number): EnquiryCounts => ({
  enquiries,
  due,
  repliedInTime,
  salesConfirmed,
});

describe("reply rates (the trust page)", () => {
  it("is the share of due enquiries answered in time, or null while none is due", () => {
    expect(replyRate(counts(4, 4, 3, 0))).toBe(0.75);
    expect(replyRate(counts(2, 0, 0, 0))).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("what the next badge needs (selling spec 2.8, plan decision 18)", () => {
  it("counts what's missing for Trusted, as the spec's example does", () => {
    expect(badgeProgress("responsive", counts(2, 2, 2, 0), OLD_ACCOUNT, NOW)).toBe(
      "1 more enquiry and 5 more confirmed sales for Trusted.",
    );
    expect(badgeProgress("responsive", counts(4, 4, 4, 3), OLD_ACCOUNT, NOW)).toBe("2 more confirmed sales for Trusted.");
  });

  it("starts a new seller on Responsive", () => {
    expect(badgeProgress("new-seller", counts(0, 0, 0, 0), OLD_ACCOUNT, NOW)).toBe("3 more enquiries for Responsive.");
    expect(badgeProgress(null, counts(1, 1, 1, 0), OLD_ACCOUNT, NOW)).toBe("2 more enquiries for Responsive.");
  });

  it("says when replies are too slow, with the rate now", () => {
    expect(badgeProgress("new-seller", counts(4, 4, 3, 0), OLD_ACCOUNT, NOW)).toBe(
      "80% of enquiries answered within 48 hours (now 75%) for Responsive.",
    );
  });

  it("asks for replies when there are enough enquiries but none has come due yet", () => {
    expect(badgeProgress("new-seller", counts(3, 0, 0, 0), OLD_ACCOUNT, NOW)).toBe(
      "80% of enquiries answered within 48 hours for Responsive.",
    );
  });

  it("counts the days a new account still needs for Trusted", () => {
    expect(badgeProgress("responsive", counts(3, 3, 3, 5), "2026-10-04T08:00:00.000Z", NOW)).toBe(
      "20 more days on Vossie for Trusted.",
    );
  });

  it("says when the rules are met, and when there's nothing higher", () => {
    expect(badgeProgress("responsive", counts(5, 5, 5, 6), OLD_ACCOUNT, NOW)).toBe(
      "You meet the rules for Trusted. Your badge changes when the Hub next updates badges.",
    );
    expect(badgeProgress("trusted", counts(0, 0, 0, 0), OLD_ACCOUNT, NOW)).toBe(
      "Trusted is the highest badge. Keep replying quickly to keep it.",
    );
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the rules**

Create `lib/selling/rules.ts`:

```ts
import { conditionLabel } from "@/lib/format";
import { characterCount, cleanText, type TextCheck } from "@/lib/messaging/rules";
import type { Condition, Handover, ListingKind, ListingState, PayMode } from "@/lib/types";

// Selling's limits and text rules (selling spec 2.6, 2.10 and 2.11; FUNCTION-MAP section 6). Plain values and
// functions: safe anywhere on the server and in tests.

export const SHOP_NAME_MIN = 2;
export const SHOP_NAME_MAX = 60;
export const TAGLINE_MAX = 80;
export const ABOUT_MAX = 600;
export const TITLE_MAX = 70;
export const DESCRIPTION_MAX = 1000;
export const SWAP_FOR_MAX = 200;
/** A photo's description (plan decision 9). */
export const ALT_MAX = 125;
/** Whole rand, from R1 (plan decision 26). */
export const PRICE_MAX = 100_000;
/** A link the seller types (selling spec 2.11). */
export const SLUG_MIN = 3;
export const SLUG_MAX = 40;

/** One line of user text: line breaks and runs of spaces become one space, control characters go, the ends are trimmed. */
export function cleanLine(raw: string): string {
  return cleanText(raw).replace(/\s+/g, " ");
}

/** A shop name as names are compared (plan decision 16): no case, accents or punctuation. "Tech Corner!" is "tech corner". */
export function nameKey(name: string): string {
  return name
    .normalize("NFKD")
    .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ")
    .trim();
}

/** A shop's link made from its name (plan decision 16): "Zoë's Café" becomes "zoes-cafe". */
export function slugify(name: string): string {
  const slug = nameKey(name.replace(/['’]/g, "")).replace(/ /g, "-").slice(0, SLUG_MAX).replace(/-+$/, "");
  return slug || "shop";
}

/** A shop's name (selling spec 2.6): 2 to 60 characters, once tidied. Unique on its campus: the caller checks that. */
export function checkShopName(raw: string): TextCheck<"name-missing" | "name-length"> {
  const value = cleanLine(raw);
  if (value === "") return { value, error: "name-missing" };
  const length = characterCount(value);
  return { value, error: length < SHOP_NAME_MIN || length > SHOP_NAME_MAX ? "name-length" : null };
}

/** An optional tagline (selling spec 2.6), up to 80 characters. */
export function checkTagline(raw: string): TextCheck<"tagline-length"> {
  const value = cleanLine(raw);
  return { value, error: characterCount(value) > TAGLINE_MAX ? "tagline-length" : null };
}

/** The shop's two or three sentences (selling spec 2.6), up to 600 characters, line breaks kept. */
export function checkAbout(raw: string): TextCheck<"about-missing" | "about-length"> {
  const value = cleanText(raw);
  if (value === "") return { value, error: "about-missing" };
  return { value, error: characterCount(value) > ABOUT_MAX ? "about-length" : null };
}

const SLUG_SHAPE = /^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;

/** A new link the seller types (selling spec 2.11): 3 to 40 lower-case letters, digits and single hyphens. */
export function checkSlug(raw: string): TextCheck<"slug-format"> {
  const value = raw.trim().toLowerCase();
  const ok = SLUG_SHAPE.test(value) && value.length >= SLUG_MIN && value.length <= SLUG_MAX;
  return { value, error: ok ? null : "slug-format" };
}

/** A price as typed, in whole rand: "250", "R1 200" and "1,200" all work. Null when it isn't one. */
export function parsePrice(raw: string): number | null {
  const digits = raw.trim().replace(/^r\s*/i, "").replace(/[\s,]/g, "");
  return /^\d{1,9}$/.test(digits) ? Number(digits) : null;
}

export const KIND_CHOICES: ReadonlyArray<{ value: ListingKind; label: string }> = [
  { value: "item", label: "An item" },
  { value: "service", label: "A service" },
];

export const CONDITION_CHOICES: ReadonlyArray<{ value: Condition; label: string }> = (
  ["new", "like-new", "good", "fair"] as const
).map((value) => ({ value, label: conditionLabel(value) }));

export const PAY_CHOICES: ReadonlyArray<{ value: PayMode; label: string }> = [
  { value: "cash", label: "Cash" },
  { value: "swap", label: "Swap" },
  { value: "both", label: "Cash or swap" },
];

export const HANDOVER_CHOICES: ReadonlyArray<{ value: Handover; label: string }> = [
  { value: "pickup", label: "Pickup at one of your points" },
  { value: "on-campus", label: "On campus" },
  { value: "on-campus-or-online", label: "On campus or online" },
  { value: "online", label: "Online" },
];

function oneOf<T extends string>(choices: ReadonlyArray<{ value: T }>, raw: string): T | null {
  return choices.find((choice) => choice.value === raw)?.value ?? null;
}

/** What the listing form posts (selling spec 2.10): its text fields and ticks. */
export type ListingInput = {
  kind: string;
  title: string;
  categorySlug: string;
  condition: string;
  price: string;
  free: boolean;
  priceIsFrom: boolean;
  payMode: string;
  swapFor: string;
  description: string;
  handover: string;
  pickupPointId: string;
};

/** A checked listing's fields, as the store keeps them. */
export type ListingFields = {
  kind: ListingKind;
  title: string;
  categorySlug: string;
  condition: Condition | null;
  priceZar: number | null;
  priceIsFrom: boolean;
  payMode: PayMode;
  swapFor: string | null;
  description: string;
  handover: Handover;
  pickupPointId: string | null;
};

export type ListingError =
  | "kind-missing"
  | "title-missing"
  | "title-length"
  | "category-missing"
  | "condition-missing"
  | "price-missing"
  | "price-invalid"
  | "price-high"
  | "pay-missing"
  | "free-swap"
  | "swap-missing"
  | "swap-length"
  | "description-missing"
  | "description-length"
  | "handover-missing"
  | "pickup-missing";

/** What a listing has to fit: the categories, and its shop's pickup spots. */
export type ListingContext = { categorySlugs: readonly string[]; pickupPointIds: readonly string[] };

/**
 * The listing form's rules (selling spec 2.10, plan decision 26). The errors come in the form's order, so the error
 * summary reads top to bottom; the fields are null while any remain. Every field shows whatever the kind or pay mode
 * (nothing can hide them without JavaScript), so a field that doesn't apply is ignored rather than refused.
 */
export function checkListing(
  input: ListingInput,
  context: ListingContext,
): { value: ListingFields | null; errors: ListingError[] } {
  const errors: ListingError[] = [];

  const kind = oneOf(KIND_CHOICES, input.kind);
  if (!kind) errors.push("kind-missing");

  const title = cleanLine(input.title);
  if (title === "") errors.push("title-missing");
  else if (characterCount(title) > TITLE_MAX) errors.push("title-length");

  const categorySlug = context.categorySlugs.includes(input.categorySlug) ? input.categorySlug : null;
  if (!categorySlug) errors.push("category-missing");

  // An item needs a condition; a service never has one (the listing model).
  const condition = kind === "item" ? oneOf(CONDITION_CHOICES, input.condition) : null;
  if (kind === "item" && !condition) errors.push("condition-missing");

  // The price, unless the listing is free (R0) or swap-only (no price).
  const payMode = oneOf(PAY_CHOICES, input.payMode);
  let priceZar: number | null = input.free ? 0 : null;
  if (!input.free && payMode !== "swap") {
    const price = parsePrice(input.price);
    if (input.price.trim() === "") errors.push("price-missing");
    else if (price === null || price < 1) errors.push("price-invalid");
    else if (price > PRICE_MAX) errors.push("price-high");
    else priceZar = price;
  }
  if (!payMode) errors.push("pay-missing");
  else if (input.free && payMode !== "cash") errors.push("free-swap");

  const swaps = !input.free && (payMode === "swap" || payMode === "both");
  const swapFor = cleanLine(input.swapFor);
  if (swaps && swapFor === "") errors.push("swap-missing");
  else if (swaps && characterCount(swapFor) > SWAP_FOR_MAX) errors.push("swap-length");

  const description = cleanText(input.description);
  if (description === "") errors.push("description-missing");
  else if (characterCount(description) > DESCRIPTION_MAX) errors.push("description-length");

  // A pickup needs one of the shop's pickup points, and a shop with none (Eduvos Online) can't offer one.
  const offered = context.pickupPointIds.length > 0 ? HANDOVER_CHOICES : HANDOVER_CHOICES.slice(1);
  const handover = oneOf(offered, input.handover);
  if (!handover) errors.push("handover-missing");
  const pickupPointId =
    handover === "pickup" && context.pickupPointIds.includes(input.pickupPointId) ? input.pickupPointId : null;
  if (handover === "pickup" && !pickupPointId) errors.push("pickup-missing");

  if (errors.length > 0 || !kind || !categorySlug || !payMode || !handover) return { value: null, errors };
  return {
    value: {
      kind,
      title,
      categorySlug,
      condition,
      priceZar,
      // "This is a starting price" is for services (selling spec 2.10).
      priceIsFrom: kind === "service" && input.priceIsFrom && priceZar !== null && priceZar > 0,
      payMode,
      swapFor: swaps ? swapFor : null,
      description,
      handover,
      pickupPointId,
    },
    errors,
  };
}

/**
 * The state a save leaves a listing in (plan decision 3): Save as draft always drafts; Save and publish makes a new
 * listing or a draft available, and keeps a paused or sold one as it is (its button says Save changes).
 */
export function nextState(current: ListingState | null, intent: "publish" | "draft"): ListingState {
  if (intent === "draft") return "draft";
  return current === "paused" || current === "sold" ? current : "available";
}
```

```ts
import { formatRand } from "@/lib/format";
import { PHOTO_MAX_MB, PHOTOS_PER_LISTING } from "@/lib/photos/rules";
import { ALT_MAX, PRICE_MAX } from "./rules";

// Selling's error words (selling spec 2.6, 2.10 and 2.11; plan decisions 24 and 27: the spec gives none), each linked
// to its field, in the shape D's ErrorSummary and errorFor take.

// A Map, so a stray code such as "toString" finds nothing.
const MESSAGES: ReadonlyMap<string, { message: string; field: string }> = new Map([
  // A shop: the wizard and Edit shop.
  ["name-missing", { message: "Enter your shop's name.", field: "name" }],
  ["name-length", { message: "Your shop's name must be 2 to 60 characters.", field: "name" }],
  ["name-taken", { message: "Another shop on your campus has that name. Try another.", field: "name" }],
  ["tagline-length", { message: "Keep the tagline to 80 characters.", field: "tagline" }],
  ["about-missing", { message: "Tell buyers about your shop in two or three sentences.", field: "about" }],
  ["about-length", { message: "Keep it to 600 characters.", field: "about" }],
  ["campus-missing", { message: "Choose your campus.", field: "campus" }],
  ["points-missing", { message: "Choose at least one pickup point.", field: "points" }],
  ["points-campus", { message: "Your campus changed, so choose your pickup points there.", field: "points" }],
  ["guidelines-missing", { message: "Tick the box to say you've read the seller guidelines.", field: "guidelines" }],
  ["slug-format", { message: "Use 3 to 40 lower-case letters, numbers and single hyphens.", field: "slug" }],
  ["slug-taken", { message: "Another shop has that link. Try another.", field: "slug" }],
  // A listing, in the form's order. "category-missing" serves a shop too.
  ["kind-missing", { message: "Choose an item or a service.", field: "kind" }],
  ["title-missing", { message: "Give your listing a title.", field: "title" }],
  ["title-length", { message: "Keep the title to 70 characters.", field: "title" }],
  ["category-missing", { message: "Choose a category.", field: "category" }],
  ["condition-missing", { message: "Choose the item's condition.", field: "condition" }],
  ["price-missing", { message: "Enter a price, or tick It's free.", field: "price" }],
  ["price-invalid", { message: "Enter the price in whole rand, like 250.", field: "price" }],
  ["price-high", { message: `Keep the price under ${formatRand(PRICE_MAX)}.`, field: "price" }],
  ["pay-missing", { message: "Choose how you want to be paid.", field: "payMode" }],
  ["free-swap", { message: "A free listing can't be a swap. Choose Cash, or untick It's free.", field: "payMode" }],
  ["swap-missing", { message: "Say what you'd swap it for.", field: "swapFor" }],
  ["swap-length", { message: "Keep it to 200 characters.", field: "swapFor" }],
  ["description-missing", { message: "Describe it in a sentence or two.", field: "description" }],
  ["description-length", { message: "Keep the description to 1,000 characters.", field: "description" }],
  ["handover-missing", { message: "Choose how it changes hands.", field: "handover" }],
  ["pickup-missing", { message: "Choose one of your pickup points.", field: "pickup" }],
  // Photos: a listing's or the shop's.
  ["photo-type", { message: "Use a JPG, PNG or WebP photo.", field: "photos" }],
  ["photo-size", { message: `Each photo must be ${PHOTO_MAX_MB} MB or less.`, field: "photos" }],
  ["photo-count", { message: `You can add up to ${PHOTOS_PER_LISTING} photos.`, field: "photos" }],
  ["photo-unreadable", { message: "We couldn't read one of the photos. Try another.", field: "photos" }],
  ["alt-length", { message: `Keep each photo's description to ${ALT_MAX} characters.`, field: "photos" }],
]);

/** The words for a form's error codes, in order, each linked to its field's id when the form has that field. */
export function sellingErrors(codes: readonly string[], fieldIds: Readonly<Record<string, string>>): FormError[] {
  return codes.flatMap((code) => {
    const known = MESSAGES.get(code);
    if (!known) return [];
    return [{ message: known.message, fieldId: Object.hasOwn(fieldIds, known.field) ? fieldIds[known.field] : null }];
  });
}

/**
 * The shop form's field ids: "onb-…" in the wizard, "profile-…" on Edit shop (plan decision 23). A radio group's or
 * a list of boxes' id is its first control's, where an error's link lands.
 */
export function shopFieldIds(prefix: "onb" | "profile") {
  return {
    name: `${prefix}-name`,
    tagline: `${prefix}-tagline`,
    category: `${prefix}-category`,
    about: `${prefix}-about`,
    photos: `${prefix}-photo`,
    campus: `${prefix}-campus`,
    points: `${prefix}-points`,
    guidelines: `${prefix}-guidelines`,
    slug: `${prefix}-slug`,
  } as const;
}

/** The listing form's id prefix: "new", or "edit-<id>" while editing that listing (plan decision 23). */
export function listingFieldPrefix(listingId: string | null): string {
  return listingId ? `edit-${listingId}` : "new";
}

/** The listing form's field ids. A radio group's id is its first radio's. */
export function listingFieldIds(prefix: string) {
  return {
    kind: `${prefix}-kind`,
    title: `${prefix}-title`,
    category: `${prefix}-category`,
    condition: `${prefix}-condition`,
    price: `${prefix}-price`,
    payMode: `${prefix}-pay`,
    swapFor: `${prefix}-swap`,
    description: `${prefix}-description`,
    handover: `${prefix}-handover`,
    pickup: `${prefix}-pickup`,
    photos: `${prefix}-photos`,
  } as const;
}
```

```ts
import type { ListingsTab, ListingState } from "@/lib/types";

// Selling's addresses (selling spec section 3). The wizard's step and My listings' tab are in the link, which holds
// no personal data (spec 2.2).

export const SELL_PATH = "/sell";
export const PROFILE_PATH = "/sell/profile";
export const NEW_LISTING_PATH = "/sell/listings/new";

/** The wizard's five steps (selling spec 2.6), with the names its progress line uses. */
export const ONBOARDING_STEPS = [
  { step: 1, name: "Basics" },
  { step: 2, name: "About" },
  { step: 3, name: "Photo" },
  { step: 4, name: "Campus and pickup points" },
  { step: 5, name: "Review" },
] as const;

export type OnboardingStep = (typeof ONBOARDING_STEPS)[number]["step"];

export function onboardingPath(step: OnboardingStep): string {
  return step === 1 ? "/sell/onboarding" : `/sell/onboarding?step=${step}`;
}

/** `?step=` as a step, 1 to 5. Anything else is step 1. */
export function parseStep(raw: string | string[] | undefined): OnboardingStep {
  const step = Number(Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw);
  return step === 2 || step === 3 || step === 4 || step === 5 ? step : 1;
}

/** My listings' tabs (selling spec 2.9), in order, with the state each shows. */
export const LISTINGS_TABS: ReadonlyArray<{ tab: ListingsTab; label: string; state: ListingState }> = [
  { tab: "available", label: "Available", state: "available" },
  { tab: "paused", label: "Paused", state: "paused" },
  { tab: "sold", label: "Sold", state: "sold" },
  { tab: "drafts", label: "Drafts", state: "draft" },
];

export function parseListingsTab(raw: string | string[] | undefined): ListingsTab {
  const value = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw;
  return LISTINGS_TABS.find((item) => item.tab === value)?.tab ?? "available";
}

export function tabFor(state: ListingState): ListingsTab {
  return LISTINGS_TABS.find((item) => item.state === state)!.tab;
}

export function myListingsPath(tab: ListingsTab = "available"): string {
  return tab === "available" ? "/sell/listings" : `/sell/listings?tab=${tab}`;
}

export function editListingPath(id: string): string {
  return `/sell/listings/${id}/edit`;
}

export function deleteListingPath(id: string): string {
  return `/sell/listings/${id}/delete`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the photo order**

Create `lib/selling/photo-order.ts`:

```ts
import { PHOTOS_PER_LISTING } from "@/lib/photos/rules";

// The listing form's photo order (selling spec 2.5 and section 4, plan decision 9). The form posts one `photo` field
// per photo, in order: a saved photo's address, or "new:<n>" for the n-th file in its file input. Its buttons post
// `photoMove`, "<photo>:earlier", ":later", ":cover" or ":remove".

export type PhotoOp = "earlier" | "later" | "cover" | "remove";
export type PhotoMove = { token: string; op: PhotoOp };

const OPS: readonly string[] = ["earlier", "later", "cover", "remove"];

export function newPhotoToken(index: number): string {
  return `new:${index}`;
}

export function newPhotoIndex(token: string): number | null {
  const match = /^new:(\d{1,2})$/.exec(token);
  return match ? Number(match[1]) : null;
}

/** A button's value as a move: the photo is everything before the last colon. */
export function parsePhotoMove(raw: string): PhotoMove | null {
  const at = raw.lastIndexOf(":");
  if (at <= 0) return null;
  const op = raw.slice(at + 1);
  return OPS.includes(op) ? { token: raw.slice(0, at), op: op as PhotoOp } : null;
}

/** The order after a move. A photo that isn't there, or a move past either end, changes nothing. */
export function applyPhotoMove(order: readonly string[], move: PhotoMove): string[] {
  const from = order.indexOf(move.token);
  if (from === -1) return [...order];
  const next = order.filter((_, index) => index !== from);
  if (move.op === "remove") return next;
  const to = move.op === "cover" ? 0 : move.op === "earlier" ? Math.max(0, from - 1) : Math.min(order.length - 1, from + 1);
  next.splice(to, 0, move.token);
  return next;
}

/** A token as the address it stands for: "new:<n>" is the n-th stored upload's, or null when that one was refused. */
export function resolveToken(token: string, stored: readonly (string | null)[]): string | null {
  const index = newPhotoIndex(token);
  return index === null ? token : (stored[index] ?? null);
}

/**
 * The photos' final order (plan decision 9): each posted token that's allowed (a saved photo's address in `allowed`,
 * or an upload the server stored), then any stored upload no token named, with no repeats and at most 6.
 */
export function photoOrder(
  tokens: readonly string[],
  allowed: ReadonlySet<string>,
  stored: readonly (string | null)[],
): { order: string[]; tooMany: boolean } {
  const order: string[] = [];
  const add = (src: string | null) => {
    if (src && !order.includes(src)) order.push(src);
  };
  for (const token of tokens) {
    const src = resolveToken(token, stored);
    add(newPhotoIndex(token) !== null || (src && allowed.has(src)) ? src : null);
  }
  for (const src of stored) add(src);
  return { order: order.slice(0, PHOTOS_PER_LISTING), tooMany: order.length > PHOTOS_PER_LISTING };
}
```

Create `lib/selling/badges.ts`:

```ts
import { badgeLabel, joinWords } from "@/lib/format";
import { DAY_MS } from "@/lib/messaging/rules";
import type { EnquiryCounts, TrustBadge } from "@/lib/types";

// The trust page's badge rules (part C's /trust; FUNCTION-MAP section 6) and what the next badge still needs
// (selling spec 2.8, plan decision 18). Stage G works the badges out; this only explains the next step.

const RULES = {
  responsive: { enquiries: 3, rate: 0.8, sales: 0, days: 0 },
  trusted: { enquiries: 3, rate: 0.85, sales: 5, days: 30 },
} as const;

/** The share of due enquiries answered within 48 hours, or null while none is due. */
export function replyRate(counts: Pick<EnquiryCounts, "due" | "repliedInTime">): number | null {
  return counts.due === 0 ? null : counts.repliedInTime / counts.due;
}

const more = (n: number, one: string, many: string) => `${n} more ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;

/**
 * What the badge above `current` still needs, as one sentence: "1 more enquiry and 5 more confirmed sales for
 * Trusted." `record` is the last 90 days' enquiries and every confirmed sale; `accountCreatedAt` is the owner's.
 */
export function badgeProgress(
  current: TrustBadge | null,
  record: EnquiryCounts,
  accountCreatedAt: string,
  now: Date,
): string {
  if (current === "trusted") return "Trusted is the highest badge. Keep replying quickly to keep it.";
  const next = current === "responsive" ? "trusted" : "responsive";
  const rule = RULES[next];
  const needs: string[] = [];
  if (record.enquiries < rule.enquiries) needs.push(more(rule.enquiries - record.enquiries, "enquiry", "enquiries"));
  const rate = replyRate(record);
  // With no enquiry due yet the rate isn't known: it's only asked for once there are enough enquiries.
  if (rate !== null ? rate < rule.rate : record.enquiries >= rule.enquiries) {
    const rateNow = rate === null ? "" : ` (now ${Math.round(rate * 100)}%)`;
    needs.push(`${Math.round(rule.rate * 100)}% of enquiries answered within 48 hours${rateNow}`);
  }
  if (record.salesConfirmed < rule.sales) {
    needs.push(more(rule.sales - record.salesConfirmed, "confirmed sale", "confirmed sales"));
  }
  const days = Math.floor((now.getTime() - Date.parse(accountCreatedAt)) / DAY_MS);
  if (days < rule.days) needs.push(more(rule.days - days, "day on Vossie", "days on Vossie"));
  const label = badgeLabel(next);
  if (needs.length === 0) return `You meet the rules for ${label}. Your badge changes when the Hub next updates badges.`;
  const sentence = `${joinWords(needs)} for ${label}.`;
  return sentence[0].toUpperCase() + sentence.slice(1);
}
```

*Changed by amendment 8: the rate now is rounded down, in whole numbers.*

```ts
// What a selling page says after an action (plan decision 27). D's form state holds a code, never words, so a stale
// or made-up code shows nothing.

const NOTICES: ReadonlyMap<string, string> = new Map([
  ["published", "Published. Buyers can find it now."],
  ["saved", "Your changes are saved."],
  ["drafted", "Saved as a draft. Only you can see it."],
  ["paused", "Paused. Buyers can't see it until you resume it."],
  ["resumed", "It's available again."],
  ["sold", "Marked as sold. Its page stays up and says Sold."],
  ["deleted", "Deleted."],
  ["shop-paused", "Your shop is paused. Buyers can't see your listings until you resume it."],
  ["shop-resumed", "Your shop is live again."],
  ["shop-saved", "Your shop is saved."],
]);

export function noticeText(code: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  return code ? (NOTICES.get(code) ?? null) : null;
}
```

Read `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/04-functions/cacheTag.md` and `updateTag.md`, `01-getting-started/09-revalidating.md`, `02-guides/how-revalidation-works.md` and `02-guides/incremental-static-regeneration-cache-components.md`. Note:
- `cacheTag` works only inside a `"use cache"` scope, and throws anywhere else (including Vitest, where the directive does nothing);
- `updateTag` works only in Server Actions; it expires the tag at once, and the next request waits for fresh data;
- a page prerendered by `next build` carries the tags of the cached functions it used, so expiring a tag expires those pages too;
- an id missing from `generateStaticParams` gets the App Shell first, then its rendered page, which Next keeps.

3. Replace E's `nextCache` function and its comment with:

```ts
/**
 * Stands in for next/cache (messaging plan, Task 6; selling plan, Task 4): refresh() and updateTag() note what they
 * were asked, and the cached readers' cacheTag() and cacheLife() do nothing, as there's no cache outside Next.
 */
export function nextCache(request: FakeRequest) {
  return {
    refresh: () => {
      request.refreshed = true;
    },
    updateTag: (tag: string) => {
      request.updatedTags = [...(request.updatedTags ?? []), tag];
    },
    cacheTag: () => {},
    cacheLife: () => {},
  };
}
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { listings } from "@/data/fixtures/listings";
import { sellers } from "@/data/fixtures/sellers";
import { makeListingRow } from "@/tests/helpers/fakes";
import {
  listedListings,
  liveSellerRows,
  publicListing,
  publicListingIds,
  sellerFor,
  shopStatus,
  storefrontRow,
  toListing,
  toSeller,
} from "./catalog";
import { resetSampleStore, sampleStore } from "./store";

beforeEach(() => {
  resetSampleStore();
});

describe("the store as buyers see it (selling spec 2.1, plan decision 7)", () => {
  it("gives back every sample listing exactly as the fixture has it", () => {
    for (const listing of listings) expect(publicListing(listing.id), listing.id).toEqual(listing);
    expect(listedListings()).toEqual(listings.filter((listing) => listing.availability === "available"));
  });

  it("gives back every sample seller exactly as the fixture has it", () => {
    for (const seller of sellers) expect(sellerFor(seller.slug), seller.slug).toEqual(seller);
    expect(liveSellerRows().map((row) => row.slug)).toEqual(sellers.map((seller) => seller.slug));
  });
});

describe("which shops are open (plan decisions 1 and 2)", () => {
  it("takes a shop's status from its owner's seller profile, and opens every sample shop no one owns", () => {
    const rows = sampleStore().sellers;
    expect(shopStatus(rows.get("hair-studio")!)).toBe("approved");
    expect(shopStatus(rows.get("tech-corner")!)).toBe("approved");
    expect(shopStatus(rows.get("crochet-corner")!)).toBe("pending");
    sampleStore().sellerProfiles.get("acc-seller")!.status = "paused";
    expect(shopStatus(rows.get("tech-corner")!)).toBe("paused");
    // An owned shop whose profile has gone shows nowhere.
    sampleStore().sellerProfiles.delete("acc-seller");
    expect(shopStatus(rows.get("tech-corner")!)).toBe("pending");
  });

  it("keeps a paused shop's storefront and pages, marked paused, and leaves it out of every list", () => {
    sampleStore().sellerProfiles.get("acc-seller")!.status = "paused";
    expect(storefrontRow("tech-corner")?.name).toBe("Tech corner");
    expect(publicListing("l-001")?.availability).toBe("paused");
    expect(listedListings().some((listing) => listing.sellerSlug === "tech-corner")).toBe(false);
    expect(liveSellerRows().some((row) => row.slug === "tech-corner")).toBe(false);
    expect(publicListingIds()).toContain("l-001");
  });

  it("gives a waiting shop no storefront, and its listings no pages", () => {
    sampleStore().listings.set(
      "l-crochet",
      makeListingRow({ id: "l-crochet", sellerSlug: "crochet-corner", campusSlug: "pretoria", pickupPointId: null }),
    );
    expect(storefrontRow("crochet-corner")).toBeNull();
    expect(publicListing("l-crochet")).toBeNull();
    expect(publicListingIds()).not.toContain("l-crochet");
  });

  it("keeps drafts to their seller", () => {
    sampleStore().listings.get("l-001")!.state = "draft";
    expect(publicListing("l-001")).toBeNull();
    expect(listedListings().some((listing) => listing.id === "l-001")).toBe(false);
    expect(publicListingIds()).not.toContain("l-001");
  });
});

describe("pictures (selling spec 2.5 and 2.6)", () => {
  it("shows a listing's category picture when it has no photos, and its title for an empty description", () => {
    const shop = sampleStore().sellers.get("tech-corner")!;
    expect(toListing(makeListingRow({ title: "Desk fan", categorySlug: "res-living", photos: [] }), shop)).toMatchObject({
      image: { src: "/placeholders/listing/res-living.svg", alt: "Desk fan" },
      morePhotos: [],
      badge: "responsive",
    });
    const photos = [
      { src: "/photos/ph-1", alt: "" },
      { src: "/photos/ph-2", alt: "The plug" },
    ];
    expect(toListing(makeListingRow({ title: "Desk fan", photos }), shop)).toMatchObject({
      image: { src: "/photos/ph-1", alt: "Desk fan" },
      morePhotos: [{ src: "/photos/ph-2", alt: "The plug" }],
    });
  });

  it("shows a shop's category picture when it has no photo", () => {
    expect(toSeller(sampleStore().sellers.get("crochet-corner")!)).toMatchObject({
      category: "Fashion & thrift",
      image: { src: "/placeholders/category/fashion-thrift.svg", alt: "" },
    });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the tags and the catalog**

Create `lib/data/tags.ts`:

```ts
import { cacheTag, updateTag } from "next/cache";

// The data layer's cache tags (selling spec 2.1, plan decision 6). The cached readers in index.ts call the tag
// functions inside "use cache"; Server Actions call the "changed" functions after a write, so the next request, and
// every prerendered page that read the data, reads the store again.

export const LISTINGS_TAG = "listings";
export const SELLERS_TAG = "sellers";

/** For a cached function that returns listings: a shop's status and badge change its listings too. */
export function tagListings(): void {
  cacheTag(LISTINGS_TAG, SELLERS_TAG);
}

/** For a cached function that returns only sellers. */
export function tagSellers(): void {
  cacheTag(SELLERS_TAG);
}

/** After a listing changes. Server Actions only. */
export function listingsChanged(): void {
  updateTag(LISTINGS_TAG);
}

/** After a shop changes: its seller and, through their second tag, its listings. Server Actions only. */
export function sellersChanged(): void {
  updateTag(SELLERS_TAG);
}
```

Create `lib/data/catalog.ts`:

```ts
import { categories } from "@/data/fixtures/categories";
import type { ImageRef, Listing, Seller, SellerStatus } from "@/lib/types";
import type { ListingRow, SellerRow } from "./rows";
import { sampleStore } from "./store";

// What buyers see (selling spec 2.1, plan decision 2): the store's rows as the public Listing and Seller shapes, and
// the rules for which rows show where. Plain functions over the store; lib/data/index.ts wraps them in "use cache".
// Stage G's hidden listings and declined shops join the rules here.

const CATEGORY_NAMES: ReadonlyMap<string, string> = new Map(
  categories.map((category) => [category.slug, category.name]),
);

/**
 * A shop's status (plan decision 1): its owner's seller profile's, or approved for a sample shop with no owner. An
 * owned shop whose profile has gone counts as waiting, so it shows nowhere.
 */
export function shopStatus(row: Pick<SellerRow, "ownerId">): SellerStatus {
  if (row.ownerId === null) return "approved";
  return sampleStore().sellerProfiles.get(row.ownerId)?.status ?? "pending";
}

/** Shops with a storefront: approved or paused (plan decision 2). */
const hasStorefront = (row: SellerRow) => {
  const status = shopStatus(row);
  return status === "approved" || status === "paused";
};

/** A shop without a photo shows its category's picture (selling spec 2.6, step 3). */
export function categoryPicture(categorySlug: string): ImageRef {
  return { src: `/placeholders/category/${categorySlug}.svg`, alt: "" };
}

export function toSeller(row: SellerRow): Seller {
  return {
    slug: row.slug,
    name: row.name,
    tagline: row.tagline,
    category: CATEGORY_NAMES.get(row.categorySlug) ?? "",
    categorySlug: row.categorySlug,
    campusSlug: row.campusSlug,
    verified: row.verified,
    badge: row.badge,
    about: row.about,
    since: row.since,
    image: row.image ? { ...row.image } : categoryPicture(row.categorySlug),
  };
}

/**
 * A listing as buyers see it: its shop's badge (shopping spec 6.1), its photos with the title for an empty
 * description (selling spec 2.5) or its category's picture when it has none, and paused while its shop is.
 */
export function toListing(row: ListingRow, shop: SellerRow): Listing {
  const [cover, ...more] = row.photos.map((photo) => ({ src: photo.src, alt: photo.alt || row.title }));
  const paused = row.state === "draft" || shopStatus(shop) === "paused";
  return {
    id: row.id,
    title: row.title,
    priceZar: row.priceZar,
    priceIsFrom: row.priceIsFrom,
    payMode: row.payMode,
    campusSlug: row.campusSlug,
    image: cover ?? { src: `/placeholders/listing/${row.categorySlug}.svg`, alt: row.title },
    badge: shop.badge,
    createdAt: row.createdAt,
    kind: row.kind,
    categorySlug: row.categorySlug,
    sellerSlug: row.sellerSlug,
    morePhotos: more,
    description: row.description,
    condition: row.condition,
    handover: row.handover,
    pickupPointId: row.pickupPointId,
    swapFor: row.swapFor,
    availability: paused ? "paused" : (row.state as Listing["availability"]),
  };
}

/** Every shop buyers can find, in the store's order: approved, or a sample shop with no owner. */
export function liveSellerRows(): SellerRow[] {
  return [...sampleStore().sellers.values()].filter((row) => shopStatus(row) === "approved");
}

/** A storefront (plan decision 2): an approved or paused shop's. A shop waiting for approval has none. */
export function storefrontRow(slug: string): SellerRow | null {
  const row = sampleStore().sellers.get(slug);
  return row && hasStorefront(row) ? row : null;
}

/** Any shop's seller, for a listing's page (its shop has a storefront, or the listing wouldn't have a page). */
export function sellerFor(slug: string): Seller | null {
  const row = storefrontRow(slug);
  return row ? toSeller(row) : null;
}

/** Listed (plan decision 2): available, in an approved shop. In the store's order. */
export function listedListings(): Listing[] {
  const { sellers, listings } = sampleStore();
  return [...listings.values()].flatMap((row) => {
    const shop = sellers.get(row.sellerSlug);
    return shop && row.state === "available" && shopStatus(shop) === "approved" ? [toListing(row, shop)] : [];
  });
}

/** A listing's page (plan decision 2): anything but a draft, in an approved or paused shop. */
export function publicListing(id: string): Listing | null {
  const { sellers, listings } = sampleStore();
  const row = listings.get(id);
  const shop = row ? sellers.get(row.sellerSlug) : undefined;
  if (!row || !shop || row.state === "draft" || !hasStorefront(shop)) return null;
  return toListing(row, shop);
}

/** Every listing with a page, for prerendering and the sitemap. */
export function publicListingIds(): string[] {
  return [...sampleStore().listings.keys()].filter((id) => publicListing(id) !== null);
}
```

```ts
export async function getCollectionListings(slug: string): Promise<readonly Listing[]> {
  "use cache";
  tagListings();
  const collection = collections.find((item) => item.slug === slug);
  if (!collection) return [];
  return listedListings()
    .filter((listing) => matchesRule(listing, collection.rule))
    .sort(newestFirst)
    .slice(0, COLLECTION_LIMIT);
}
```

```ts
export async function getJustListed({ campus, limit }: CampusQuery): Promise<readonly Listing[]> {
  "use cache";
  tagListings();
  return listedListings()
    .filter((listing) => campus === ALL_CAMPUSES || listing.campusSlug === campus)
    .sort(newestFirst)
    .slice(0, limit);
}
```

```ts
export async function getFeatured({ campus, day }: { campus: CampusChoice; day: string }): Promise<readonly Seller[]> {
  "use cache";
  tagSellers();
  // Only open shops take part (plan decision 2). Stage G adds pins, the week's break and "something listed".
  return drawFeatured(liveSellerRows().map(toSeller), campus, day);
}
```

```ts
export async function getVerifiedSellers(): Promise<readonly Seller[]> {
  "use cache";
  tagSellers();
  return liveSellerRows()
    .filter((row) => row.verified)
    .map(toSeller)
    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
}
```

```ts
export async function getListingDetail(id: string): Promise<ListingDetail | null> {
  "use cache";
  tagListings();
  const listing = publicListing(id);
  if (!listing) return null;
  const seller = sellerFor(listing.sellerSlug);
  const category = categories.find((item) => item.slug === listing.categorySlug);
  const campus = campuses.find((item) => item.slug === listing.campusSlug);
  if (!seller || !category || !campus) return null;
  const pickupPoint = pickupPoints.find((point) => point.id === listing.pickupPointId) ?? null;
  return { listing, seller, category, campus, pickupPoint };
}
```

In `getMoreFrom`, replace `const others = available(listings)` with `const others = listedListings()`, and add `  tagListings();` straight after its `"use cache";` line.

```ts
export async function searchListings(query: SearchQuery): Promise<SearchResult> {
  "use cache";
  tagListings();
  return findListings(listedListings(), query, {
    sellers: new Map(liveSellerRows().map((row) => [row.slug, row.name])),
    categories: new Map(categories.map((category) => [category.slug, category.name])),
  });
}

export async function getListingIds(): Promise<readonly string[]> {
  "use cache";
  tagListings();
  return publicListingIds();
}

export async function getSeller(slug: string): Promise<Seller | null> {
  "use cache";
  tagSellers();
  const row = storefrontRow(slug);
  return row ? toSeller(row) : null;
}

export async function getSellerSlugs(): Promise<readonly string[]> {
  "use cache";
  tagSellers();
  return liveSellerRows().map((row) => row.slug);
}
```

```ts
export async function getSellerListings(slug: string): Promise<readonly Listing[]> {
  "use cache";
  tagListings();
  return listedListings()
    .filter((listing) => listing.sellerSlug === slug)
    .sort(newestFirst);
}
```

```ts
export async function getListingsByIds(ids: readonly string[]): Promise<readonly Listing[]> {
  "use cache";
  tagListings();
  return ids.flatMap((id) => publicListing(id) ?? []);
}
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { pngBytes } from "@/tests/helpers/image-bytes";
import {
  deletePhotosOf,
  deletePhotosUsedBy,
  getPhoto,
  photoIdOf,
  photoSrc,
  photoSrcsOf,
  storePhotos,
  usePhotos,
  type Upload,
} from "./photos";
import { resetSampleStore, sampleStore } from "./store";

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-14T08:00:00.000Z");
const upload = (): Upload => ({ type: "image/png", bytes: pngBytes() });

beforeEach(() => {
  resetSampleStore();
});

describe("stored photos (selling spec 2.4, plan decision 11)", () => {
  it("stores photos waiting on a form, gives back their addresses, and hands out their bytes", async () => {
    const [src] = await storePhotos("acc-seller", [upload()], NOW);
    expect(src).toMatch(/^\/photos\/ph-[0-9a-f-]{36}$/);
    expect(await getPhoto(photoIdOf(src)!)).toEqual({ type: "image/png", bytes: pngBytes() });
    expect(await photoSrcsOf("acc-seller", null)).toEqual(new Set([src]));
    expect(await getPhoto("ph-nope")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("reads a photo's id from its address, and none from a placeholder's or a made-up one", () => {
    expect(photoSrc("ph-1")).toBe("/photos/ph-1");
    expect(photoIdOf("/placeholders/listing/tech.svg")).toBeNull();
    expect(photoIdOf("/photos/../secrets")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("gives the owner's photos a use, and never anyone else's", async () => {
    const [mine] = await storePhotos("acc-seller", [upload()], NOW);
    const [theirs] = await storePhotos("acc-student", [upload()], NOW);
    await usePhotos("acc-seller", [mine, theirs, "/placeholders/listing/tech.svg"], "listing:l-001");
    expect(await photoSrcsOf("acc-seller", "listing:l-001")).toEqual(new Set([mine]));
    expect(await photoSrcsOf("acc-student", null)).toEqual(new Set([theirs]));
  });

  it("deletes a use's photos except the ones it keeps, and every photo an account uploaded", async () => {
    const [a, b] = await storePhotos("acc-seller", [upload(), upload()], NOW);
    await usePhotos("acc-seller", [a, b], "shop");
    await deletePhotosUsedBy("acc-seller", "shop", new Set([b]));
    expect(await photoSrcsOf("acc-seller", "shop")).toEqual(new Set([b]));
    await deletePhotosOf("acc-seller");
    expect(sampleStore().photos.size).toBe(0);
  });

  it("tidies away waiting photos after a day, and keeps each account's newest 12", async () => {
    const [old] = await storePhotos("acc-seller", [upload()], new Date("2026-10-13T07:59:00.000Z"));
    await storePhotos("acc-seller", [upload()], NOW);
    expect(await getPhoto(photoIdOf(old)!)).toBeNull();
    const many = await storePhotos("acc-student", Array.from({ length: 14 }, upload), NOW);
    expect(await photoSrcsOf("acc-student", null)).toEqual(new Set(many.slice(2)));
  });
});
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { ListingFields } from "@/lib/selling/rules";
import { pngBytes } from "@/tests/helpers/image-bytes";
import {
  deleteOwnListing,
  getOwnListing,
  listOwnListings,
  saveOwnListing,
  setOwnListingState,
  type ListingSave,
} from "./own-listings";
import { getPhoto, photoIdOf, photoSrcsOf, storePhotos } from "./photos";
import { resetSampleStore, sampleStore } from "./store";

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-14T08:00:00.000Z");
const LATER = new Date("2026-10-15T08:00:00.000Z");

const fields: ListingFields = {
  kind: "item",
  title: "Desk fan",
  categorySlug: "res-living",
  condition: "good",
  priceZar: 150,
  priceIsFrom: false,
  payMode: "cash",
  swapFor: null,
  description: "Quiet and strong.",
  handover: "pickup",
  pickupPointId: "pp-midrand-library",
};

const save = (overrides: Partial<ListingSave> = {}) =>
  saveOwnListing({
    sellerSlug: "tech-corner",
    ownerId: "acc-seller",
    id: null,
    fields,
    photos: [],
    intent: "publish",
    now: NOW,
    ...overrides,
  });

const upload = () => ({ type: "image/png" as const, bytes: pngBytes() });

beforeEach(() => {
  resetSampleStore();
});

describe("a shop's own listings (selling spec 2.9 and 2.10, plan decision 3)", () => {
  it("lists the shop's listings, drafts included, most recently changed first", async () => {
    await save({ intent: "draft" });
    expect((await listOwnListings("tech-corner")).map((row) => [row.title, row.state])).toEqual([
      ["Desk fan", "draft"],
      ["Casio fx-991ZA Plus calculator", "available"],
      ["Gaming headset, barely used", "available"],
    ]);
    expect(await listOwnListings("crochet-corner")).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("publishes a new listing on the shop's campus, with a new id and the time it was first published", async () => {
    const row = (await save())!;
    expect(row).toMatchObject({
      id: expect.stringMatching(/^l-[0-9a-f-]{36}$/),
      state: "available",
      campusSlug: "midrand",
      title: "Desk fan",
      createdAt: NOW.toISOString(),
      updatedAt: NOW.toISOString(),
    });
    expect(await getOwnListing("tech-corner", row.id)).toEqual(row);
  });

  it("dates a draft from when it's first published, then keeps that date", async () => {
    const draft = (await save({ intent: "draft" }))!;
    expect((await save({ id: draft.id, now: LATER }))).toMatchObject({ state: "available", createdAt: LATER.toISOString() });
    const edited = await save({
      id: draft.id,
      now: new Date("2026-10-16T08:00:00.000Z"),
      fields: { ...fields, title: "Desk fan, white" },
    });
    expect(edited).toMatchObject({ title: "Desk fan, white", createdAt: LATER.toISOString() });
  });

  it("keeps a sold listing sold on Save changes", async () => {
    await setOwnListingState("tech-corner", "l-001", "sold", NOW);
    expect((await save({ id: "l-001" }))?.state).toBe("sold");
  });

  it("never finds or changes another shop's listing", async () => {
    expect(await getOwnListing("tech-corner", "l-004")).toBeNull();
    expect(await save({ id: "l-004" })).toBeNull();
    expect(await setOwnListingState("tech-corner", "l-004", "paused", NOW)).toBeNull();
    expect(await deleteOwnListing("tech-corner", "acc-seller", "l-004")).toBe(false);
    expect(sampleStore().listings.get("l-004")).toMatchObject({ title: "Box braids, shoulder length", state: "available" });
  });

  it("pauses, resumes, marks sold and relists, and makes no other move", async () => {
    expect((await setOwnListingState("tech-corner", "l-001", "paused", NOW))?.state).toBe("paused");
    expect(await setOwnListingState("tech-corner", "l-001", "paused", NOW)).toBeNull();
    expect((await setOwnListingState("tech-corner", "l-001", "available", NOW))?.state).toBe("available");
    expect((await setOwnListingState("tech-corner", "l-001", "sold", NOW))?.state).toBe("sold");
    expect((await setOwnListingState("tech-corner", "l-001", "available", LATER))?.updatedAt).toBe(LATER.toISOString());
    const draft = (await save({ intent: "draft" }))!;
    expect(await setOwnListingState("tech-corner", draft.id, "available", NOW)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("makes its photos its own, and deletes the ones it stops using, and the rest with it", async () => {
    const [a, b] = await storePhotos("acc-seller", [upload(), upload()], NOW);
    const row = (await save({ photos: [{ src: a, alt: "" }, { src: b, alt: "The plug" }] }))!;
    expect(await photoSrcsOf("acc-seller", `listing:${row.id}`)).toEqual(new Set([a, b]));
    await save({ id: row.id, photos: [{ src: b, alt: "" }] });
    expect(await getPhoto(photoIdOf(a)!)).toBeNull();
    expect(await deleteOwnListing("tech-corner", "acc-seller", row.id)).toBe(true);
    expect(await getPhoto(photoIdOf(b)!)).toBeNull();
    expect(await getOwnListing("tech-corner", row.id)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { pngBytes } from "@/tests/helpers/image-bytes";
import { getPhoto, photoIdOf, photoSrcsOf, storePhotos } from "./photos";
import {
  changeShopSlug,
  createShop,
  emptyDraft,
  exportShop,
  forgetShopOf,
  getOnboardingDraft,
  getShopOf,
  isShopNameTaken,
  restoreSampleShop,
  saveOnboardingDraft,
  setShopPaused,
  updateShop,
  type ShopDetails,
} from "./shops";
import { resetSampleStore, sampleStore } from "./store";

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-14T08:00:00.000Z");

const details: ShopDetails = {
  name: "Zola's Bakes",
  tagline: "Muffins on Mondays.",
  categorySlug: "food",
  about: "I bake between lectures.",
  image: null,
  campusSlug: "midrand",
  pickupPointIds: ["pp-midrand-library"],
};

const upload = () => ({ type: "image/png" as const, bytes: pngBytes() });
const applyAs = (accountId: string, overrides: Partial<ShopDetails> = {}) =>
  createShop(accountId, { ...details, ...overrides }, NOW);

beforeEach(() => {
  resetSampleStore();
});

describe("applying to sell (selling spec 2.6 and 2.7, plan decisions 1 and 16)", () => {
  it("makes a shop with a link from its name, and a seller profile waiting for the Hub, with no storefront yet", async () => {
    const shop = (await applyAs("acc-student"))!;
    expect(shop).toMatchObject({
      slug: "zolas-bakes",
      ownerId: "acc-student",
      verified: false,
      badge: "new-seller",
      since: "2026-10",
      slugChanged: false,
    });
    expect(sampleStore().sellerProfiles.get("acc-student")).toEqual({
      accountId: "acc-student",
      status: "pending",
      sellerSlug: null,
      appliedAt: NOW.toISOString(),
    });
    expect(await getShopOf("acc-student")).toEqual({ shop, status: "pending" });
  });

  it("makes every link unique, and never a second shop for one account", async () => {
    await applyAs("acc-student");
    expect((await applyAs("acc-mentor", { campusSlug: "pretoria" }))?.slug).toBe("zolas-bakes-2");
    expect(await applyAs("acc-student")).toBeNull();
    expect(await applyAs("acc-seller")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("knows a name taken on the same campus, however it's written", async () => {
    expect(await isShopNameTaken("TECH corner!", "midrand", null)).toBe(true);
    expect(await isShopNameTaken("Tech corner", "pretoria", null)).toBe(false);
    expect(await isShopNameTaken("Tech corner", "midrand", "tech-corner")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("keeps the wizard's draft and its photo, which become the shop's", async () => {
    const [photo] = await storePhotos("acc-student", [upload()], NOW);
    await saveOnboardingDraft({ ...emptyDraft("acc-student", "midrand", NOW), name: "Zola's Bakes", photoSrc: photo });
    expect((await getOnboardingDraft("acc-student"))?.photoSrc).toBe(photo);
    expect(await photoSrcsOf("acc-student", "draft")).toEqual(new Set([photo]));
    await applyAs("acc-student", { image: { src: photo, alt: "" } });
    expect(await getOnboardingDraft("acc-student")).toBeNull();
    expect(await photoSrcsOf("acc-student", "shop")).toEqual(new Set([photo]));
  });

  it("deletes a draft's photo once another replaces it", async () => {
    const [first, second] = await storePhotos("acc-student", [upload(), upload()], NOW);
    await saveOnboardingDraft({ ...emptyDraft("acc-student", "midrand", NOW), photoSrc: first });
    await saveOnboardingDraft({ ...emptyDraft("acc-student", "midrand", NOW), photoSrc: second });
    expect(await getPhoto(photoIdOf(first)!)).toBeNull();
    expect(await photoSrcsOf("acc-student", "draft")).toEqual(new Set([second]));
  });
});

describe("editing the shop (selling spec 2.11, plan decisions 16 and 17)", () => {
  it("changes the details, and a waiting shop's link follows its name", async () => {
    const shop = await updateShop(
      "acc-pending",
      { ...details, name: "Hook club", campusSlug: "pretoria", pickupPointIds: ["pp-pretoria-library"] },
      NOW,
    );
    expect(shop).toMatchObject({ slug: "hook-club", name: "Hook club", categorySlug: "food" });

  });

  it("keeps an approved shop's link whatever its name", async () => {
    expect((await updateShop("acc-seller", { ...details, name: "Gadget corner" }, NOW))?.slug).toBe("tech-corner");
  });

  it("moves its listings to a new campus, and a pickup to one of its spots, or online when it has none", async () => {
    const pretoria = { ...details, name: "Tech corner", campusSlug: "pretoria", pickupPointIds: ["pp-pretoria-reception"] };
    await updateShop("acc-seller", pretoria, NOW);
    expect(sampleStore().listings.get("l-001")).toMatchObject({
      campusSlug: "pretoria",
      handover: "pickup",
      pickupPointId: "pp-pretoria-reception",

    expect(sampleStore().listings.get("l-001")).toMatchObject({
      campusSlug: "online",
      handover: "online",
      pickupPointId: null,
    });
  });

  it("swaps the shop's photo, deleting the old one", async () => {
    const [first, second] = await storePhotos("acc-seller", [upload(), upload()], NOW);
    await updateShop("acc-seller", { ...details, image: { src: first, alt: "" } }, NOW);
    await updateShop("acc-seller", { ...details, image: { src: second, alt: "" } }, NOW);
    expect(await getPhoto(photoIdOf(first)!)).toBeNull();
    expect(await photoSrcsOf("acc-seller", "shop")).toEqual(new Set([second]));
  });

  it("changes an approved shop's link once, and its listings, profile, conversations and reports go with it", async () => {
    sampleStore().reports.set("rep-t1", {
      id: "rep-t1",
      reporterId: "acc-student",
      targetKind: "seller",
      targetId: "tech-corner",
      targetTitle: "Tech corner",
      reason: "spam",
      detail: "",
      status: "open",
      createdAt: NOW.toISOString(),
    });
    expect(await changeShopSlug("acc-seller", "gadgets", NOW)).toBe("changed");
    const store = sampleStore();
    expect(store.sellers.has("tech-corner")).toBe(false);
    expect(store.sellers.get("gadgets")).toMatchObject({ name: "Tech corner", slugChanged: true });
    expect(store.listings.get("l-001")?.sellerSlug).toBe("gadgets");
    expect(store.sellerProfiles.get("acc-seller")?.sellerSlug).toBe("gadgets");
    expect([...store.conversations.values()].map((row) => row.sellerSlug)).toEqual(["gadgets", "gadgets"]);
    expect(store.reports.get("rep-t1")?.targetId).toBe("gadgets");
    expect(await changeShopSlug("acc-seller", "gadgets-two", NOW)).toBe("used");
  });

  it("won't take another shop's link, or change a waiting shop's", async () => {
    expect(await changeShopSlug("acc-seller", "hair-studio", NOW)).toBe("taken");
    expect(await changeShopSlug("acc-seller", "tech-corner", NOW)).toBe("same");
    expect(await changeShopSlug("acc-pending", "lerato", NOW)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("pausing the shop (selling spec 2.8)", () => {
  it("pauses an approved shop and resumes a paused one, and nothing else", async () => {
    expect(await setShopPaused("acc-seller", true)).toBe("paused");
    expect(await setShopPaused("acc-seller", true)).toBeNull();
    expect(await setShopPaused("acc-seller", false)).toBe("approved");
    expect(await setShopPaused("acc-pending", true)).toBeNull();
    expect(await setShopPaused("acc-student", true)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("deleting and bringing back (plan decision 22, D's plan decision 15)", () => {
  it("removes an account's shop, its listings, its photos and its draft, and nothing of anyone else's", async () => {
    await storePhotos("acc-seller", [upload()], NOW);
    await storePhotos("acc-student", [upload()], NOW);
    await saveOnboardingDraft(emptyDraft("acc-seller", "midrand", NOW));
    expect(await forgetShopOf("acc-seller")).toBe(true);
    const store = sampleStore();
    expect(store.sellers.has("tech-corner")).toBe(false);
    expect(store.listings.has("l-001") || store.listings.has("l-007")).toBe(false);
    expect(store.listings.size).toBe(36);
    expect([...store.photos.values()].map((row) => row.ownerId)).toEqual(["acc-student"]);
    expect(store.onboardingDrafts.size).toBe(0);
    expect(await forgetShopOf("acc-student")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("puts a sample shop and its sample listings back, once", async () => {
    await forgetShopOf("acc-seller");
    expect(await restoreSampleShop("acc-seller")).toBe(true);
    expect(sampleStore().sellers.get("tech-corner")?.ownerId).toBe("acc-seller");
    expect(sampleStore().listings.has("l-001")).toBe(true);
    expect(await restoreSampleShop("acc-seller")).toBe(false);
    expect(await restoreSampleShop("acc-student")).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("the data export (plan decision 22)", () => {
  it("gives the shop and its listings, with pickup points by name and photos by address, and no ids", async () => {
    const data = await exportShop("acc-seller");
    expect(data.shop).toMatchObject({
      name: "Tech corner",
      link: "tech-corner",
      status: "approved",
      category: "Tech",
      campus: "midrand",
      photo: "/placeholders/seller/tech-corner.svg",
      pickupPoints: ["Library entrance", "Main reception"],
      since: "2026-03",
    });
    expect(data.listings.map((row) => row.title)).toEqual(["Casio fx-991ZA Plus calculator", "Gaming headset, barely used"]);
    expect(data.listings[0]).toMatchObject({
      state: "available",
      category: "Tech",
      pickupPoint: "Library entrance",
      photos: [
        { address: "/placeholders/listing/tech.svg", description: "" },
        { address: "/placeholders/photo/2.svg", description: "The back of the calculator, with the battery cover" },
        { address: "/placeholders/photo/3.svg", description: "The slide-on cover" },
      ],
    });
    expect(JSON.stringify(data)).not.toMatch(/acc-seller|l-001|pp-midrand/);
    expect(await exportShop("acc-student")).toEqual({ shop: null, listings: [] });
  });
});
```
