- [ ] **Step 4: Write the dashboard numbers' test**

Create `lib/data/shop-stats.test.ts`:

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { ConversationRow } from "./rows";
import { shopStats } from "./shop-stats";
import { resetSampleStore, sampleStore } from "./store";

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-14T10:00:00.000Z");
const HOUR_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;
const ago = (hours: number) => new Date(NOW.getTime() - hours * HOUR_MS).toISOString();

let made = 0;
/** A conversation with Tech corner about a listing, asked an hour ago. */
function add(overrides: Partial<ConversationRow>): void {
  made += 1;
  const row: ConversationRow = {
    id: `conv-t${made}`,
    listingId: "l-001",
    listingTitle: "Casio fx-991ZA Plus calculator",
    sellerSlug: "tech-corner",
    buyerId: "acc-student",
    sellerGone: false,
    status: "asked",
    createdAt: ago(1),
    lastMessageAt: ago(1),
    firstReplyAt: null,
    completedAt: null,
    confirmedAt: null,
    confirmedBy: null,
    ...overrides,
  };
  sampleStore().conversations.set(row.id, row);
}

beforeEach(() => {
  resetSampleStore();
});

describe("the dashboard's numbers (selling spec 2.8, plan decision 18)", () => {
  it("has the sample seller's two enquiries this week, both answered in time, and no sale yet", async () => {
    const counts = { enquiries: 2, due: 2, repliedInTime: 2, salesConfirmed: 0 };
    expect(await shopStats("tech-corner", NOW)).toEqual({ week: counts, record: counts });
  });

  it("counts an enquiry as due once it's answered or 48 hours old, and answered in time within 48 hours", async () => {
    sampleStore().conversations.clear();
    add({ createdAt: ago(10), firstReplyAt: ago(9) });
    add({ createdAt: ago(60), firstReplyAt: ago(5) });
    add({ createdAt: ago(50) });
    add({ createdAt: ago(5) });
    expect((await shopStats("tech-corner", NOW)).week).toEqual({
      enquiries: 4,
      due: 3,
      repliedInTime: 1,
      salesConfirmed: 0,
    });
  });

  it("leaves out questions to the shop, other shops, and enquiries older than each window", async () => {
    sampleStore().conversations.clear();
    add({ listingId: null, listingTitle: null });
    add({ sellerSlug: "hair-studio" });
    add({ createdAt: ago(24 * 8) });
    add({ createdAt: ago(24 * 91) });
    const stats = await shopStats("tech-corner", NOW);
    expect(stats.week.enquiries).toBe(0);
    expect(stats.record.enquiries).toBe(1);
  });

  it("counts sales the buyer confirmed, and ones marked done 7 days ago, writing nothing", async () => {
    sampleStore().conversations.clear();
    add({ status: "confirmed", confirmedAt: ago(24), confirmedBy: "buyer" });
    add({ status: "done", completedAt: ago(24 * 8) });
    add({ status: "done", completedAt: ago(24 * 2) });
    add({ status: "confirmed", confirmedAt: ago(24 * 30), confirmedBy: "auto" });
    const stats = await shopStats("tech-corner", NOW);
    expect(stats.week.salesConfirmed).toBe(2);
    expect(stats.record.salesConfirmed).toBe(3);
    expect([...sampleStore().conversations.values()].filter((row) => row.status === "done")).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the photos**

Create `lib/data/photos.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { DAY_MS } from "@/lib/messaging/rules";
import type { PhotoType } from "@/lib/types";
import type { PhotoRow } from "./rows";
import { sampleStore } from "./store";

// Uploaded photos (selling spec 2.4, plan decision 11), standing in for Supabase Storage. Server Actions store, use
// and delete them; /photos/[id] hands them out. The bytes are shared, never copied: nothing changes them once stored.

/** A photo uploaded on a form that hasn't saved yet is kept for a day, and each account keeps its newest 12. */
export const LOOSE_PHOTO_TTL_MS = DAY_MS;
export const LOOSE_PHOTOS_PER_ACCOUNT = 12;

const PREFIX = "/photos/";
const PHOTO_ID = /^ph-[0-9a-f-]{36}$/;

/** A photo checked and stripped (lib/selling/uploads.ts), ready to store. */
export type Upload = { type: PhotoType; bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> };

export function isPhotoId(id: string): boolean {
  return PHOTO_ID.test(id);
}

export function photoSrc(id: string): string {
  return `${PREFIX}${id}`;
}

/** The photo's id from its address, or null when the address isn't a stored photo's (a placeholder, say). */
export function photoIdOf(src: string): string | null {
  const id = src.startsWith(PREFIX) ? src.slice(PREFIX.length) : "";
  return isPhotoId(id) ? id : null;
}

/** What a listing's photos are used for. */
export function listingPhotoUse(listingId: string): string {
  return `listing:${listingId}`;
}

/**
 * Stores photos for `ownerId`, waiting on a form (no use yet), and gives back their addresses in order. Tidies away
 * waiting photos more than a day old, and keeps each account's newest 12.
 */
export async function storePhotos(ownerId: string, uploads: readonly Upload[], now: Date): Promise<string[]> {
  const { photos } = sampleStore();
  const srcs = uploads.map((upload) => {
    const id = `ph-${randomUUID()}`;
    photos.set(id, { id, ownerId, use: null, type: upload.type, bytes: upload.bytes, createdAt: now.toISOString() });
    return photoSrc(id);
  });
  const cutoff = now.getTime() - LOOSE_PHOTO_TTL_MS;
  for (const [id, row] of photos) {
    if (row.use === null && Date.parse(row.createdAt) <= cutoff) photos.delete(id);
  }
  // The store keeps insertion order, so the oldest waiting photos come first.
  const waiting = [...photos.values()].filter((row) => row.use === null && row.ownerId === ownerId);
  for (const row of waiting.slice(0, Math.max(0, waiting.length - LOOSE_PHOTOS_PER_ACCOUNT))) photos.delete(row.id);
  return srcs;
}

/** A photo's type and bytes, for /photos/[id]. */
export async function getPhoto(id: string): Promise<Pick<PhotoRow, "type" | "bytes"> | null> {
  const row = sampleStore().photos.get(id);
  return row ? { type: row.type, bytes: row.bytes } : null;
}

/** The addresses of `ownerId`'s photos with this use; null means waiting on a form. */
export async function photoSrcsOf(ownerId: string, use: string | null): Promise<Set<string>> {
  const rows = [...sampleStore().photos.values()].filter((row) => row.ownerId === ownerId && row.use === use);
  return new Set(rows.map((row) => photoSrc(row.id)));
}

/** Gives `ownerId`'s photos at these addresses a use. Anyone else's photos, and placeholders, are left alone. */
export async function usePhotos(ownerId: string, srcs: Iterable<string>, use: string): Promise<void> {
  const { photos } = sampleStore();
  for (const src of srcs) {
    const row = photos.get(photoIdOf(src) ?? "");
    if (row?.ownerId === ownerId) row.use = use;
  }
}

/** Deletes `ownerId`'s photos with this use, except those at the addresses in `keep`. */
export async function deletePhotosUsedBy(
  ownerId: string,
  use: string,
  keep: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(),
): Promise<void> {
  const { photos } = sampleStore();
  for (const [id, row] of photos) {
    if (row.ownerId === ownerId && row.use === use && !keep.has(photoSrc(id))) photos.delete(id);
  }
}

/** Deletes every photo `ownerId` uploaded (deleting an account, plan decision 22). */
export async function deletePhotosOf(ownerId: string): Promise<void> {
  const { photos } = sampleStore();
  for (const [id, row] of photos) {
    if (row.ownerId === ownerId) photos.delete(id);
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the own listings**

Create `lib/data/own-listings.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { nextState, type ListingFields } from "@/lib/selling/rules";
import type { ImageRef, ListingState } from "@/lib/types";
import { deletePhotosUsedBy, listingPhotoUse, usePhotos } from "./photos";
import type { ListingRow } from "./rows";
import { sampleStore } from "./store";

// A shop's own listings (selling spec 2.9 and 2.10, plan decision 3), drafts included. Every function takes the
// shop's slug and only ever finds or changes that shop's listings, so a listing id from a form can't reach anyone
// else's. Times come from the caller.

const copy = (row: ListingRow): ListingRow => structuredClone(row);

/** The shop's listings, most recently changed first. */
export async function listOwnListings(sellerSlug: string): Promise<ListingRow[]> {
  return [...sampleStore().listings.values()]
    .filter((row) => row.sellerSlug === sellerSlug)
    .sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt.localeCompare(a.updatedAt))
    .map(copy);
}

export async function getOwnListing(sellerSlug: string, id: string): Promise<ListingRow | null> {
  const row = sampleStore().listings.get(id);
  return row && row.sellerSlug === sellerSlug ? copy(row) : null;
}

export type ListingSave = {
  sellerSlug: string;
  /** The shop's owner, whose photos these are. */
  ownerId: string;
  /** Null for a new listing. */
  id: string | null;
  fields: ListingFields;
  /** In order, the cover first. An empty alt means the title. */
  photos: readonly ImageRef[];
  intent: "publish" | "draft";
  now: Date;
};

/**
 * Saves a new or changed listing (plan decision 3) on its shop's campus. The photos it uses become its own, and the
 * ones it no longer uses are deleted. A draft's `createdAt` moves to when it's first published. Null when `id` isn't
 * one of the shop's listings, or the shop has gone.
 */
export async function saveOwnListing(save: ListingSave): Promise<ListingRow | null> {
  const { listings, sellers } = sampleStore();
  const shop = sellers.get(save.sellerSlug);
  const existing = save.id ? listings.get(save.id) : undefined;
  if (!shop || (save.id && existing?.sellerSlug !== save.sellerSlug)) return null;
  const at = save.now.toISOString();
  const state = nextState(existing?.state ?? null, save.intent);
  const firstPublish = state !== "draft" && (!existing || existing.state === "draft");
  const row: ListingRow = {
    id: existing?.id ?? `l-${randomUUID()}`,
    sellerSlug: save.sellerSlug,
    state,
    ...save.fields,
    campusSlug: shop.campusSlug,
    photos: save.photos.map((photo) => ({ src: photo.src, alt: photo.alt })),
    createdAt: !existing || firstPublish ? at : existing.createdAt,
    updatedAt: at,
  };
  listings.set(row.id, row);
  const srcs = new Set(row.photos.map((photo) => photo.src));
  await usePhotos(save.ownerId, srcs, listingPhotoUse(row.id));
  await deletePhotosUsedBy(save.ownerId, listingPhotoUse(row.id), srcs);
  return copy(row);
}

/** The row buttons' moves (plan decision 3): Pause, Resume, Mark sold and Relist. A draft publishes from its form. */
const MOVES_TO: Readonly<Record<"available" | "paused" | "sold", readonly ListingState[]>> = {
  paused: ["available"],
  available: ["paused", "sold"],
  sold: ["available", "paused"],
};

/** Moves one of the shop's listings to `to`. Null when it isn't the shop's, or can't move there. */
export async function setOwnListingState(
  sellerSlug: string,
  id: string,
  to: "available" | "paused" | "sold",
  now: Date,
): Promise<ListingRow | null> {
  const row = sampleStore().listings.get(id);
  if (!row || row.sellerSlug !== sellerSlug || !MOVES_TO[to].includes(row.state)) return null;
  row.state = to;
  row.updatedAt = now.toISOString();
  return copy(row);
}

/** Deletes one of the shop's listings and its photos (selling spec 2.9). Conversations keep its title (messaging). */
export async function deleteOwnListing(sellerSlug: string, ownerId: string, id: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const { listings } = sampleStore();
  const row = listings.get(id);
  if (!row || row.sellerSlug !== sellerSlug) return false;
  listings.delete(id);
  await deletePhotosUsedBy(ownerId, listingPhotoUse(id));
  return true;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write the shops**

Create `lib/data/shops.ts`:

```ts
import { categories } from "@/data/fixtures/categories";
import { pickupPoints } from "@/data/fixtures/pickup-points";
import { sampleListingRows, sampleSellerRows, sampleShopOwners } from "@/data/fixtures/shops";
import { nameKey, SLUG_MAX, slugify } from "@/lib/selling/rules";
import { sastDate } from "@/lib/time";
import type { Condition, Handover, ImageRef, ListingKind, ListingState, PayMode, SellerStatus } from "@/lib/types";
import { deletePhotosOf, deletePhotosUsedBy, usePhotos } from "./photos";
import type { ListingRow, OnboardingDraftRow, SellerRow } from "./rows";
import { sampleStore } from "./store";

// A seller's own shop (selling spec 2.6 to 2.8 and 2.11): applying, editing, pausing, the one change of link, the
// wizard's draft, and what deleting an account removes. Status lives on the owner's seller profile (plan decision 1).
// Only Server Actions change anything; pages only read. Times come from the caller.

export type OwnShop = { shop: SellerRow; status: SellerStatus };

/** The account's shop and its status, or null when it has none. */
export async function getShopOf(accountId: string): Promise<OwnShop | null> {
  const store = sampleStore();
  const profile = store.sellerProfiles.get(accountId);
  if (!profile) return null;
  for (const row of store.sellers.values()) {
    if (row.ownerId === accountId) return { shop: structuredClone(row), status: profile.status };
  }
  return null;
}

/** Whether another shop on `campusSlug` has this name (plan decision 16). `exceptSlug` is the shop being edited. */
export async function isShopNameTaken(name: string, campusSlug: string, exceptSlug: string | null): Promise<boolean> {
  const key = nameKey(name);
  for (const row of sampleStore().sellers.values()) {
    if (row.slug !== exceptSlug && row.campusSlug === campusSlug && nameKey(row.name) === key) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

export async function isSlugTaken(slug: string): Promise<boolean> {
  return sampleStore().sellers.has(slug);
}

/** A link no other shop has, from `base`: "crochet-corner", then "crochet-corner-2" and so on. `own` keeps its own. */
function freeSlug(base: string, own: string | null): string {
  const { sellers } = sampleStore();
  for (let n = 1; ; n += 1) {
    const suffix = n === 1 ? "" : `-${n}`;
    const slug = `${base.slice(0, SLUG_MAX - suffix.length).replace(/-+$/, "")}${suffix}`;
    if (slug === own || !sellers.has(slug)) return slug;
  }
}

/** Moves a shop to a new link, and everything that names it by its link with it (plan decision 16). */
function moveShop(from: string, to: string): void {
  const store = sampleStore();
  const row = store.sellers.get(from);
  if (!row || from === to) return;
  store.sellers.delete(from);
  row.slug = to;
  store.sellers.set(to, row);
  for (const listing of store.listings.values()) {
    if (listing.sellerSlug === from) listing.sellerSlug = to;
  }
  for (const profile of store.sellerProfiles.values()) {
    if (profile.sellerSlug === from) profile.sellerSlug = to;
  }
  // Messaging names the shop by its link (messaging plan decision 1), so its conversations and reports move too.
  for (const conversation of store.conversations.values()) {
    if (conversation.sellerSlug === from) conversation.sellerSlug = to;
  }
  for (const report of store.reports.values()) {
    if (report.targetKind === "seller" && report.targetId === from) report.targetId = to;
  }
}

/** What the wizard and Edit shop set (selling spec 2.6 and 2.11). */
export type ShopDetails = {
  name: string;
  tagline: string;
  categorySlug: string;
  about: string;
  /** A stored photo, or null for the category's picture. */
  image: ImageRef | null;
  campusSlug: string;
  pickupPointIds: string[];
};

/**
 * Sends an application (selling spec 2.6 and 2.7): a new shop with a link made from its name, and a seller profile
 * waiting for the Hub, with no storefront yet (plan decision 1). The draft goes, and its photo becomes the shop's.
 * Null when the account already has a shop.
 */
export async function createShop(accountId: string, details: ShopDetails, now: Date): Promise<SellerRow | null> {
  const store = sampleStore();
  if (store.sellerProfiles.has(accountId)) return null;
  const at = now.toISOString();
  const row: SellerRow = {
    slug: freeSlug(slugify(details.name), null),
    ownerId: accountId,
    name: details.name,
    tagline: details.tagline,
    categorySlug: details.categorySlug,
    campusSlug: details.campusSlug,
    verified: false,
    badge: "new-seller",
    about: details.about,
    since: sastDate(now).slice(0, 7),
    image: details.image ? { ...details.image } : null,
    pickupPointIds: [...details.pickupPointIds],
    slugChanged: false,
    createdAt: at,
    updatedAt: at,
  };
  store.sellers.set(row.slug, row);
  store.sellerProfiles.set(accountId, { accountId, status: "pending", sellerSlug: null, appliedAt: at });
  store.onboardingDrafts.delete(accountId);
  if (row.image) await usePhotos(accountId, [row.image.src], "shop");
  await deletePhotosUsedBy(accountId, "draft");
  return structuredClone(row);
}

/**
 * Edit shop (selling spec 2.11). While the shop waits for approval its link follows its name (plan decision 16). Its
 * listings follow its campus, and a pickup whose point it no longer uses moves to its first point, or online when it
 * has none (plan decision 17). A replaced or removed photo is deleted. Null when the account has no shop.
 */
export async function updateShop(accountId: string, details: ShopDetails, now: Date): Promise<SellerRow | null> {
  const store = sampleStore();
  const own = await getShopOf(accountId);
  if (!own) return null;
  const row = store.sellers.get(own.shop.slug)!;
  const oldPhoto = row.image?.src ?? null;
  row.name = details.name;
  row.tagline = details.tagline;
  row.categorySlug = details.categorySlug;
  row.about = details.about;
  row.image = details.image ? { ...details.image } : null;
  row.campusSlug = details.campusSlug;
  row.pickupPointIds = [...details.pickupPointIds];
  row.updatedAt = now.toISOString();
  if (own.status === "pending") moveShop(row.slug, freeSlug(slugify(row.name), row.slug));
  for (const listing of store.listings.values()) {
    if (listing.sellerSlug !== row.slug) continue;
    listing.campusSlug = row.campusSlug;
    if (listing.handover === "pickup" && !row.pickupPointIds.includes(listing.pickupPointId ?? "")) {
      listing.pickupPointId = row.pickupPointIds[0] ?? null;
      if (listing.pickupPointId === null) listing.handover = "online";
    }
  }
  const newPhoto = row.image?.src ?? null;
  if (newPhoto !== oldPhoto) {
    await deletePhotosUsedBy(accountId, "shop", new Set(newPhoto ? [newPhoto] : []));
    if (newPhoto) await usePhotos(accountId, [newPhoto], "shop");
  }
  return structuredClone(row);
}

export type SlugChange = "changed" | "same" | "used" | "taken";

/**
 * The seller's one change of link (selling spec 2.11), for an approved or paused shop. Old links then 404. Null when
 * the account has no shop, or its shop is still waiting (its link follows its name).
 */
export async function changeShopSlug(accountId: string, slug: string, now: Date): Promise<SlugChange | null> {
  const own = await getShopOf(accountId);
  if (!own || own.status === "pending") return null;
  if (own.shop.slug === slug) return "same";
  if (own.shop.slugChanged) return "used";
  if (sampleStore().sellers.has(slug)) return "taken";
  moveShop(own.shop.slug, slug);
  const row = sampleStore().sellers.get(slug)!;
  row.slugChanged = true;
  row.updatedAt = now.toISOString();
  return "changed";
}

/** Pause my shop and Resume (selling spec 2.8): approved to paused and back. The new status, or null when it can't. */
export async function setShopPaused(accountId: string, paused: boolean): Promise<SellerStatus | null> {
  const profile = sampleStore().sellerProfiles.get(accountId);
  if (!profile || profile.status !== (paused ? "approved" : "paused")) return null;
  profile.status = paused ? "paused" : "approved";
  return profile.status;
}

/** A wizard with nothing answered yet, on the account's campus. */
export function emptyDraft(accountId: string, campusSlug: string, now: Date): OnboardingDraftRow {
  return {
    accountId,
    name: "",
    tagline: "",
    categorySlug: "",
    about: "",
    photoSrc: null,
    campusSlug,
    pickupPointIds: [],
    updatedAt: now.toISOString(),
  };
}

export async function getOnboardingDraft(accountId: string): Promise<OnboardingDraftRow | null> {
  const row = sampleStore().onboardingDrafts.get(accountId);
  return row ? structuredClone(row) : null;
}

/** Saves the wizard's answers (selling spec 2.2). Its photo becomes the draft's, and one it no longer uses goes. */
export async function saveOnboardingDraft(draft: OnboardingDraftRow): Promise<void> {
  sampleStore().onboardingDrafts.set(draft.accountId, structuredClone(draft));
  await deletePhotosUsedBy(draft.accountId, "draft", new Set(draft.photoSrc ? [draft.photoSrc] : []));
  if (draft.photoSrc) await usePhotos(draft.accountId, [draft.photoSrc], "draft");
}

/**
 * Deleting an account (plan decision 22): its shop, the shop's listings, every photo it uploaded and its wizard
 * draft. True when it had a shop. Its seller profile goes with the account (D); its conversations stay for the
 * buyers, with each listing's title (messaging plan decision 14).
 */
export async function forgetShopOf(accountId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const store = sampleStore();
  let hadShop = false;
  for (const [slug, row] of store.sellers) {
    if (row.ownerId !== accountId) continue;
    hadShop = true;
    store.sellers.delete(slug);
    for (const [id, listing] of store.listings) {
      if (listing.sellerSlug === slug) store.listings.delete(id);
    }
  }
  store.onboardingDrafts.delete(accountId);
  await deletePhotosOf(accountId);
  return hadShop;
}

/**
 * Brings a sample account's shop and its sample listings back from the fixtures, once deleted (D's plan decision 15:
 * a sample account's button puts it back). True when it did.
 */
export async function restoreSampleShop(accountId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const store = sampleStore();
  if ([...store.sellers.values()].some((row) => row.ownerId === accountId)) return false;
  let restored = false;
  for (const [slug, owner] of sampleShopOwners) {
    const seed = sampleSellerRows.find((row) => row.slug === slug);
    if (owner !== accountId || !seed || store.sellers.has(slug)) continue;
    store.sellers.set(slug, structuredClone(seed));
    for (const listing of sampleListingRows) {
      if (listing.sellerSlug === slug && !store.listings.has(listing.id)) {
        store.listings.set(listing.id, structuredClone(listing));
      }
    }
    restored = true;
  }
  return restored;
}

/** The shop in the account's data export (plan decision 22): pickup points by name, the photo by its address, no ids. */
export type ShopExport = {
  name: string;
  link: string;
  status: SellerStatus;
  tagline: string;
  category: string;
  campus: string;
  about: string;
  photo: string | null;
  pickupPoints: string[];
  since: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type ListingExport = {
  title: string;
  state: ListingState;
  kind: ListingKind;
  category: string;
  condition: Condition | null;
  priceZar: number | null;
  priceIsFrom: boolean;
  payMode: PayMode;
  swapFor: string | null;
  description: string;
  handover: Handover;
  pickupPoint: string | null;
  campus: string;
  /** Each photo's address (a placeholder's, or /photos/… to download it) and description ("" means the title). */
  photos: { address: string; description: string }[];
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
};

const spotName = (id: string | null) => pickupPoints.find((point) => point.id === id)?.name ?? null;
const categoryName = (slug: string) => categories.find((category) => category.slug === slug)?.name ?? slug;

function listingExport(row: ListingRow): ListingExport {
  return {
    title: row.title,
    state: row.state,
    kind: row.kind,
    category: categoryName(row.categorySlug),
    condition: row.condition,
    priceZar: row.priceZar,
    priceIsFrom: row.priceIsFrom,
    payMode: row.payMode,
    swapFor: row.swapFor,
    description: row.description,
    handover: row.handover,
    pickupPoint: spotName(row.pickupPointId),
    campus: row.campusSlug,
    photos: row.photos.map((photo) => ({ address: photo.src, description: photo.alt })),
    createdAt: row.createdAt,
    updatedAt: row.updatedAt,
  };
}

/** The account's shop and listings, most recently changed first, for the data export. */
export async function exportShop(accountId: string): Promise<{ shop: ShopExport | null; listings: ListingExport[] }> {
  const own = await getShopOf(accountId);
  if (!own) return { shop: null, listings: [] };
  const { shop, status } = own;
  const listings = [...sampleStore().listings.values()]
    .filter((row) => row.sellerSlug === shop.slug)
    .sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt.localeCompare(a.updatedAt));
  return {
    shop: {
      name: shop.name,
      link: shop.slug,
      status,
      tagline: shop.tagline,
      category: categoryName(shop.categorySlug),
      campus: shop.campusSlug,
      about: shop.about,
      photo: shop.image?.src ?? null,
      pickupPoints: shop.pickupPointIds.flatMap((id) => spotName(id) ?? []),
      since: shop.since,
      createdAt: shop.createdAt,
    },
    listings: listings.map(listingExport),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write the dashboard's numbers**

Create `lib/data/shop-stats.ts`:

```ts
import { autoConfirmAt, DAY_MS } from "@/lib/messaging/rules";
import type { EnquiryCounts, ShopStats } from "@/lib/types";
import type { ConversationRow } from "./rows";
import { sampleStore } from "./store";

// The dashboard's numbers (selling spec 2.8, plan decision 18), from messaging's conversations. The trust page's
// rules: an enquiry is a buyer's first message about a listing; one still inside its 48 hours doesn't count yet; a
// sale counts once the buyer confirms it, or 7 days after the seller marks it done. Nothing here writes.

const REPLY_WINDOW_MS = 2 * DAY_MS;
const WEEK_MS = 7 * DAY_MS;
const RECORD_MS = 90 * DAY_MS;

/** When the sale counts from (E's 7-day rule, applied without writing), or null while it doesn't. */
function soldAt(row: ConversationRow, now: Date): string | null {
  if (row.status === "confirmed") return row.confirmedAt;
  if (row.status !== "done" || !row.completedAt) return null;
  const due = autoConfirmAt(row.completedAt);
  return Date.parse(due) <= now.getTime() ? due : null;
}

function counts(rows: readonly ConversationRow[], enquiriesSince: number, salesSince: number, now: Date): EnquiryCounts {
  const enquiries = rows.filter((row) => row.listingId !== null && Date.parse(row.createdAt) >= enquiriesSince);
  const inTime = enquiries.filter(
    (row) => row.firstReplyAt !== null && Date.parse(row.firstReplyAt) - Date.parse(row.createdAt) <= REPLY_WINDOW_MS,
  );
  const due = enquiries.filter(
    (row) => inTime.includes(row) || now.getTime() - Date.parse(row.createdAt) >= REPLY_WINDOW_MS,
  );
  const sales = rows.filter((row) => {
    const at = soldAt(row, now);
    return at !== null && Date.parse(at) >= salesSince;
  });
  return { enquiries: enquiries.length, due: due.length, repliedInTime: inTime.length, salesConfirmed: sales.length };
}

/** A shop's last 7 days, and its record for the badges: 90 days of enquiries, and every sale. */
export async function shopStats(sellerSlug: string, now: Date): Promise<ShopStats> {
  const rows = [...sampleStore().conversations.values()].filter((row) => row.sellerSlug === sellerSlug);
  const weekStart = now.getTime() - WEEK_MS;
  return {
    week: counts(rows, weekStart, weekStart, now),
    record: counts(rows, now.getTime() - RECORD_MS, Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY, now),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the route**

Create `app/photos/[id]/route.ts`:

```ts
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { getPhoto, isPhotoId } from "@/lib/data/photos";

/**
 * A stored photo (selling spec 2.4): its stripped bytes with their real type. A photo never changes once stored and
 * its id is random, so browsers may keep it for a year; pages show it resized through next/image (plan decision 12).
 * Supabase Storage replaces this route.
 */
export async function GET(_request: Request, { params }: RouteContext<"/photos/[id]">): Promise<Response> {
  // Per request, always: the store changes, so nothing here may be prerendered.
  await connection();
  const { id } = await params;
  const photo = isPhotoId(id) ? await getPhoto(id) : null;
  if (!photo) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404, headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } });
  return new Response(photo.bytes, {
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": photo.type,
      "Content-Length": String(photo.bytes.byteLength),
      "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=31536000, immutable",
    },
  });
}
```

(The site-wide `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff` header from `next.config.ts` applies here too, and the server only ever stores real JPEG, PNG or WebP files.)

```ts
    // resized; the SVG placeholders never are.
```

Create `lib/selling/action-helpers.ts`:

```ts
import { refresh } from "next/cache";
import { redirect, RedirectType } from "next/navigation";

// Shared by the selling Server Actions (selling plan, Tasks 7 and 8). A plain module that the "use server" files import.

/** A form field as text. A missing field, or a file, is "". */
export function field(formData: FormData, name: string): string {
  const value = formData.get(name);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : "";
}

/** Every text value of a repeated field, in order. */
export function fieldList(formData: FormData, name: string): string[] {
  return formData.getAll(name).filter((value): value is string => typeof value === "string");
}

/** The files a file input posted. An empty input posts one nameless, empty file, which is left out. */
export function fileList(formData: FormData, name: string): File[] {
  return formData.getAll(name).filter((value): value is File => typeof value !== "string" && value.size > 0);
}

/**
 * Ends an action (messaging plan decision 6): the browser drops its kept pages, and the redirect replaces this history
 * entry, so Back never lands on a form that's just been sent.
 */
export function done(path: string): never {
  refresh();
  redirect(path, RedirectType.replace);
}
```

```ts
import type { OnboardingStep } from "./paths";

// The selling forms' names in D's form state (accounts plan decision 3): their errors, typed values and notices, kept

export const onboardingForm = (step: OnboardingStep) => `onboarding:${step}`;

/** The dashboard's notice after Pause my shop or Resume. */

/** Edit shop's errors, typed values and notice. */
export const PROFILE_FORM = "shop-profile";

/** Forgets this browser's state for `form`. Server Actions only. */
export async function clearForm(form: string): Promise<void> {
  const sessionKey = await getSessionKey();
  if (sessionKey) await clearFormState(sessionKey, form);
}
```

```ts
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { getViewer, requireAccount } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import type { SellerRow } from "@/lib/data/rows";
import { getShopOf } from "@/lib/data/shops";
import type { Account, SellerStatus } from "@/lib/types";
import { SELL_PATH } from "./paths";

/** A signed-in seller and their shop: who may act on it (selling spec section 3). */
export type ShopOwner = { account: Account; shop: SellerRow; status: SellerStatus };

/** The signed-in account and its shop, or null. Request-time: call it inside <Suspense>. */
export async function getShopOwner(): Promise<ShopOwner | null> {
  const account = await getViewer();
  if (!account) return null;
  const own = await getShopOf(account.id);
  return own ? { account, ...own } : null;
}

/**
 * The signed-in account's shop (selling spec section 3). Signed out: sign in, then come back to `nextPath`. No shop:
 * the pitch at /sell. Pages call it inside <Suspense>; Server Actions call it first.
 */
export async function requireShopOwner(nextPath: string): Promise<ShopOwner> {
  const account = await requireAccount(nextPath);
  const own = await getShopOf(account.id);
  if (!own) redirect(SELL_PATH);
  return { account, ...own };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the uploads**

Create `lib/selling/uploads.ts`:

```ts
import type { Upload } from "@/lib/data/photos";
import { PHOTO_MAX_BYTES } from "@/lib/photos/rules";
import { photoTypeOf } from "@/lib/photos/signature";
import { stripMetadata } from "@/lib/photos/strip-metadata";

export type UploadError = "photo-type" | "photo-size" | "photo-unreadable" | "photo-count";

/**
 * Posted photo files, checked (selling spec 2.3, plan decision 11): at most `room` good ones, each up to 5 MB, each a
 * JPEG, PNG or WebP by its signature, each stripped of its metadata. Good ones come back in order, with null in a
 * refused one's place, so "new:<n>" still points at the right file. Each kind of problem is reported once. Nothing
 * is stored here.
 */
export async function readUploads(
  files: readonly File[],
  room: number,
): Promise<{ uploads: (Upload | null)[]; errors: UploadError[] }> {
  const errors = new Set<UploadError>();
  const uploads: (Upload | null)[] = [];
  // Only good photos take up room, so a refused file doesn't push a good one out.
  let accepted = 0;
  for (const file of files) {
    const result = await check(file, accepted < room);
    if (typeof result === "string") {
      errors.add(result);
      uploads.push(null);
    } else {
      accepted += 1;
      uploads.push(result);
    }
  }
  return { uploads, errors: [...errors] };
}

async function check(file: File, hasRoom: boolean): Promise<Upload | UploadError> {
  if (!hasRoom) return "photo-count";
  if (file.size > PHOTO_MAX_BYTES) return "photo-size";
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer());
  const type = photoTypeOf(bytes);
  if (!type) return "photo-type";
  const stripped = stripMetadata(bytes, type);
  return stripped ? { type, bytes: stripped } : "photo-unreadable";
}
```

```ts
  });

  it("sends a draft with problems back to its first step, and asks for the guidelines box", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-student");
    await saveOnboardingDraft({ ...finishedDraft(), about: "", pickupPointIds: [] });
    expect(await redirectOf(() => sendForApproval(form({ guidelines: "yes" })))).toBe("/sell/onboarding?step=2");
    expect((await readFormState(onboardingForm(2)))?.errors).toEqual(["about-missing"]);
    expect((await readFormState(onboardingForm(4)))?.errors).toEqual(["points-missing"]);
    await saveOnboardingDraft(finishedDraft());
    expect(await redirectOf(() => sendForApproval(form({})))).toBe("/sell/onboarding?step=5");
    expect((await readFormState(onboardingForm(5)))?.errors).toEqual(["guidelines-missing"]);
    expect(await getShopOf("acc-student")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("sends anyone with a shop to /sell, and anyone signed out to sign in first", async () => {
    expect(await redirectOf(() => saveOnboardingStep(form({ step: "2", about: "Hi" })))).toBe(
      "/login?next=%2Fsell%2Fonboarding%3Fstep%3D2",
    );
    await signedInAs("acc-pending");
    expect(await redirectOf(() => saveOnboardingStep(form({ step: "1", name: "Another shop", category: "food" })))).toBe(
      "/sell",
    );
    expect(await redirectOf(() => sendForApproval(form({ guidelines: "yes" })))).toBe("/sell");
  });
});

    campus: "midrand",

describe("editing the shop (selling spec 2.11, plan decisions 16 and 17)", () => {

    await signedInAs("acc-seller");

    expect([redirect.url, redirect.type, request.refreshed]).toEqual(["/sell/profile", "replace", true]);
    expect((await getShopOf("acc-seller"))?.shop).toMatchObject({
      tagline: "Calculators, tested.",

    });

  });

    expect(request.updatedTags).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("changes the link once, and never to one another shop has", async () => {

    await redirectOf(() => saveShopProfile(profile({ slug: "hair-studio" })));

    await redirectOf(() => saveShopProfile(profile({ slug: "Gadgets" })));

    await redirectOf(() => saveShopProfile(profile({ slug: "gizmos" })));

  });

    await signedInAs("acc-seller");

    const src = (await getShopOf("acc-seller"))!.shop.image!.src;
    expect(src).toMatch(/^\/photos\/ph-/);
    await redirectOf(() => saveShopProfile(profile({ removePhoto: "yes" })));
    expect((await getShopOf("acc-seller"))?.shop.image).toBeNull();
    expect(await getPhoto(photoIdOf(src)!)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("sends a student with no shop to /sell, changing nobody's shop", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-student");
    expect(await redirectOf(() => saveShopProfile(profile({ name: "Hijacked" })))).toBe("/sell");
    expect((await getShopOf("acc-seller"))?.shop.name).toBe("Tech corner");
  });
});

describe("pausing the shop (selling spec 2.8)", () => {
  it("pauses an approved shop and resumes it, saying so each time", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-seller");
    const redirect = await redirectSignalOf(() => pauseShop());
    expect([redirect.url, redirect.type]).toEqual(["/sell", "replace"]);
    expect((await getShopOf("acc-seller"))?.status).toBe("paused");
    expect((await readFormState(DASHBOARD_FORM))?.notice).toBe("shop-paused");
    await redirectOf(() => resumeShop());
    expect((await getShopOf("acc-seller"))?.status).toBe("approved");
    expect((await readFormState(DASHBOARD_FORM))?.notice).toBe("shop-resumed");
    expect(request.updatedTags).toEqual(["sellers", "sellers"]);
  });

  it("leaves a shop that's waiting for approval as it is", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-pending");
    await redirectOf(() => pauseShop());
    expect((await getShopOf("acc-pending"))?.status).toBe("pending");
    expect(request.updatedTags).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the shop actions**

Create `lib/selling/shop-actions.ts`:

```ts
"use server";

import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { writeFormState } from "@/lib/auth/form-state";
import { requireAccount } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { getCampuses, getCategories, getPickupPoints } from "@/lib/data";
import { storePhotos, type Upload } from "@/lib/data/photos";
import type { OnboardingDraftRow } from "@/lib/data/rows";
import {
  changeShopSlug,
  createShop,
  emptyDraft,
  getOnboardingDraft,
  getShopOf,
  isShopNameTaken,
  isSlugTaken,
  saveOnboardingDraft,
  setShopPaused,
  updateShop,
} from "@/lib/data/shops";
import { sellersChanged } from "@/lib/data/tags";
import { keepTyped } from "@/lib/messaging/rules";
import { done, field, fieldList, fileList } from "./action-helpers";
import { DASHBOARD_FORM, onboardingForm, PROFILE_FORM } from "./form-state";
import { requireShopOwner } from "./owner";
import { onboardingPath, parseStep, PROFILE_PATH, SELL_PATH, type OnboardingStep } from "./paths";
import { checkAbout, checkShopName, checkSlug, checkTagline } from "./rules";
import { readUploads } from "./uploads";

// The shop's Server Actions (selling spec 2.6 to 2.8 and 2.11): the wizard, Edit shop, and Pause my shop. Each is a
// plain form's action, so it works before the app's JavaScript arrives (spec 2.2). Identity comes only from the
// session; errors and typed values go to D's form state; every action ends with done() (refresh, then a replace
// redirect), and one that changes what buyers see expires the sellers tag first (plan decision 6).

async function isCategory(slug: string): Promise<boolean> {
  return (await getCategories()).some((category) => category.slug === slug);
}

async function spotsAt(campusSlug: string): Promise<string[]> {
  return (await getPickupPoints()).filter((point) => point.campusSlug === campusSlug).map((point) => point.id);
}

/**
 * The campus and pickup points (step 4 and Edit shop, plan decision 15). The form shows the points of the campus it was
 * drawn with (`pointsCampus`); when the seller picks another, it comes back with that campus's points to choose.
 */
async function readCampusAndSpots(
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<{ campusSlug: string; pickupPointIds: string[]; errors: string[] }> {
  const chosen = field(formData, "campus");
  const campusSlug = (await getCampuses()).some((campus) => campus.slug === chosen) ? chosen : "";
  if (!campusSlug) return { campusSlug, pickupPointIds: [], errors: ["campus-missing"] };
  const spots = await spotsAt(campusSlug);
  if (field(formData, "pointsCampus") !== campusSlug) {
    return { campusSlug, pickupPointIds: [], errors: spots.length > 0 ? ["points-campus"] : [] };
  }
  const pickupPointIds = [...new Set(fieldList(formData, "pickupPoint"))].filter((id) => spots.includes(id));
  const errors = spots.length > 0 && pickupPointIds.length === 0 ? ["points-missing"] : [];
  return { campusSlug, pickupPointIds, errors };
}

/** A shop photo from the form (spec 2.6, step 3), checked and stripped, not stored yet. */
async function readShopPhoto(formData: FormData): Promise<{ upload: Upload | null; errors: string[] }> {
  const files = fileList(formData, "photo");
  if (files.length === 0) return { upload: null, errors: [] };
  const { uploads, errors } = await readUploads(files.slice(0, 1), 1);
  return { upload: uploads[0] ?? null, errors };
}

/** Every step's problems with a draft, for Send for approval (plan decision 15). */
async function draftProblems(draft: OnboardingDraftRow): Promise<Map<OnboardingStep, string[]>> {
  const problems = new Map<OnboardingStep, string[]>();
  const note = (step: OnboardingStep, error: string | null) => {
    if (error) problems.set(step, [...(problems.get(step) ?? []), error]);
  };
  const name = checkShopName(draft.name);
  note(1, name.error ?? ((await isShopNameTaken(draft.name, draft.campusSlug, null)) ? "name-taken" : null));
  note(1, checkTagline(draft.tagline).error);
  note(1, (await isCategory(draft.categorySlug)) ? null : "category-missing");
  note(2, checkAbout(draft.about).error);
  const campusKnown = (await getCampuses()).some((campus) => campus.slug === draft.campusSlug);
  const spots = await spotsAt(draft.campusSlug);
  note(4, campusKnown ? null : "campus-missing");
  note(4, spots.length > 0 && !draft.pickupPointIds.some((id) => spots.includes(id)) ? "points-missing" : null);
  return problems;
}

/**
 * One of the wizard's first four steps (selling spec 2.6, plan decision 15). What was typed goes into the draft, and
 * Continue moves on only when the step is right; otherwise the step shows its errors. Step 3's photo is optional.
 */
export async function saveOnboardingStep(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const step = parseStep(field(formData, "step"));
  const account = await requireAccount(onboardingPath(step));
  if (await getShopOf(account.id)) redirect(SELL_PATH);
  const now = new Date();
  const draft = (await getOnboardingDraft(account.id)) ?? emptyDraft(account.id, account.campusSlug, now);
  const errors: string[] = [];
  let stay = false;
  switch (step) {
    case 1: {
      const name = checkShopName(field(formData, "name"));
      const tagline = checkTagline(field(formData, "tagline"));
      const categorySlug = field(formData, "category");
      draft.name = keepTyped(name.value);
      draft.tagline = keepTyped(tagline.value);
      draft.categorySlug = (await isCategory(categorySlug)) ? categorySlug : "";
      if (name.error) errors.push(name.error);
      else if (await isShopNameTaken(name.value, draft.campusSlug, null)) errors.push("name-taken");
      if (tagline.error) errors.push(tagline.error);
      if (!draft.categorySlug) errors.push("category-missing");
      break;
    }
    case 2: {
      const about = checkAbout(field(formData, "about"));
      draft.about = keepTyped(about.value);
      if (about.error) errors.push(about.error);
      break;
    }
    case 3: {
      if (field(formData, "photoAction") === "remove") {
        draft.photoSrc = null;
        stay = true;
        break;
      }
      const photo = await readShopPhoto(formData);
      errors.push(...photo.errors);
      if (photo.upload) [draft.photoSrc] = await storePhotos(account.id, [photo.upload], now);
      break;
    }
    case 4: {
      const place = await readCampusAndSpots(formData);
      if (place.campusSlug) draft.campusSlug = place.campusSlug;
      draft.pickupPointIds = place.pickupPointIds;
      errors.push(...place.errors);
      break;
    }
    case 5:
      // Step 5's form posts to sendForApproval.
      redirect(onboardingPath(5));
  }
  draft.updatedAt = now.toISOString();
  await saveOnboardingDraft(draft);
  await writeFormState(onboardingForm(step), { errors, values: {}, notice: null }, now);
  done(errors.length > 0 || stay ? onboardingPath(step) : onboardingPath((step + 1) as OnboardingStep));
}

/**
 * Send for approval (selling spec 2.6 step 5, and 2.7). Every step is checked again, and the first with a problem
 * opens with its errors (plan decision 15). Then the shop is made, waiting for the Hub, and the seller lands on /sell.
 */
export async function sendForApproval(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const account = await requireAccount(onboardingPath(5));
  if (await getShopOf(account.id)) redirect(SELL_PATH);
  const now = new Date();
  const draft = await getOnboardingDraft(account.id);
  if (!draft) done(onboardingPath(1));
  const problems = await draftProblems(draft);
  for (const [step, errors] of problems) {
    await writeFormState(onboardingForm(step), { errors, values: {}, notice: null }, now);
  }
  const first = [...problems.keys()].sort((a, b) => a - b)[0];
  if (first) done(onboardingPath(first));
  if (field(formData, "guidelines") !== "yes") {
    await writeFormState(onboardingForm(5), { errors: ["guidelines-missing"], values: {}, notice: null }, now);
    done(onboardingPath(5));
  }
  await createShop(
    account.id,
    {
      name: draft.name,
      tagline: draft.tagline,
      categorySlug: draft.categorySlug,
      about: draft.about,
      image: draft.photoSrc ? { src: draft.photoSrc, alt: "" } : null,
      campusSlug: draft.campusSlug,
      pickupPointIds: draft.pickupPointIds,
    },
    now,
  );
  sellersChanged();
  done(SELL_PATH);
}

/**
 * Edit shop (selling spec 2.11, plan decisions 15 to 17): the wizard's fields on one page, and the link, which an
 * approved or paused shop may change once. Nothing is saved until everything is right; then the shop, its listings
 * and, with a new link, everything that names the shop change together.
 */
export async function saveShopProfile(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const owner = await requireShopOwner(PROFILE_PATH);
  const now = new Date();
  const { shop, status } = owner;
  const errors: string[] = [];

  const name = checkShopName(field(formData, "name"));
  const tagline = checkTagline(field(formData, "tagline"));
  const categorySlug = field(formData, "category");
  const about = checkAbout(field(formData, "about"));
  const removePhoto = field(formData, "removePhoto") === "yes";
  const photo = removePhoto ? { upload: null, errors: [] } : await readShopPhoto(formData);
  const place = await readCampusAndSpots(formData);
  const typedSlug = field(formData, "slug").trim().toLowerCase();
  const changeSlug = status !== "pending" && !shop.slugChanged && typedSlug !== "" && typedSlug !== shop.slug;
  const slug = checkSlug(typedSlug);

  // In the form's order, so the error summary reads top to bottom.
  if (name.error) errors.push(name.error);
  else if (place.campusSlug && (await isShopNameTaken(name.value, place.campusSlug, shop.slug))) errors.push("name-taken");
  if (tagline.error) errors.push(tagline.error);
  if (!(await isCategory(categorySlug))) errors.push("category-missing");
  if (about.error) errors.push(about.error);
  errors.push(...photo.errors, ...place.errors);
  if (changeSlug && slug.error) errors.push(slug.error);
  else if (changeSlug && (await isSlugTaken(slug.value))) errors.push("slug-taken");

  if (errors.length > 0) {
    const values = {
      name: keepTyped(name.value),
      tagline: keepTyped(tagline.value),
      category: categorySlug,
      about: keepTyped(about.value),
      campus: place.campusSlug,
      points: place.pickupPointIds.join(" "),
      slug: keepTyped(typedSlug),
    };
    await writeFormState(PROFILE_FORM, { errors, values, notice: null }, now);
    done(PROFILE_PATH);
  }

  let image = removePhoto ? null : shop.image;
  if (photo.upload) {
    const [src] = await storePhotos(owner.account.id, [photo.upload], now);
    image = { src, alt: "" };
  }
  await updateShop(
    owner.account.id,
    {
      name: name.value,
      tagline: tagline.value,
      categorySlug,
      about: about.value,
      image,
      campusSlug: place.campusSlug,
      pickupPointIds: place.pickupPointIds,
    },
    now,
  );
  if (changeSlug) await changeShopSlug(owner.account.id, slug.value, now);
  sellersChanged();
  await writeFormState(PROFILE_FORM, { errors: [], values: {}, notice: "shop-saved" }, now);
  done(PROFILE_PATH);
}

async function setPaused(paused: boolean): Promise<never> {
  const owner = await requireShopOwner(SELL_PATH);
  if (await setShopPaused(owner.account.id, paused)) {
    sellersChanged();
    const notice = paused ? "shop-paused" : "shop-resumed";
    await writeFormState(DASHBOARD_FORM, { errors: [], values: {}, notice }, new Date());
  }
  done(SELL_PATH);
}

/** Pause my shop (selling spec 2.8): buyers stop seeing its listings until Resume. Only an approved shop pauses. */
export async function pauseShop(): Promise<void> {
  await setPaused(true);
}

/** Resume (selling spec 2.8). */
export async function resumeShop(): Promise<void> {
  await setPaused(false);
}
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { readFormState } from "@/lib/auth/form-state";
import { hashToken } from "@/lib/auth/secrets";

import { listingForm, MY_LISTINGS_FORM } from "./form-state";
import { changeListingState, deleteListingForm, saveListingForm } from "./listing-actions";

const request = vi.hoisted((): FakeRequest => ({ cookies: new Map(), sent: new Map(), proto: "http" }));

vi.mock("next/headers", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextHeaders(request));
vi.mock("next/navigation", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextNavigation());
vi.mock("next/server", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextServer());
vi.mock("next/cache", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextCache(request));

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-14T08:42:00.000Z");
const IN_30_DAYS = new Date("2026-11-13T08:42:00.000Z");

function form(fields: Record<string, string | string[] | File | File[]>): FormData {
  const data = new FormData();
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(fields)) {
    for (const item of Array.isArray(value) ? value : [value]) data.append(name, item);
  }
  return data;
}

async function signedInAs(accountId: string) {
  await createSession({ key: hashToken("token-1"), accountId, createdAt: NOW, expiresAt: IN_30_DAYS });
  request.cookies.set(SESSION_COOKIE, "token-1");
}

const photoFile = (bytes: Uint8Array, name = "photo.png") => new File([bytes], name, { type: "image/png" });

/** A good listing form for Tech corner: a desk fan at the library, to publish. */
const fanForm = (overrides: Record<string, string | string[] | File | File[]> = {}) =>
  form({
    kind: "item",
    title: "Desk fan",
    category: "res-living",
    condition: "good",
    price: "150",
    payMode: "cash",
    description: "Quiet and strong.",
    handover: "pickup",
    pickup: "pp-midrand-library",
    intent: "publish",
    ...overrides,
  });

const fan = async () => (await listOwnListings("tech-corner")).find((row) => row.title === "Desk fan");

describe("saving a listing (selling spec 2.10, plan decisions 3 and 4)", () => {

    await signedInAs("acc-seller");
    const redirect = await redirectSignalOf(() => saveListingForm(fanForm()));
    expect([redirect.url, redirect.type, request.refreshed]).toEqual(["/sell/listings", "replace", true]);
    const row = (await fan())!;
    expect(row).toMatchObject({ state: "available", priceZar: 150, campusSlug: "midrand", photos: [] });
    expect(request.updatedTags).toEqual(["listings"]);
    expect(await readFormState(MY_LISTINGS_FORM)).toMatchObject({ notice: "published", values: { listing: row.id } });
  });

  it("saves a draft, and a waiting shop's Save and publish as a draft too", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-seller");
    expect(await redirectOf(() => saveListingForm(fanForm({ intent: "draft" })))).toBe("/sell/listings?tab=drafts");
    expect((await fan())?.state).toBe("draft");
    expect((await readFormState(MY_LISTINGS_FORM))?.notice).toBe("drafted");
    await signedInAs("acc-pending");
    await redirectOf(() => saveListingForm(fanForm({ pickup: "pp-pretoria-library" })));
    expect((await listOwnListings("crochet-corner"))[0]).toMatchObject({ title: "Desk fan", state: "draft" });
  });

  it("keeps everything typed, the errors and the uploaded photo when a save fails", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-seller");
    const failing = fanForm({ title: "", price: "lots", newPhotos: photoFile(pngBytes()) });
    expect(await redirectOf(() => saveListingForm(failing))).toBe("/sell/listings/new");
    const state = (await readFormState(listingForm(null)))!;
    expect(state.errors).toEqual(["title-missing", "price-invalid"]);
    expect(state.values).toMatchObject({ title: "", price: "lots", category: "res-living" });
    expect(state.values.photos).toMatch(/^\/photos\/ph-/);
    expect(await getPhoto(photoIdOf(state.values.photos)!)).not.toBeNull();
    expect(await fan()).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("strips each upload, puts the photos in the order posted, and keeps each one's description", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-seller");
    const posted = fanForm({
      photo: ["new:1", "new:0"],
      newPhotos: [photoFile(pngBytes({ exif: true }), "back.png"), photoFile(pngBytes({ text: true }), "front.png")],
      "alt:new:1": " The fan from the front ",
    });
    await redirectOf(() => saveListingForm(posted));
    const [front, back] = (await fan())!.photos;
    expect([front.alt, back.alt]).toEqual(["The fan from the front", ""]);
    for (const photo of [front, back]) {
      expect(containsText((await getPhoto(photoIdOf(photo.src)!))!.bytes, GPS)).toBe(false);
    }
  });

  it("moves a photo without saving the listing, keeping what was typed, and comes back to the photos", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-seller");
    const moving = fanForm({ listing: "l-001", title: "Typed but not saved", photo: [COVER, BACK, CASE], photoMove: `${CASE}:cover` });
    expect(await redirectOf(() => saveListingForm(moving))).toBe("/sell/listings/l-001/edit#edit-l-001-photos");
    const state = (await readFormState(listingForm("l-001")))!;
    expect(state.values).toMatchObject({ title: "Typed but not saved", photos: [CASE, COVER, BACK].join("\n") });
    expect((await getOwnListing("tech-corner", "l-001"))?.title).toBe("Casio fx-991ZA Plus calculator");
    expect(request.updatedTags).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("refuses a file that isn't a photo, and photos past the sixth", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-seller");
    const files = [ascii("GIF89a"), pngBytes(), pngBytes(), pngBytes(), pngBytes()].map((bytes) => photoFile(bytes));
    await redirectOf(() => saveListingForm(fanForm({ listing: "l-001", photo: [COVER, BACK, CASE], newPhotos: files })));
    const state = (await readFormState(listingForm("l-001")))!;
    expect(state.errors).toEqual(["photo-type", "photo-count"]);
    expect(state.values.photos.split("\n")).toHaveLength(6);
  });

  it("drops photos that aren't the seller's own", async () => {
    const [theirs] = await storePhotos("acc-student", [{ type: "image/png", bytes: pngBytes() }], NOW);
    await signedInAs("acc-seller");
    await redirectOf(() => saveListingForm(fanForm({ photo: [theirs, "/placeholders/listing/food.svg"] })));
    expect((await fan())?.photos).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("saves changes to a listing, and keeps a sold one sold", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-seller");
    expect(await redirectOf(() => saveListingForm(fanForm({ listing: "l-001", title: "Desk fan" })))).toBe("/sell/listings");
    expect((await readFormState(MY_LISTINGS_FORM))?.notice).toBe("saved");
    await setOwnListingState("tech-corner", "l-001", "sold", NOW);
    expect(await redirectOf(() => saveListingForm(fanForm({ listing: "l-001" })))).toBe("/sell/listings?tab=sold");
    expect((await getOwnListing("tech-corner", "l-001"))?.state).toBe("sold");
  });
});

describe("My listings' buttons and Delete listing (selling spec 2.9, plan decision 3)", () => {
  it("pauses, resumes, marks sold and relists, coming back to the same tab and saying so", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-seller");
    const move = (to: string, tab: string) => redirectOf(() => changeListingState(form({ listing: "l-001", to, tab })));
    expect(await move("paused", "available")).toBe("/sell/listings");
    expect((await getOwnListing("tech-corner", "l-001"))?.state).toBe("paused");
    expect((await readFormState(MY_LISTINGS_FORM))?.notice).toBe("paused");
    expect(await move("available", "paused")).toBe("/sell/listings?tab=paused");
    expect((await readFormState(MY_LISTINGS_FORM))?.notice).toBe("resumed");
    await move("sold", "available");
    await move("available", "sold");
    expect((await getOwnListing("tech-corner", "l-001"))?.state).toBe("available");
    await move("draft", "available");
    expect((await getOwnListing("tech-corner", "l-001"))?.state).toBe("available");
    expect(request.updatedTags).toEqual(["listings", "listings", "listings", "listings"]);
  });

  it("deletes a listing and its photos, saying so", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-seller");
    await redirectOf(() => saveListingForm(fanForm({ newPhotos: photoFile(pngBytes()) })));
    const row = (await fan())!;
    expect(await redirectOf(() => deleteListingForm(form({ listing: row.id })))).toBe("/sell/listings");
    expect(await getOwnListing("tech-corner", row.id)).toBeNull();
    expect(await getPhoto(photoIdOf(row.photos[0].src)!)).toBeNull();
    expect((await readFormState(MY_LISTINGS_FORM))?.notice).toBe("deleted");
  });

  it("never touches another shop's listing", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-seller");
    expect(await redirectOf(() => saveListingForm(fanForm({ listing: "l-004" })))).toBe("/sell/listings");
    await redirectOf(() => changeListingState(form({ listing: "l-004", to: "paused", tab: "available" })));
    await redirectOf(() => deleteListingForm(form({ listing: "l-004" })));
    expect(sampleStore().listings.get("l-004")).toMatchObject({ title: "Box braids, shoulder length", state: "available" });
    expect(request.updatedTags).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("sends a signed-out visitor to sign in, and a student with no shop to /sell", async () => {
    expect(await redirectOf(() => saveListingForm(fanForm()))).toBe("/login?next=%2Fsell%2Flistings%2Fnew");
    await signedInAs("acc-student");
    expect(await redirectOf(() => saveListingForm(fanForm()))).toBe("/sell");
    expect(await redirectOf(() => changeListingState(form({ listing: "l-001", to: "paused", tab: "available" })))).toBe(
      "/sell",
    );
    expect((await getOwnListing("tech-corner", "l-001"))?.state).toBe("available");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the listing actions**

Create `lib/selling/listing-actions.ts`:

```ts
"use server";

import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { writeFormState } from "@/lib/auth/form-state";
import { getCategories } from "@/lib/data";
import { deleteOwnListing, getOwnListing, saveOwnListing, setOwnListingState } from "@/lib/data/own-listings";
import { photoSrcsOf, storePhotos, type Upload } from "@/lib/data/photos";
import { listingsChanged } from "@/lib/data/tags";
import { isId } from "@/lib/messaging/paths";
import { characterCount, keepTyped } from "@/lib/messaging/rules";
import { PHOTOS_PER_LISTING } from "@/lib/photos/rules";
import { done, field, fieldList, fileList } from "./action-helpers";
import { listingFieldPrefix } from "./errors";
import { clearForm, listingForm, MY_LISTINGS_FORM } from "./form-state";
import { requireShopOwner } from "./owner";
import { editListingPath, myListingsPath, NEW_LISTING_PATH, parseListingsTab, tabFor } from "./paths";
import { applyPhotoMove, parsePhotoMove, photoOrder, resolveToken } from "./photo-order";
import { ALT_MAX, checkListing, cleanLine, type ListingInput } from "./rules";
import { readUploads } from "./uploads";

// The listing Server Actions (selling spec 2.9 and 2.10): the listing form's save and photo buttons, My listings'
// row buttons, and Delete listing. Identity comes only from the session, and every listing id from a form is looked
// up among the signed-in seller's own listings. Each ends with done(), after listingsChanged() when what buyers see
// changed (plan decision 6).

function listingInput(formData: FormData): ListingInput {
  return {
    kind: field(formData, "kind"),
    title: field(formData, "title"),
    categorySlug: field(formData, "category"),
    condition: field(formData, "condition"),
    price: field(formData, "price"),
    free: field(formData, "free") === "yes",
    priceIsFrom: field(formData, "from") === "yes",
    payMode: field(formData, "payMode"),
    swapFor: field(formData, "swapFor"),
    description: field(formData, "description"),
    handover: field(formData, "handover"),
    pickupPointId: field(formData, "pickup"),
  };
}

/** What the form shows again after a failed save or a photo button (plan decision 9): all that was typed, the photos in order, and each one's description. */
function typedValues(
  formData: FormData,
  order: readonly string[],
  alts: ReadonlyMap<string, string>,
): Record<string, string> {
  const values: Record<string, string> = {
    kind: field(formData, "kind"),
    title: keepTyped(field(formData, "title")),
    category: field(formData, "category"),
    condition: field(formData, "condition"),
    price: keepTyped(field(formData, "price")),
    free: field(formData, "free"),
    from: field(formData, "from"),
    payMode: field(formData, "payMode"),
    swapFor: keepTyped(field(formData, "swapFor")),
    description: keepTyped(field(formData, "description")),
    handover: field(formData, "handover"),
    pickup: field(formData, "pickup"),
    photos: order.join("\n"),
  };
  for (const src of order) values[`alt:${src}`] = keepTyped(alts.get(src) ?? "");
  return values;
}

/**
 * The listing form (selling spec 2.10): Save and publish, Save as draft, and the photo buttons. Photos are checked,
 * stripped and stored first, so a save that fails, or a photo button pressed without the app's JavaScript, keeps
 * them (plan decisions 9 and 11). A shop waiting for approval saves drafts only (plan decision 4).
 */
export async function saveListingForm(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const posted = field(formData, "listing");
  const owner = await requireShopOwner(posted && isId(posted) ? editListingPath(posted) : NEW_LISTING_PATH);
  const now = new Date();
  const existing = posted && isId(posted) ? await getOwnListing(owner.shop.slug, posted) : null;
  if (posted && !existing) redirect(myListingsPath());
  const id = existing?.id ?? null;
  const form = listingForm(id);
  const formPath = id ? editListingPath(id) : NEW_LISTING_PATH;

  // Photos: the listing's own, and photos this seller uploaded on a form that hasn't saved yet. Anything else is dropped.
  const tokens = fieldList(formData, "photo");
  const allowed = new Set([
    ...(existing?.photos.map((photo) => photo.src) ?? []),
    ...(await photoSrcsOf(owner.account.id, null)),
  ]);
  const room = PHOTOS_PER_LISTING - new Set(tokens.filter((token) => allowed.has(token))).size;
  const { uploads, errors: uploadErrors } = await readUploads(fileList(formData, "newPhotos"), room);
  const queue = await storePhotos(
    owner.account.id,
    uploads.filter((upload): upload is Upload => upload !== null),
    now,
  );
  const stored = uploads.map((upload) => (upload ? (queue.shift() ?? null) : null));
  const { order, tooMany } = photoOrder(tokens, allowed, stored);
  const alts = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const token of tokens) {
    const src = resolveToken(token, stored);
    if (src && order.includes(src)) alts.set(src, cleanLine(field(formData, `alt:${token}`)));
  }
  const photoErrors: string[] = [...uploadErrors];
  if (tooMany && !photoErrors.includes("photo-count")) photoErrors.push("photo-count");

  // A photo button: apply it, keep everything typed, and come back to the photos.
  const move = parsePhotoMove(field(formData, "photoMove"));
  if (move) {
    const token = resolveToken(move.token, stored);
    const moved = token ? applyPhotoMove(order, { token, op: move.op }) : order;
    await writeFormState(form, { errors: photoErrors, values: typedValues(formData, moved, alts), notice: null }, now);
    done(`${formPath}#${listingFieldPrefix(id)}-photos`);
  }

  const categories = await getCategories();
  const check = checkListing(listingInput(formData), {
    categorySlugs: categories.map((category) => category.slug),
    pickupPointIds: owner.shop.pickupPointIds,
  });
  const errors: string[] = [...check.errors, ...photoErrors];
  if ([...alts.values()].some((alt) => characterCount(alt) > ALT_MAX)) errors.push("alt-length");
  if (errors.length > 0 || !check.value) {
    await writeFormState(form, { errors, values: typedValues(formData, order, alts), notice: null }, now);
    done(formPath);
  }

  const intent = owner.status !== "pending" && field(formData, "intent") !== "draft" ? "publish" : "draft";
  const saved = await saveOwnListing({
    sellerSlug: owner.shop.slug,
    ownerId: owner.account.id,
    id,
    fields: check.value,
    photos: order.map((src) => ({ src, alt: alts.get(src) ?? "" })),
    intent,
    now,
  });
  if (!saved) redirect(myListingsPath());
  await clearForm(form);
  listingsChanged();
  let notice = "published";
  if (saved.state === "draft") notice = "drafted";
  else if (existing && existing.state !== "draft") notice = "saved";
  await writeFormState(MY_LISTINGS_FORM, { errors: [], values: { listing: saved.id }, notice }, now);
  done(myListingsPath(tabFor(saved.state)));
}

const NOTICE_FOR = { paused: "paused", available: "resumed", sold: "sold" } as const;

/** Pause, Resume, Mark sold and Relist on My listings (selling spec 2.9, plan decision 3), back to the same tab. */
export async function changeListingState(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const tab = parseListingsTab(field(formData, "tab"));
  const owner = await requireShopOwner(myListingsPath(tab));
  const id = field(formData, "listing");
  const to = field(formData, "to");
  if (isId(id) && (to === "available" || to === "paused" || to === "sold")) {
    const now = new Date();
    const row = await setOwnListingState(owner.shop.slug, id, to, now);
    if (row) {
      listingsChanged();
      await writeFormState(MY_LISTINGS_FORM, { errors: [], values: { listing: row.id }, notice: NOTICE_FOR[to] }, now);
    }
  }
  done(myListingsPath(tab));
}

/** Delete listing, from its confirm page (selling spec 2.9): it and its photos go; conversations keep its title. */
export async function deleteListingForm(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const owner = await requireShopOwner(myListingsPath());
  const id = field(formData, "listing");
  const row = isId(id) ? await getOwnListing(owner.shop.slug, id) : null;
  if (row && (await deleteOwnListing(owner.shop.slug, owner.account.id, row.id))) {
    listingsChanged();
    await writeFormState(MY_LISTINGS_FORM, { errors: [], values: {}, notice: "deleted" }, new Date());
  }
  done(myListingsPath(row ? tabFor(row.state) : "available"));
}
```

### Task 9: `/sell`: the pitch, waiting for approval, and the dashboard

`/sell` (spec section 3, 2.7 and 2.8): the pitch for anyone without a shop, the waiting page while the Hub checks one, and the dashboard once it's approved or paused. import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
```tsx
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { emptyDraft, saveOnboardingDraft } from "@/lib/data/shops";
import { resetSampleStore, sampleStore } from "@/lib/data/store";
import { SellHome } from "./SellHome";

const viewer = vi.hoisted(() => ({ id: null as string | null }));

// The session is the sample account named here; everything else is the real sample store.
vi.mock("@/lib/auth/session", async () => {
  const { getAccount } = await import("@/lib/data/accounts");
  return { getViewer: async () => (viewer.id ? getAccount(viewer.id) : null), getSessionKey: async () => null };
});
vi.mock("next/server", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextServer());
vi.mock("@/lib/selling/shop-actions", () => ({ pauseShop: async () => {}, resumeShop: async () => {} }));

beforeEach(() => {
  resetSampleStore();
  viewer.id = null;
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] });
  vi.setSystemTime(new Date("2026-10-14T10:00:00.000Z"));
});

describe("/sell (selling spec section 3)", () => {
  it("pitches to a signed-out visitor, with Start selling through sign-in to the wizard", async () => {
    render(await SellHome());
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveTextContent("Turn your hustle into a campus-wide business");
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Start selling" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/login?next=%2Fsell%2Fonboarding");
  });

  it("sends a signed-in student without a shop to the wizard, or back to where they stopped", async () => {
    viewer.id = "acc-student";
    const first = render(await SellHome());
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Start selling" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/sell/onboarding");
    first.unmount();
    const draft = emptyDraft("acc-student", "midrand", new Date());
    await saveOnboardingDraft({ ...draft, name: "Zola's Bakes", categorySlug: "food" });
    render(await SellHome());
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Continue setting up your shop" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/sell/onboarding?step=2",
    );
  });

  it("tells a seller waiting for approval that the Hub is checking their shop (spec 2.7)", async () => {
    viewer.id = "acc-pending";
    render(await SellHome());
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "The Hub is checking your shop" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/You sent Crochet corner for approval on 10 October 2026\./)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "New listing" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/sell/listings/new");
  });

  it("shows an approved seller the dashboard: this week's numbers and the next badge (spec 2.8)", async () => {
    viewer.id = "acc-seller";
    render(await SellHome());
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "Tech corner" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Your shop is live")).toBeInTheDocument();
    const week = screen.getByRole("region", { name: "In the last 7 days" });
    expect(within(week).getByText("New enquiries").nextElementSibling).toHaveTextContent("2");
    expect(within(week).getByText("Replied within 48 hours").nextElementSibling).toHaveTextContent("2 of 2");
    expect(within(week).getByText("Sales confirmed").nextElementSibling).toHaveTextContent("0");
    expect(screen.getByText("1 more enquiry and 5 more confirmed sales for Trusted.")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows a paused shop as paused, with Resume", async () => {
    viewer.id = "acc-seller";
    sampleStore().sellerProfiles.get("acc-seller")!.status = "paused";
    render(await SellHome());
    expect(screen.getByText("Paused: buyers can't see your listings")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Resume my shop" })).toHaveAttribute("type", "submit");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the pitch, the waiting page and the dashboard**

Create `components/sell/SellPitch.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";

type SellPitchProps = {
  /** Where Start selling goes: through sign-in when signed out, else the wizard's first unanswered step. */
  startHref: string;
  /** A half-done application: the button says so. */
  resuming: boolean;
};

/**
 * The pitch (selling spec section 3): why sell on Vossie, the three steps, the fees (none), and Start selling. The
 * words are samples for Lama to change (plan decision 27).
 */
export function SellPitch({ startHref, resuming }: SellPitchProps) {
  return (
    <div className="sell-column">
      <header className="sell-head">
        <p className="eyebrow">Sell on Vossie</p>
        <h1 className="sell-title">Turn your hustle into a campus-wide business</h1>
        <p className="sell-lede">
          List what you make, sell or know, for students on every Eduvos campus. It&apos;s free.
        </p>
        <Link href={startHref} prefetch={false} className="btn btn-primary">
          {resuming ? "Continue setting up your shop" : "Start selling"}
        </Link>
      </header>

      <section aria-labelledby="sell-why-title" className="sell-section">
        <h2 id="sell-why-title" className="sell-section-title">
          Why sell here
        </h2>
        <ul className="sell-cards">
          <li>
            <strong>Buyers on your campus.</strong> Students find you by campus and category, and meet you at a pickup
            point you choose.
          </li>
          <li>
            <strong>Messages stay on Vossie.</strong> Buyers message you here, so your number stays yours.
          </li>
          <li>
            <strong>The Hub has your back.</strong> The Eduvos Incubation Hub checks every shop and helps you grow.
          </li>
        </ul>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="sell-steps-title" className="sell-section">
        <h2 id="sell-steps-title" className="sell-section-title">
          Three steps
        </h2>
        <ol className="sell-cards sell-numbered">
          <li>Set up your shop: five short steps, about three minutes.</li>
          <li>The Hub checks it, usually within two working days.</li>
          <li>List what you sell, and answer buyers&apos; messages.</li>
        </ol>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="sell-fees-title" className="sell-section">
        <h2 id="sell-fees-title" className="sell-section-title">
          Fees
        </h2>
        <p>None. Vossie takes no cut and sells no places. Buyers pay you when you meet.</p>
      </section>

      <p className="sell-note">
        Read the{" "}
        <Link href="/seller-guidelines" prefetch={false}>
          seller guidelines
        </Link>{" "}
        before you start.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { myListingsPath, NEW_LISTING_PATH, PROFILE_PATH } from "@/lib/selling/paths";
import { longSastDate } from "@/lib/time";

type ShopWaitingProps = {
  shopName: string;
  /** When the application was sent, ISO 8601 in UTC. */
  sentAt: string;
};

/**
 * Waiting for approval (selling spec 2.7): what the Hub is doing, what happens next, and that listings can wait as
 * drafts. The words are samples for Lama to change (plan decision 27).
 */
export function ShopWaiting({ shopName, sentAt }: ShopWaitingProps) {
  return (
    <div className="sell-column">
      <header className="sell-head">
        <p className="eyebrow">Your shop</p>
        <h1 className="sell-title">The Hub is checking your shop</h1>
        <p className="sell-lede">
          You sent {shopName} for approval on {longSastDate(sentAt)}. The Hub usually takes up to two working days.
        </p>
      </header>

      <section aria-labelledby="sell-next-title" className="sell-section">
        <h2 id="sell-next-title" className="sell-section-title">
          What happens next
        </h2>
        <ol className="sell-cards sell-numbered">
          <li>The Hub looks at your shop&apos;s name, photo and what you&apos;ll sell.</li>
          <li>Once it&apos;s approved, you&apos;ll see it here and in your notifications.</li>
          <li>Your shop goes live, and you can publish your listings.</li>
        </ol>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="sell-prepare-title" className="sell-section">
        <h2 id="sell-prepare-title" className="sell-section-title">
          Get your listings ready
        </h2>
        <p>
          You can prepare listings now. They&apos;re saved as drafts, which only you can see, until your shop is
          approved.
        </p>
        <div className="sell-actions">
          <Link href={NEW_LISTING_PATH} prefetch={false} className="btn btn-primary">
            New listing
          </Link>
          <Link href={myListingsPath("drafts")} prefetch={false} className="btn btn-outline text-navy">
            Your drafts
          </Link>
          <Link href={PROFILE_PATH} prefetch={false} className="link-button">
            Edit your shop
          </Link>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```
