Create `components/sell/Dashboard.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { ChevronRightIcon } from "@/components/icons";
import { cx } from "@/lib/cx";
import { badgeLabel } from "@/lib/format";
import { inboxPath } from "@/lib/messaging/paths";
import { myListingsPath, NEW_LISTING_PATH, PROFILE_PATH } from "@/lib/selling/paths";
import { pauseShop, resumeShop } from "@/lib/selling/shop-actions";
import type { ShopStats, TrustBadge } from "@/lib/types";

type DashboardProps = {
  shop: { name: string; slug: string; badge: TrustBadge | null };
  paused: boolean;
  stats: ShopStats;
  /** What the next badge needs (lib/selling/badges.ts). */
  badgeLine: string;
  /** The words for the last action's notice, if any. */
  notice: string | null;
};

/**
 * The dashboard (selling spec 2.8, plan decision 18): whether the shop is live, the last 7 days' numbers, the badge
 * and what the next one needs, the shop's pages, and Pause my shop or Resume. No view counts: nothing records views.
 */
export function Dashboard({ shop, paused, stats, badgeLine, notice }: DashboardProps) {
  const { week } = stats;
  const pages: ReadonlyArray<[label: string, href: string]> = [
    ["New listing", NEW_LISTING_PATH],
    ["My listings", myListingsPath()],
    ["Messages from buyers", inboxPath("selling")],
    ["Edit shop", PROFILE_PATH],
    ["View your shop", `/sellers/${shop.slug}`],
  ];
  return (
    <div className="sell-column">
      <header className="sell-head">
        <p className="eyebrow">Your shop</p>
        <h1 className="sell-title">{shop.name}</h1>
        <p className={cx("sell-status", paused && "sell-status-paused")}>
          {paused ? "Paused: buyers can't see your listings" : "Your shop is live"}
        </p>
      </header>

      {notice && (
        <p role="status" className="form-notice">
          {notice}
        </p>
      )}

      <section aria-labelledby="sell-week-title" className="sell-section">
        <h2 id="sell-week-title" className="sell-section-title">
          In the last 7 days
        </h2>
        <dl className="sell-numbers">
          <div>
            <dt>New enquiries</dt>
            <dd>{week.enquiries}</dd>
          </div>
          <div>
            <dt>Replied within 48 hours</dt>
            <dd>{week.due === 0 ? "None due yet" : `${week.repliedInTime} of ${week.due}`}</dd>
          </div>
          <div>
            <dt>Sales confirmed</dt>
            <dd>{week.salesConfirmed}</dd>
          </div>
        </dl>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="sell-badge-title" className="sell-section">
        <h2 id="sell-badge-title" className="sell-section-title">
          Your badge
        </h2>
        <p className="sell-badge">{shop.badge ? badgeLabel(shop.badge) : "No badge yet"}</p>
        <p>
          {badgeLine}{" "}
          <Link href="/trust" prefetch={false}>
            How badges work
          </Link>
        </p>
      </section>

      <nav aria-labelledby="sell-pages-title" className="sell-section">
        <h2 id="sell-pages-title" className="sell-section-title">
          Your shop&apos;s pages
        </h2>
        <ul className="sell-rows">
          {pages.map(([label, href]) => (
            <li key={href}>
              <Link href={href} prefetch={false} className="sell-row">
                {label}
                <ChevronRightIcon />
              </Link>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </nav>

      <form action={paused ? resumeShop : pauseShop} className="sell-pause">
        <p>
          {paused
            ? "Your listings come back as soon as you resume."
            : "Taking a break? Pausing hides your listings until you resume."}
        </p>
        <button type="submit" className="btn btn-outline text-navy">
          {paused ? "Resume my shop" : "Pause my shop"}
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `components/sell/SellHome.tsx`:

```tsx
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { readFormState } from "@/lib/auth/form-state";
import { getViewer } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import type { OnboardingDraftRow } from "@/lib/data/rows";
import { shopStats } from "@/lib/data/shop-stats";
import { getOnboardingDraft, getShopOf } from "@/lib/data/shops";
import { signInHref } from "@/lib/next-path";
import { badgeProgress } from "@/lib/selling/badges";
import { DASHBOARD_FORM } from "@/lib/selling/form-state";
import { noticeText } from "@/lib/selling/notices";
import { onboardingPath, type OnboardingStep } from "@/lib/selling/paths";
import { Dashboard } from "./Dashboard";
import { SellPitch } from "./SellPitch";
import { ShopWaiting } from "./ShopWaiting";

/** Where a half-done application picks up: the first step not yet answered (step 3's photo is optional). */
function resumeStep(draft: OnboardingDraftRow): OnboardingStep {
  if (!draft.name || !draft.categorySlug) return 1;
  if (!draft.about) return 2;
  if (draft.pickupPointIds.length === 0) return 3;
  return 5;
}

/**
 * Which /sell this visitor sees (selling spec section 3): the pitch without a shop, the waiting page while the Hub
 * checks one (spec 2.7), the dashboard once it's approved or paused (spec 2.8). Request-time: it reads the session.
 */
export async function SellHome() {
  const account = await getViewer().catch(() => null);
  if (!account) return <SellPitch startHref={signInHref(onboardingPath(1))} resuming={false} />;
  const own = await getShopOf(account.id);
  if (!own) {
    const draft = await getOnboardingDraft(account.id);
    return <SellPitch startHref={onboardingPath(draft ? resumeStep(draft) : 1)} resuming={draft !== null} />;
  }
  if (own.status === "pending") return <ShopWaiting shopName={own.shop.name} sentAt={own.shop.createdAt} />;
  // The week's numbers read the clock (decision 26).
  await connection();
  const now = new Date();
  const [stats, state] = await Promise.all([shopStats(own.shop.slug, now), readFormState(DASHBOARD_FORM)]);
  return (
    <Dashboard
      shop={{ name: own.shop.name, slug: own.shop.slug, badge: own.shop.badge }}
      paused={own.status === "paused"}
      stats={stats}
      badgeLine={badgeProgress(own.shop.badge, stats.record, account.createdAt, now)}
      notice={noticeText(state?.notice)}
    />
  );
}
```

```css
/* --- Selling: the frame, the pitch, waiting and the dashboard (selling plan, Task 9, spec 2.7, 2.8 and section 3) --- */
@layer components {
  /* .page and .sell-page sit on one element, so this padding is the whole content edge: 16px, then 40px. */
  .sell-page {
    padding: 24px 16px 64px;
  }

  @media (min-width: 48rem) and (min-height: 30rem) {
    .sell-page {
      padding: 40px 40px 96px;
    }
  }

  /* One readable column, centred in the page column. */
  .sell-column {
    display: grid;
    gap: 32px;
    max-width: 44rem;
    margin-inline: auto;
  }

  .sell-head {
    display: grid;
    justify-items: start;
    gap: 12px;
  }

  .sell-title {
    color: var(--color-navy);
    font-family: var(--vos-font-display);
    font-size: var(--text-section);
    line-height: 1;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    overflow-wrap: anywhere;
  }

  .sell-lede {
    max-width: 36rem;
    color: var(--color-ink);
    font-size: 18px;
  }

  .sell-section {
    display: grid;
    gap: 12px;
  }

  .sell-section-title {
    color: var(--color-navy);
    font-family: var(--vos-font-display);
    font-size: 22px;
    line-height: 1.05;
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }

  /* Links in the selling pages' text are sky-dark on white (4.7:1), underlined. */
  .sell-section p a,
  .sell-note a {
    color: var(--color-sky-dark);
    font-weight: 600;
    text-decoration: underline;
    text-underline-offset: 3px;
  }

  .sell-cards {
    display: grid;
    gap: 12px;
    counter-reset: sell-step;
  }

  .sell-cards li {
    padding: 14px 16px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-card);
    background: var(--color-panel);
    color: var(--color-ink);
  }

  .sell-cards strong {
    color: var(--color-navy);
  }

  .sell-numbered li {
    counter-increment: sell-step;
  }

  .sell-numbered li::before {
    content: counter(sell-step) ". ";
    color: var(--color-navy);
    font-weight: 700;
  }

  .sell-note {
    color: var(--color-muted);
  }

  .sell-actions {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 12px 16px;
  }

  .sell-status {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    min-height: 32px;
    padding: 4px 14px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    background: var(--color-pale);
    color: var(--color-navy);
    font-weight: 600;
  }

  /* Danger red on panel is 6.0:1. */
  .sell-status-paused {
    background: var(--color-panel);
    color: var(--color-danger);
  }

  .sell-numbers {
    display: grid;
    gap: 12px;
  }

  @media (min-width: 48rem) and (min-height: 30rem) {
    .sell-numbers {
      grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
    }
  }

  .sell-numbers div {
    display: grid;
    align-content: start;
    gap: 6px;
    padding: 16px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-card);
    background: var(--color-panel);
  }

  .sell-numbers dt {
    color: var(--color-muted);
    font-size: 14px;
  }

  .sell-numbers dd {
    color: var(--color-navy);
    font-family: var(--vos-font-display);
    font-size: 28px;
    line-height: 1;
  }

  .sell-badge {
    color: var(--color-navy);
    font-weight: 700;
  }

  .sell-rows {
    display: grid;
    border-top: 1.5px solid var(--color-line);
  }

  .sell-row {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 12px;
    min-height: 56px;
    border-bottom: 1.5px solid var(--color-line);
    color: var(--color-navy);
    font-weight: 600;
  }

  .sell-row svg {
    flex: none;

  .sell-pause {
    display: grid;
    justify-items: start;
    gap: 12px;
    padding-top: 24px;

  }

  /* While /sell streams in: about the pitch's height, so the footer doesn't jump. Task 17 checks it. */
  .sell-skeleton-head {
    height: 13rem;
  }

  .sell-skeleton-body {
    height: 34rem;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the form parts' test**

Create `components/sell/fields.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { CheckList, RadioGroup, SelectField, TextField } from "./fields";

const choices = [
  { value: "cash", label: "Cash" },
  { value: "swap", label: "Swap" },
];

describe("the selling forms' parts (selling spec section 4)", () => {
  it("labels a text field, joins its hint and error to it, and marks it invalid", () => {
    render(<TextField id="t-title" name="title" label="Title" hint="Up to 70." defaultValue="Fan" error="Too long." />);
    const input = screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Title" });
    expect(input).toHaveValue("Fan");
    expect(input).toHaveAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
    expect(input).toHaveAccessibleDescription("Up to 70. Error: Too long.");
  });

  it("makes a long text field a textarea", () => {
    render(<TextField id="t-about" name="about" label="About" hint="Two lines." defaultValue={"a\nb"} multiline />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "About" }).tagName).toBe("TEXTAREA");
  });

  it("gives a select a first choice that asks", () => {
    render(<SelectField id="t-cat" name="category" label="Category" options={choices} placeholder="Choose" defaultValue="" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Category" })).toHaveValue("");
    expect(screen.getByRole("option", { name: "Choose" })).toHaveValue("");
  });

  it("groups radios under a legend, with the first one's id the group's", () => {
    render(<RadioGroup id="t-pay" name="payMode" legend="How do you want to be paid?" options={choices} defaultValue="swap" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("group", { name: "How do you want to be paid?" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "Cash" })).toHaveAttribute("id", "t-pay");
    expect(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "Swap" })).toBeChecked();
  });

  it("groups boxes under a legend, ticking the chosen ones", () => {
    render(
      <CheckList id="t-spots" name="pickupPoint" legend="Pickup points" options={choices} chosen={["swap"]} hint="One or more." />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Pickup points" })).toHaveAccessibleDescription("One or more.");
    expect(screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Cash" })).not.toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Swap" })).toBeChecked();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { campuses } from "@/data/fixtures/campuses";
import { categories } from "@/data/fixtures/categories";
import { pickupPoints } from "@/data/fixtures/pickup-points";
import type { OnboardingDraftRow } from "@/lib/data/rows";
import { OnboardingView } from "./OnboardingView";

// The forms only need something to post to; the actions have their own tests.
vi.mock("@/lib/selling/shop-actions", () => ({ saveOnboardingStep: async () => {}, sendForApproval: async () => {} }));

const draft: OnboardingDraftRow = {
  accountId: "acc-student",
  name: "Zola's Bakes",
  tagline: "",
  categorySlug: "food",
  about: "I bake between lectures.",
  photoSrc: null,
  campusSlug: "midrand",
  pickupPointIds: ["pp-midrand-library"],
  updatedAt: "2026-10-14T08:00:00.000Z",
};

const show = (step: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5, overrides: Partial<OnboardingDraftRow> = {}, errors = [] as never[]) =>
  render(
    <OnboardingView
      step={step}
      draft={{ ...draft, ...overrides }}
      errors={errors}
      campuses={campuses}
      categories={categories}
      pickupPoints={pickupPoints}
    />,
  );

describe("the wizard (selling spec 2.6, plan decision 15)", () => {
  it("says which step it is in words, and fills step 1 from the draft", () => {
    show(1);
    expect(screen.getByText("Step 1 of 5: Basics")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Shop name" })).toHaveValue("Zola's Bakes");
    expect(screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Main category" })).toHaveValue("food");
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Continue" })).toHaveAttribute("type", "submit");
    expect(screen.queryByRole("link", { name: "Back" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("shows the errors, linked to their fields", () => {
    show(1, { name: "Z" }, [{ message: "Your shop's name must be 2 to 60 characters.", fieldId: "onb-name" }] as never[]);
    expect(screen.getByRole("alert", { name: "There's a problem" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Shop name" })).toHaveAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
  });

  it("offers a photo, or the category's picture, and a way back", () => {
    const { unmount } = show(3);
    expect(screen.getByText("Step 3 of 5: Photo")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Shop photo (optional)")).toHaveAttribute("type", "file");
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Remove photo" })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Back" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/sell/onboarding?step=2");
    unmount();
    show(3, { photoSrc: "/photos/ph-1" });
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove photo" })).toHaveAttribute("value", "remove");
  });

  it("lists the pickup spots of the campus it shows, and says when there are none", () => {
    const { unmount } = show(4);
    const spots = screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Pickup points at Midrand" });
    expect(within(spots).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Library entrance" })).toBeChecked();
    expect(within(spots).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Main reception" })).not.toBeChecked();
    expect(document.querySelector('input[name="pointsCampus"]')).toHaveValue("midrand");
    unmount();
    show(4, { campusSlug: "online", pickupPointIds: [] });
    expect(screen.getByText(/Eduvos Online has no pickup points/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("reviews every answer with a way to change it, then asks for the guidelines box", () => {
    show(5);
    const review = screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Your answers" });
    expect(within(review).getByText("Zola's Bakes")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(review).getByRole("link", { name: "Change shop name" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/sell/onboarding");
    expect(within(review).getByRole("link", { name: "Change pickup points" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/sell/onboarding?step=4",
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "I've read the seller guidelines" })).toBeRequired();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Send for approval" })).toHaveAttribute("type", "submit");
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { getImageProps } from "next/image";

type ThumbProps = { src: string; alt: string; size?: number };

/**
 * A small square picture on the selling pages (plan decision 13): next/image's resized addresses on a plain <img>,
 * so these pages carry none of its client code (spec section 5).
 */
export function Thumb({ src, alt, size = 96 }: ThumbProps) {
  const { props } = getImageProps({ src, alt, width: size, height: size });
  // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element -- getImageProps gives next/image's addresses; no client code.
  return <img {...props} alt={alt} className="sell-thumb" />;
}
```

Create `components/sell/fields.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { describedBy, FieldError } from "@/components/forms/FieldError";
import { cx } from "@/lib/cx";

// The selling forms' fields (selling spec section 4, plan decision 24), in D's pattern: a label (or a legend), a
// hint, the error, then the control, with the hint and the error joined to it by aria-describedby.

type Choice = { value: string; label: string };

type TextFieldProps = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  label: string;
  hint?: ReactNode;
  defaultValue: string;
  error?: string;
  required?: boolean;
  multiline?: boolean;
  inputMode?: "numeric" | "text";
};

export function TextField({ id, name, label, hint, defaultValue, error, required, multiline, inputMode }: TextFieldProps) {
  const hintId = hint ? `${id}-hint` : null;
  const errorId = `${id}-error`;
  const shared = {
    id,
    name,
    defaultValue,
    required,
    "aria-invalid": error ? true : undefined,
    "aria-describedby": describedBy(hintId, error && errorId),
  };
  return (
    <div className="form-field">
      <label htmlFor={id} className="form-label">
        {label}
      </label>
      {hint && (
        <p id={hintId!} className="form-hint">
          {hint}
        </p>
      )}
      <FieldError id={errorId} message={error} />
      {multiline ? (
        <textarea {...shared} rows={5} className="form-input sell-textarea" />
      ) : (
        <input {...shared} type="text" inputMode={inputMode} autoComplete="off" className="form-input" />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

type SelectFieldProps = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  label: string;
  hint?: ReactNode;
  options: readonly Choice[];
  /** The first option, with no value: "Choose a category". */
  placeholder: string;
  defaultValue: string;
  error?: string;
  /** Announced as required unless it's optional for some answers (a service's condition, say). */
  required?: boolean;
};

export function SelectField({
  id,
  name,
  label,
  hint,
  options,
  placeholder,
  defaultValue,
  error,
  required = true,
}: SelectFieldProps) {
  const hintId = hint ? `${id}-hint` : null;
  return (
    <div className="form-field">
      <label htmlFor={id} className="form-label">
        {label}
      </label>
      {hint && (
        <p id={hintId!} className="form-hint">
          {hint}
        </p>
      )}
      <FieldError id={`${id}-error`} message={error} />
      <select
        id={id}
        name={name}
        defaultValue={defaultValue}
        required={required}
        aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
        aria-describedby={describedBy(hintId, error && `${id}-error`)}
        className="form-input form-select"
      >
        <option value="">{placeholder}</option>
        {options.map((option) => (
          <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
            {option.label}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
    </div>
  );
}

type GroupProps = {
  /** The first control's id, where an error's link lands; the others add their value. */
  id: string;
  name: string;
  legend: string;
  hint?: ReactNode;
  options: readonly Choice[];
  error?: string;
  /** Lay the choices out in a row from the tablet step. */
  row?: boolean;
};

/** A fieldset with its legend, hint and error, around radios or boxes (`name` and `options` are the caller's). */
function Group({ id, legend, hint, error, row, children }: GroupProps & { children: ReactNode }) {
  const hintId = hint ? `${id}-hint` : null;
  return (
    <fieldset className="form-field sell-fieldset" aria-describedby={describedBy(hintId, error && `${id}-error`)}>
      <legend className="form-label">{legend}</legend>
      {hint && (
        <p id={hintId!} className="form-hint">
          {hint}
        </p>
      )}
      <FieldError id={`${id}-error`} message={error} />
      <div className={cx("sell-choices", row && "sell-choices-row")}>{children}</div>
    </fieldset>
  );
}

const controlId = (id: string, index: number, value: string) => (index === 0 ? id : `${id}-${value}`);

export function RadioGroup({ defaultValue, ...props }: GroupProps & { defaultValue: string }) {
  return (
    <Group {...props}>
      {props.options.map((option, index) => (
        <div key={option.value} className="form-check">
          <input
            id={controlId(props.id, index, option.value)}
            type="radio"
            name={props.name}
            value={option.value}
            defaultChecked={option.value === defaultValue}
            required
            aria-invalid={props.error ? true : undefined}
            className="form-checkbox"
          />
          <label htmlFor={controlId(props.id, index, option.value)}>{option.label}</label>
        </div>
      ))}
    </Group>
  );
}

export function CheckList({ chosen, ...props }: GroupProps & { chosen: readonly string[] }) {
  return (
    <Group {...props}>
      {props.options.map((option, index) => (
        <div key={option.value} className="form-check">
          <input
            id={controlId(props.id, index, option.value)}
            type="checkbox"
            name={props.name}
            value={option.value}
            defaultChecked={chosen.includes(option.value)}
            aria-invalid={props.error ? true : undefined}
            className="form-checkbox"
          />
          <label htmlFor={controlId(props.id, index, option.value)}>{option.label}</label>
        </div>
      ))}
    </Group>
  );
}
```

Create `components/sell/ShopFields.tsx`:

```tsx
import { CampusSelect } from "@/components/forms/CampusSelect";
import { describedBy, FieldError } from "@/components/forms/FieldError";
import { errorFor, type FormError } from "@/lib/auth/messages";
import { categoryPicture } from "@/lib/data/catalog";
import type { shopFieldIds } from "@/lib/selling/errors";
import { PHOTO_ACCEPT, PHOTO_MAX_MB } from "@/lib/photos/rules";
import type { Campus, Category, PickupPoint } from "@/lib/types";
import { CheckList, SelectField, TextField } from "./fields";
import { Thumb } from "./Thumb";

// The shop's fields (selling spec 2.6 and 2.11), shared by the wizard and Edit shop. Hints and wording are samples
// for Lama to change (plan decision 27).

type Ids = ReturnType<typeof shopFieldIds>;

export function NameFields({
  ids,
  errors,
  name,
  tagline,
  categorySlug,
  categories,
}: {
  ids: Ids;
  errors: readonly FormError[];
  name: string;
  tagline: string;
  categorySlug: string;
  categories: readonly Pick<Category, "slug" | "name">[];
}) {
  return (
    <>
      <TextField
        id={ids.name}
        name="name"
        label="Shop name"
        hint="2 to 60 characters. It can't be the same as another shop's name on your campus."
        defaultValue={name}
        error={errorFor(errors, ids.name)}
        required
      />
      <TextField
        id={ids.tagline}
        name="tagline"
        label="Tagline (optional)"
        hint="Up to 80 characters, such as “Braids and cornrows on campus.”"
        defaultValue={tagline}
        error={errorFor(errors, ids.tagline)}
      />
      <SelectField
        id={ids.category}
        name="category"
        label="Main category"
        options={categories.map((category) => ({ value: category.slug, label: category.name }))}
        placeholder="Choose a category"
        defaultValue={categorySlug}
        error={errorFor(errors, ids.category)}
      />
    </>
  );
}

export function AboutField({ ids, errors, about }: { ids: Ids; errors: readonly FormError[]; about: string }) {
  return (
    <TextField
      id={ids.about}
      name="about"
      label="About your shop"
      hint="Two or three sentences: what you sell, and how buyers find and pay you. Up to 600 characters. Line breaks are kept."
      defaultValue={about}
      error={errorFor(errors, ids.about)}
      required
      multiline
    />
  );
}

/**
 * The shop's photo (selling spec 2.6 step 3): the one it has, or its category's picture, and a file input. The server
 * checks and strips it (no client code here). `remove` is the wizard's Remove photo button or Edit shop's box.
 */
export function ShopPhotoField({
  ids,
  errors,
  current,
  categorySlug,
  remove,
}: {
  ids: Ids;
  errors: readonly FormError[];
  /** The shop's photo's address ("/photos/…" or a sample's placeholder), or null for the category's picture. */
  current: string | null;
  categorySlug: string;
  remove: "button" | "checkbox";
}) {
  const error = errorFor(errors, ids.photos);
  const picture = current ?? categoryPicture(categorySlug || "services").src;
  return (
    <div className="form-field">
      <div className="sell-photo-now">
        <Thumb src={picture} alt="" />
        <p>{current ? "Your shop's photo." : "No photo yet: buyers see your category's picture."}</p>
      </div>
      <label htmlFor={ids.photos} className="form-label">
        {current ? "A different photo (optional)" : "Shop photo (optional)"}
      </label>
      <p id={`${ids.photos}-hint`} className="form-hint">
        A JPG, PNG or WebP, up to {PHOTO_MAX_MB} MB. Hidden data such as your location is removed. If someone else is in
        it, ask them first.
      </p>
      <FieldError id={`${ids.photos}-error`} message={error} />
      <input
        id={ids.photos}
        name="photo"
        type="file"
        accept={PHOTO_ACCEPT}
        aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
        aria-describedby={describedBy(`${ids.photos}-hint`, error && `${ids.photos}-error`)}
        className="form-input sell-file"
      />
      {current && remove === "button" && (
        <button type="submit" name="photoAction" value="remove" className="link-button">
          Remove photo
        </button>
      )}
      {current && remove === "checkbox" && (
        <div className="form-check">
          <input id={`${ids.photos}-remove`} name="removePhoto" type="checkbox" value="yes" className="form-checkbox" />
          <label htmlFor={`${ids.photos}-remove`}>Remove the photo and use my category&apos;s picture</label>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * The campus and its pickup points (selling spec 2.6 step 4, plan decision 15). The points shown are `campusSlug`'s,
 * named in a hidden field, so choosing another campus comes back with that campus's points, with no client code.
 */
export function CampusSpotsFields({
  ids,
  errors,
  campusSlug,
  chosen,
  campuses,
  pickupPoints,
}: {
  ids: Ids;
  errors: readonly FormError[];
  campusSlug: string;
  chosen: readonly string[];
  campuses: readonly Pick<Campus, "slug" | "name">[];
  pickupPoints: readonly PickupPoint[];
}) {
  const campusError = errorFor(errors, ids.campus);
  const campusName = campuses.find((campus) => campus.slug === campusSlug)?.name ?? "";
  const spots = pickupPoints.filter((point) => point.campusSlug === campusSlug);
  return (
    <>
      <div className="form-field">
        <label htmlFor={ids.campus} className="form-label">
          Your campus
        </label>
        <p id={`${ids.campus}-hint`} className="form-hint">
          Choosing another? Press the button below, and its pickup points appear here.
        </p>
        <FieldError id={`${ids.campus}-error`} message={campusError} />
        <CampusSelect
          id={ids.campus}
          defaultValue={campusSlug}
          campuses={campuses}
          invalid={Boolean(campusError)}
          describedBy={describedBy(`${ids.campus}-hint`, campusError && `${ids.campus}-error`)}
        />
      </div>
      <input type="hidden" name="pointsCampus" value={campusSlug} />
      {spots.length > 0 ? (
        <CheckList
          id={ids.points}
          name="pickupPoint"
          legend={`Pickup points at ${campusName}`}
          hint="Where buyers can meet you. Choose at least one."
          options={spots.map((point) => ({ value: point.id, label: point.name }))}
          chosen={chosen}
          error={errorFor(errors, ids.points)}
        />
      ) : (
        campusSlug && (
          <p className="form-hint">
            {campusName || "This campus"} has no pickup points: you&apos;ll meet buyers online.
          </p>
        )
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the wizard**

Create `components/sell/OnboardingView.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { ErrorSummary } from "@/components/forms/ErrorSummary";
import { FieldError } from "@/components/forms/FieldError";
import { errorFor, type FormError } from "@/lib/auth/messages";
import type { OnboardingDraftRow } from "@/lib/data/rows";
import { shopFieldIds } from "@/lib/selling/errors";
import { ONBOARDING_STEPS, onboardingPath, type OnboardingStep } from "@/lib/selling/paths";
import { saveOnboardingStep, sendForApproval } from "@/lib/selling/shop-actions";
import type { Campus, Category, PickupPoint } from "@/lib/types";
import { AboutField, CampusSpotsFields, NameFields, ShopPhotoField } from "./ShopFields";

type OnboardingViewProps = {
  step: OnboardingStep;
  draft: OnboardingDraftRow;
  errors: readonly FormError[];
  campuses: readonly Pick<Campus, "slug" | "name">[];
  categories: readonly Pick<Category, "slug" | "name">[];
  pickupPoints: readonly PickupPoint[];
};

const IDS = shopFieldIds("onb");

/** A step's Back link: it replaces the history entry, so the wizard is one entry in the history (plan decision 15). */
function BackLink({ step }: { step: OnboardingStep }) {
  if (step === 1) return null;
  return (
    <Link href={onboardingPath((step - 1) as OnboardingStep)} replace prefetch={false} className="link-button">
      Back
    </Link>
  );
}

/**
 * One step of the wizard (selling spec 2.6): its progress in words, its errors, and a plain form posting to
 * saveOnboardingStep, or the review and Send for approval. Continue comes first in each form, so Enter continues.
 */
export function OnboardingView({ step, draft, errors, campuses, categories, pickupPoints }: OnboardingViewProps) {
  return (
    <div className="sell-section">
      <p className="sell-progress">
        Step {step} of 5: {ONBOARDING_STEPS[step - 1].name}
      </p>
      <ErrorSummary errors={errors} />
      {step === 5 ? (
        <Review draft={draft} errors={errors} campuses={campuses} categories={categories} pickupPoints={pickupPoints} />
      ) : (
        <form action={saveOnboardingStep} noValidate className="form-stack">
          <input type="hidden" name="step" value={step} />
          {step === 1 && (
            <NameFields
              ids={IDS}
              errors={errors}
              name={draft.name}
              tagline={draft.tagline}
              categorySlug={draft.categorySlug}
              categories={categories}
            />
          )}
          {step === 2 && <AboutField ids={IDS} errors={errors} about={draft.about} />}
          {step === 3 && (
            <ShopPhotoField
              ids={IDS}
              errors={errors}
              current={draft.photoSrc}
              categorySlug={draft.categorySlug}
              remove="button"
            />
          )}
          {step === 4 && (
            <CampusSpotsFields
              ids={IDS}
              errors={errors}
              campusSlug={draft.campusSlug}
              chosen={draft.pickupPointIds}
              campuses={campuses}
              pickupPoints={pickupPoints}
            />
          )}
          <div className="sell-form-buttons">
            <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary form-submit">
              Continue
            </button>
            <BackLink step={step} />
          </div>
        </form>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/** Step 5 (selling spec 2.6): every answer with a way to change it, the guidelines box, and Send for approval. */
function Review({
  draft,
  errors,
  campuses,
  categories,
  pickupPoints,
}: Omit<OnboardingViewProps, "step">) {
  const campus = campuses.find((item) => item.slug === draft.campusSlug);
  const spots = pickupPoints.filter((point) => point.campusSlug === draft.campusSlug);
  const chosen = spots.filter((point) => draft.pickupPointIds.includes(point.id)).map((point) => point.name);
  let spotsLine = chosen.join(", ") || "Not answered";
  if (spots.length === 0) spotsLine = "None: you'll meet buyers online";
  const rows: ReadonlyArray<[label: string, value: string, step: OnboardingStep]> = [
    ["Shop name", draft.name || "Not answered", 1],
    ["Tagline", draft.tagline || "None", 1],
    ["Main category", categories.find((item) => item.slug === draft.categorySlug)?.name ?? "Not answered", 1],
    ["About", draft.about || "Not answered", 2],
    ["Photo", draft.photoSrc ? "Your photo" : "Your category's picture", 3],
    ["Campus", campus?.name ?? "Not answered", 4],
    ["Pickup points", spotsLine, 4],
  ];
  const guidelinesError = errorFor(errors, IDS.guidelines);
  return (
    <>
      <section aria-labelledby="onb-review-title" className="sell-section">
        <h2 id="onb-review-title" className="sell-section-title">
          Your answers
        </h2>
        <dl className="sell-review">
          {rows.map(([label, value, step]) => (
            <div key={label}>
              <dt>{label}</dt>
              <dd>{value}</dd>
              <dd>
                <Link href={onboardingPath(step)} replace prefetch={false}>
                  Change <span className="sr-only">{label.toLowerCase()}</span>
                </Link>
              </dd>
            </div>
          ))}
        </dl>
      </section>
      <form action={sendForApproval} noValidate className="form-stack">
        <div className="form-field">
          <FieldError id={`${IDS.guidelines}-error`} message={guidelinesError} />
          <div className="form-check">
            <input
              id={IDS.guidelines}
              name="guidelines"
              type="checkbox"
              value="yes"
              required
              aria-invalid={guidelinesError ? true : undefined}
              aria-describedby={guidelinesError ? `${IDS.guidelines}-error` : undefined}
              className="form-checkbox"
            />
            <label htmlFor={IDS.guidelines}>
              I&apos;ve read the{" "}
              <Link href="/seller-guidelines" prefetch={false}>
                seller guidelines
              </Link>
            </label>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="sell-form-buttons">
          <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary form-submit">
            Send for approval
          </button>
          <BackLink step={5} />
        </div>
      </form>
    </>
  );
}
```

Create `components/sell/Onboarding.tsx`:

```tsx
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { readFormState } from "@/lib/auth/form-state";
import { requireAccount } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { getCampuses, getCategories, getPickupPoints } from "@/lib/data";
import { emptyDraft, getOnboardingDraft, getShopOf } from "@/lib/data/shops";
import { sellingErrors, shopFieldIds } from "@/lib/selling/errors";
import { onboardingForm } from "@/lib/selling/form-state";
import { onboardingPath, parseStep, SELL_PATH } from "@/lib/selling/paths";
import { OnboardingView } from "./OnboardingView";

type OnboardingProps = { searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>> };

/**
 * The wizard's step for this account (selling spec 2.6 and section 3): request-time, as it reads the link, the
 * session and the draft. A visitor with a shop already goes to /sell.
 */
export async function Onboarding({ searchParams }: OnboardingProps) {
  const step = parseStep((await searchParams).step);
  const account = await requireAccount(onboardingPath(step));
  if (await getShopOf(account.id)) redirect(SELL_PATH);
  // A first visit makes an empty draft dated now (decision 26).
  await connection();
  const [draft, state, campuses, categories, pickupPoints] = await Promise.all([
    getOnboardingDraft(account.id),
    readFormState(onboardingForm(step)),
    getCampuses(),
    getCategories(),
    getPickupPoints(),
  ]);
  const shown = draft ?? emptyDraft(account.id, account.campusSlug, new Date());
  return (
    <OnboardingView
      step={step}
      draft={shown}
      errors={sellingErrors(state?.errors ?? [], shopFieldIds("onb"))}
      campuses={campuses}
      categories={categories}
      pickupPoints={pickupPoints}
    />
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { ListingsTab } from "@/lib/types";
import { makeListingRow } from "@/tests/helpers/fakes";
import { MyListingsView } from "./MyListingsView";

// The buttons only need something to post to; the actions have their own tests.
vi.mock("@/lib/selling/listing-actions", () => ({ changeListingState: async () => {} }));

const rows = [
  makeListingRow({ id: "l-a", title: "Desk fan", state: "available", priceZar: 150, updatedAt: "2026-10-13T08:00:00.000Z" }),
  makeListingRow({ id: "l-p", title: "Kettle", state: "paused" }),
  makeListingRow({ id: "l-s", title: "Lamp", state: "sold" }),
  makeListingRow({ id: "l-d", title: "Bike", state: "draft" }),
];

const show = (tab: ListingsTab, extra: Partial<Parameters<typeof MyListingsView>[0]> = {}) =>
  render(<MyListingsView tab={tab} rows={rows} waiting={false} notice={null} noticeListing={null} {...extra} />);

const itemFor = (title: string) => screen.getByText(title).closest("li")!;

describe("My listings (selling spec 2.9)", () => {
  it("links each tab with its count, marking the open one", () => {
    show("available");
    const tabs = within(screen.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Listings by state" })).getAllByRole("link");
    expect(tabs.map((tab) => [tab.textContent, tab.getAttribute("href"), tab.getAttribute("aria-current")])).toEqual([
      ["Available (1)", "/sell/listings", "page"],
      ["Paused (1)", "/sell/listings?tab=paused", null],
      ["Sold (1)", "/sell/listings?tab=sold", null],
      ["Drafts (1)", "/sell/listings?tab=drafts", null],
    ]);
  });

  it("shows an available listing's price and last update, and actions that each name it", () => {
    show("available");
    const item = itemFor("Desk fan");
    expect(within(item).getByText("R150 · Updated 13 Oct")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(item).getByRole("link", { name: "Edit Desk fan" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/sell/listings/l-a/edit");
    expect(within(item).getByRole("button", { name: "Pause Desk fan" })).toHaveAttribute("type", "submit");
    expect(within(item).getByRole("button", { name: "Mark sold Desk fan" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(item).getByRole("link", { name: "Delete Desk fan" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/sell/listings/l-a/delete");
    expect(item.querySelector('input[name="tab"]')).toHaveValue("available");
    expect(screen.queryByText("Kettle")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("offers Resume on a paused listing, Relist on a sold one, and only Edit and Delete on a draft", () => {
    const paused = show("paused");
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Resume Kettle" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Mark sold Kettle" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    paused.unmount();
    const sold = show("sold");
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Relist Lamp" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /^Mark sold/ })).toBeNull();
    sold.unmount();
    show("drafts");
    expect(screen.queryAllByRole("button")).toEqual([]);
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Edit Bike" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("says when a tab is empty", () => {
    render(<MyListingsView tab="sold" rows={[]} waiting={false} notice={null} noticeListing={null} />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Nothing sold yet.")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows the last action's notice with a link to the listing, and tells a waiting shop its listings are drafts", () => {
    show("available", { notice: "Published. Buyers can find it now.", noticeListing: "l-a", waiting: true });
    const notice = screen.getByRole("status");
    expect(notice).toHaveTextContent("Published. Buyers can find it now.");
    expect(within(notice).getByRole("link", { name: "See it as buyers do" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/listings/l-a");
    expect(screen.getByText(/Your shop is waiting for the Hub/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write My listings**

Create `components/sell/MyListingsView.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import type { ListingRow } from "@/lib/data/rows";
import { priceLabel } from "@/lib/format";
import { changeListingState } from "@/lib/selling/listing-actions";
import { deleteListingPath, editListingPath, LISTINGS_TABS, myListingsPath } from "@/lib/selling/paths";
import { shortSastDate } from "@/lib/time";
import type { ListingsTab } from "@/lib/types";
import { Thumb } from "./Thumb";

type MyListingsViewProps = {
  tab: ListingsTab;
  /** All the shop's listings; the view picks the tab's. */
  rows: readonly ListingRow[];
  /** The shop is waiting for approval, so everything is a draft (plan decision 4). */
  waiting: boolean;
  notice: string | null;
  /** The listing the notice is about, for its link. */
  noticeListing: string | null;
};

const EMPTY: Readonly<Record<ListingsTab, string>> = {
  available: "Nothing available right now.",
  paused: "Nothing paused.",
  sold: "Nothing sold yet.",
  drafts: "No drafts.",
};

/**
 * My listings (selling spec 2.9): the last action's notice, the four tabs as links with their counts, and the open
 * tab's listings, each with its photo, title, price, last update and actions. Each button and link names its listing.
 */
export function MyListingsView({ tab, rows, waiting, notice, noticeListing }: MyListingsViewProps) {
  const open = LISTINGS_TABS.find((item) => item.tab === tab)!;
  const shown = rows.filter((row) => row.state === open.state);
  const linkable = rows.some((row) => row.id === noticeListing && row.state !== "draft");
  return (
    <>
      {notice && (
        <p role="status" className="form-notice">
          {notice}
          {linkable && (
            <>
              {" "}
              <Link href={`/listings/${noticeListing}`} prefetch={false}>
                See it as buyers do
              </Link>
            </>
          )}
        </p>
      )}
      {waiting && (
        <p className="form-notice">
          Your shop is waiting for the Hub, so your listings stay drafts until it&apos;s approved.
        </p>
      )}

      <nav aria-label="Listings by state" className="sell-tabs">
        <ul>
          {LISTINGS_TABS.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.tab}>
              <Link
                href={myListingsPath(item.tab)}
                prefetch={false}
                aria-current={item.tab === tab ? "page" : undefined}
                className="sell-tab"
              >
                {item.label} <span className="sell-tab-count">({rows.filter((row) => row.state === item.state).length})</span>
              </Link>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </nav>

      <section aria-labelledby="sell-list-title" className="sell-section">
        <h2 id="sell-list-title" className="sr-only">
          {open.label}
        </h2>
        {shown.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="sell-empty">{EMPTY[tab]}</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="sell-list">
            {shown.map((row) => (
              <ListingItem key={row.id} row={row} tab={tab} />
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

/** A listing's photo, title, price and last update. Delete listing's page shows it too. */
export function ListingSummary({ row }: { row: ListingRow }) {
  const cover = row.photos[0]?.src ?? `/placeholders/listing/${row.categorySlug}.svg`;
  return (
    <>
      <Thumb src={cover} alt="" size={64} />
      <div>
        <p className="sell-item-title">{row.title}</p>
        <p className="sell-item-meta">
          {priceLabel(row)} · Updated {shortSastDate(row.updatedAt)}
        </p>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

function ListingItem({ row, tab }: { row: ListingRow; tab: ListingsTab }) {
  return (
    <li className="sell-item">
      <ListingSummary row={row} />
      <div className="sell-item-actions">
        <Link href={editListingPath(row.id)} prefetch={false} className="sell-action">
          Edit <span className="sr-only">{row.title}</span>
        </Link>
        {row.state === "available" && <StateButton row={row} tab={tab} to="paused" label="Pause" />}
        {row.state === "paused" && <StateButton row={row} tab={tab} to="available" label="Resume" />}
        {row.state === "sold" && <StateButton row={row} tab={tab} to="available" label="Relist" />}
        {(row.state === "available" || row.state === "paused") && (
          <StateButton row={row} tab={tab} to="sold" label="Mark sold" />
        )}
        <Link href={deleteListingPath(row.id)} prefetch={false} className="sell-action sell-action-danger">
          Delete <span className="sr-only">{row.title}</span>
        </Link>
      </div>
    </li>
  );
}

/** One of the row's buttons (plan decision 3): a small form posting the listing, the move and the open tab. */
function StateButton({ row, tab, to, label }: { row: ListingRow; tab: ListingsTab; to: string; label: string }) {
  return (
    <form action={changeListingState}>
      <input type="hidden" name="listing" value={row.id} />
      <input type="hidden" name="to" value={to} />
      <input type="hidden" name="tab" value={tab} />
      <button type="submit" className="sell-action">
        {label} <span className="sr-only">{row.title}</span>
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

Create `components/sell/MyListings.tsx`:

```tsx
import { readFormState } from "@/lib/auth/form-state";
import { listOwnListings } from "@/lib/data/own-listings";
import { MY_LISTINGS_FORM } from "@/lib/selling/form-state";
import { noticeText } from "@/lib/selling/notices";
import { requireShopOwner } from "@/lib/selling/owner";
import { myListingsPath, parseListingsTab } from "@/lib/selling/paths";
import { MyListingsView } from "./MyListingsView";

type MyListingsProps = { searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>> };

/** My listings for this seller (selling spec 2.9): request-time, as it reads the link and the session. */
export async function MyListings({ searchParams }: MyListingsProps) {
  const tab = parseListingsTab((await searchParams).tab);
  const owner = await requireShopOwner(myListingsPath(tab));
  const [rows, state] = await Promise.all([listOwnListings(owner.shop.slug), readFormState(MY_LISTINGS_FORM)]);
  return (
    <MyListingsView
      tab={tab}
      rows={rows}
      waiting={owner.status === "pending"}
      notice={noticeText(state?.notice)}
      noticeListing={state?.values.listing ?? null}
    />
  );
}
```

Create `components/sell/DeleteListing.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { getOwnListing } from "@/lib/data/own-listings";
import { isId } from "@/lib/messaging/paths";
import { deleteListingForm } from "@/lib/selling/listing-actions";
import { requireShopOwner } from "@/lib/selling/owner";
import { deleteListingPath, myListingsPath, tabFor } from "@/lib/selling/paths";
import { ListingSummary } from "./MyListingsView";

/**
 * Delete listing's confirmation (selling spec 2.9): which listing, what deleting does, Delete listing and Keep it.
 * Request-time; another shop's listing, or none, is the 404.
 */
export async function DeleteListing({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const owner = await requireShopOwner(deleteListingPath(id));
  const row = isId(id) ? await getOwnListing(owner.shop.slug, id) : null;
  if (!row) notFound();
  return (
    <>
      <div className="sell-item sell-item-alone">
        <ListingSummary row={row} />
      </div>
      <p>
        Buyers won&apos;t see it any more, and its photos are deleted. Conversations about it stay, with a note that it
        was removed. This can&apos;t be undone.
      </p>
      <form action={deleteListingForm} className="sell-form-buttons">
        <input type="hidden" name="listing" value={row.id} />
        <button type="submit" className="btn btn-danger">
          Delete listing
        </button>
        <Link href={myListingsPath(tabFor(row.state))} prefetch={false} className="link-button">
          Keep it
        </Link>
      </form>
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { ChevronLeftIcon } from "@/components/icons";
import { MyListings } from "@/components/sell/MyListings";
import { SellFormSkeleton } from "@/components/sell/SellSkeleton";
import { NEW_LISTING_PATH, SELL_PATH } from "@/lib/selling/paths";

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "My listings", robots: { index: false } };

/** My listings (selling spec 2.9): the heading is prerendered; the tab and its listings stream in. */
export default function MyListingsPage({ searchParams }: PageProps<"/sell/listings">) {
  return (
    <div className="page sell-page">
      <div className="sell-column">
        <header className="sell-head">
          <Link href={SELL_PATH} prefetch={false} className="sell-crumb">
            <ChevronLeftIcon className="size-4" />
            Your shop
          </Link>
          <h1 className="sell-title">My listings</h1>
          <Link href={NEW_LISTING_PATH} prefetch={false} className="btn btn-primary">
            New listing
          </Link>
        </header>
        <Suspense fallback={<SellFormSkeleton />}>
          <MyListings searchParams={searchParams} />
        </Suspense>
      </div>
    </div>

}
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Suspense } from "react";

}
```

```tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { PhotoField } from "./PhotoField";

const A = "/photos/ph-aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa";
const B = "/placeholders/photo/2.svg";
const C = "/placeholders/photo/3.svg";
const saved = [
  { src: A, alt: "", thumb: "/_next/image?url=a" },
  { src: B, alt: "The back", thumb: B },
  { src: C, alt: "The cover", thumb: C },
];

// jsdom has no DataTransfer and no object URLs; these stand in for the browser's.
class FakeDataTransfer {
  readonly list: File[] = [];
  readonly items = { add: (file: File) => this.list.push(file) };
  get files() {
    return this.list as unknown as FileList;
  }
}

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.stubGlobal("DataTransfer", FakeDataTransfer);
  Object.defineProperty(URL, "createObjectURL", { configurable: true, writable: true, value: (file: File) => `blob:${file.name}` });
  Object.defineProperty(URL, "revokeObjectURL", { configurable: true, writable: true, value: () => {} });
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

const tokens = () => [...document.querySelectorAll<HTMLInputElement>('input[name="photo"]')].map((input) => input.value);

function show(photos = saved) {
  const submitted = vi.fn();
  render(
    <form
      onSubmit={(event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        submitted();
      }}
    >
      <PhotoField prefix="edit-l-001" photos={photos} error={undefined} />
    </form>,
  );
  return submitted;
}

describe("the photo field (selling spec 2.3, 2.5 and section 4, plan decisions 9 and 10)", () => {
  it("lists the photos in order, the first as the cover, each with its description and buttons that post without JavaScript", () => {
    show();
    expect(tokens()).toEqual([A, B, C]);
    expect(screen.getByText("Photo 1 (the cover)")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Describe photo 2" })).toHaveValue("The back");
    expect(screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Describe photo 2" })).toHaveAttribute("name", `alt:${B}`);
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Make cover (photo 1)" })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Move later (photo 3)" })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Make cover (photo 3)" })).toHaveAttribute("value", `${C}:cover`);
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Move earlier (photo 2)" })).toHaveAttribute("type", "submit");
  });

  it("moves a photo in place, says so, and keeps focus on it, without posting the form", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const submitted = show();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Make cover (photo 3)" }));
    expect(tokens()).toEqual([C, A, B]);
    expect(screen.getByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("That photo is the cover now.");
    expect(document.activeElement?.closest("li")).toBe(screen.getByText("Photo 1 (the cover)").closest("li"));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Move later (photo 2)" }));
    expect(tokens()).toEqual([C, B, A]);
    expect(screen.getByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("Moved to photo 3 of 3.");
    expect(submitted).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("removes a photo", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    show();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove (photo 2)" }));
    expect(tokens()).toEqual([A, C]);
    expect(screen.getByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("Photo removed. 2 left.");
  });

  it("adds picked photos as new ones, numbered by their place in the file input, and says what it left out", async () => {
    show(saved.slice(0, 1));
    const input = screen.getByLabelText<HTMLInputElement>("Add photos");
    const picked = [
      new File(["a"], "desk.png", { type: "image/png" }),
      new File(["b"], "cat.gif", { type: "image/gif" }),
      new File(["c"], "side.jpg", { type: "image/jpeg" }),
    ];
    Object.defineProperty(input, "files", { configurable: true, writable: true, value: picked });
    fireEvent.change(input);
    expect(await screen.findByText("Photo 3")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(tokens()).toEqual([A, "new:0", "new:1"]);
    expect(screen.getByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("2 photos added. 1 wasn't a JPG, PNG or WebP.");
    // jsdom can't decode them, so they go as they are, for the server to check and strip (plan decision 10).
    expect(input.files).toEqual([picked[0], picked[2]]);
  });
});
```

```tsx
const values = { kind: "item", payMode: "cash", handover: "pickup", pickup: "pp-midrand-library" };

const show = (overrides: Partial<Parameters<typeof ListingFormView>[0]> = {}) =>
  render(
    <ListingFormView
      listingId={null}
      state={null}
      waiting={false}
      values={values}
      photos={[]}
      errors={[]}
      categories={categories}
      spots={spots}

      {...overrides}

describe("the listing form (selling spec 2.10, plan decision 26)", () => {

    show();

      expect(screen.getByLabelText(label), label).toBeInTheDocument();
    }

      "At Midrand, your shop's campus.",

    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save as draft" })).toHaveAttribute("value", "draft");

      ],

    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Price in rand")).toHaveValue("lots");
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Price in rand")).toHaveAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
  });

  it("offers a shop without pickup spots no pickup", () => {
    show({ spots: [], campusName: "Eduvos Online", values: { kind: "service", payMode: "cash", handover: "online" } });
    expect(screen.queryByRole("radio", { name: "Pickup at one of your points" })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText("Pickup point")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the photo field**

Create `lib/selling/thumbs.ts`:

```ts
import { getImageProps } from "next/image";

/**
 * A small picture's address for the photo field (plan decision 13): next/image's resized copy of a stored photo, or a
 * placeholder as it is, worked out on the server so the client component carries none of next/image's code. `size`
 * is the CSS width; the address covers 2x screens.
 */
export function thumbSrc(src: string, size = 96): string {
  return getImageProps({ src, alt: "", width: size, height: size }).props.src;
}
```

Create `components/sell/PhotoField.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";

import { useRef, useState, type ChangeEvent, type MouseEvent } from "react";
import { flushSync } from "react-dom";
import { describedBy, FieldError } from "@/components/forms/FieldError";
import { cx } from "@/lib/cx";
import {
  PHOTO_ACCEPT,
  PHOTO_MAX_MB,
  PHOTO_TYPES,
  PHOTOS_PER_LISTING,
  SHRINK_MAX_SIDE,
  SHRINK_QUALITY,
} from "@/lib/photos/rules";
import { applyPhotoMove, newPhotoToken, type PhotoOp } from "@/lib/selling/photo-order";
import type { PhotoType } from "@/lib/types";

/** A photo the server already has: its address, its description ("" means the title) and its small picture's address. */
export type SavedPhoto = { src: string; alt: string; thumb: string };

type Item = { key: string; src: string | null; file: File | null; alt: string; thumb: string };

type PhotoFieldProps = {
  /** The form's id prefix (plan decision 23): "new" or "edit-<id>". */
  prefix: string;
  photos: readonly SavedPhoto[];
  /** The server's message about the photos, if any. */
  error: string | undefined;
};

const BUTTONS: ReadonlyArray<{ op: PhotoOp; label: string }> = [
  { op: "cover", label: "Make cover" },
  { op: "earlier", label: "Move earlier" },
  { op: "later", label: "Move later" },
  { op: "remove", label: "Remove" },
];

const encode = (canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, type: string) =>
  new Promise<Blob | null>((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve, type, SHRINK_QUALITY));

/**
 * The browser's part (selling spec 2.3, plan decision 10): the longest side at most 1,600px, re-encoded as WebP at
 * 0.8, or JPEG where the browser can't encode WebP (Safari), which leaves every bit of hidden data behind. A photo the
 * browser can't decode goes as it is, for the server to check and strip.
 */
async function shrink(file: File): Promise<File> {
  try {
    const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(file);
    const scale = Math.min(1, SHRINK_MAX_SIDE / Math.max(bitmap.width, bitmap.height));
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(bitmap.width * scale));
    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(bitmap.height * scale));
    canvas.getContext("2d")?.drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    bitmap.close();
    let blob = await encode(canvas, "image/webp");
    if (blob?.type !== "image/webp") blob = await encode(canvas, "image/jpeg");
    if (!blob) return file;
    const name = `${file.name.replace(/\.[^.]*$/, "") || "photo"}.${blob.type === "image/webp" ? "webp" : "jpg"}`;
    return new File([blob], name, { type: blob.type });
  } catch {
    return file;
  }
}

function pickedMessage(added: number, refused: number, leftOut: number): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (added > 0) parts.push(added === 1 ? "1 photo added." : `${added} photos added.`);
  if (refused > 0) parts.push(refused === 1 ? "1 wasn't a JPG, PNG or WebP." : `${refused} weren't JPG, PNG or WebP.`);
  if (leftOut > 0) {
    parts.push(`Only ${PHOTOS_PER_LISTING} photos fit, so ${leftOut === 1 ? "1 was" : `${leftOut} were`} left out.`);
  }
  return parts.join(" ");
}

/**
 * The listing form's photos (selling spec 2.3, 2.5 and section 4; plan decisions 9 and 10). Every photo has a hidden
 * `photo` field (a saved photo's address, or "new:<n>" for the n-th file in the file input), a description, and
 * Make cover, Move earlier, Move later and Remove as submit buttons. Rendered on the server, that's a form that works
 * without this component's code: each button posts it. Once the code runs, the buttons work in place, picked photos
 * are shrunk and put back into the file input, and a status line says what happened.
 */
export function PhotoField({ prefix, photos, error }: PhotoFieldProps) {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<Item[]>(() =>
    photos.map((photo) => ({ key: photo.src, src: photo.src, file: null, alt: photo.alt, thumb: photo.thumb })),
  );
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const listRef = useRef<HTMLOListElement>(null);
  const made = useRef(0);
  const inputId = `${prefix}-photos`;

  const tokenOf = (item: Item) => item.src ?? newPhotoToken(files.indexOf(item.file!));

  /** Puts these files in the file input, so the plain form posts them. */
  function putInInput(next: readonly File[]) {
    const transfer = new DataTransfer();
    for (const file of next) transfer.items.add(file);
    if (inputRef.current) inputRef.current.files = transfer.files;
  }

  async function pick(event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    // The browser has swapped the input's files for this pick; the ones already chosen are put back below.
    const picked = [...(event.currentTarget.files ?? [])];
    if (busy) {
      putInInput(files);
      setStatus("Wait for your photos to be ready, then add more.");
      return;
    }
    const fitting = picked.filter((file) => PHOTO_TYPES.includes(file.type as PhotoType));
    const taken = fitting.slice(0, Math.max(0, PHOTOS_PER_LISTING - items.length));
    setBusy(true);
    setStatus("Getting your photos ready…");
    const shrunk = await Promise.all(taken.map(shrink));
    const nextFiles = [...files, ...shrunk];
    putInInput(nextFiles);
    setFiles(nextFiles);
    const added = shrunk.map((file) => {
      made.current += 1;
      return { key: `new-${made.current}`, src: null, file, alt: "", thumb: URL.createObjectURL(file) };
    });
    setItems([...items, ...added]);
    setBusy(false);
    setStatus(pickedMessage(taken.length, picked.length - fitting.length, fitting.length - taken.length));
  }

  function press(event: MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>, item: Item, op: PhotoOp) {
    // With this code running, the buttons work here rather than posting the form (plan decision 9).
    event.preventDefault();
    const from = items.indexOf(item);
    const keys = applyPhotoMove(
      items.map((each) => each.key),
      { token: item.key, op },
    );
    const next = keys.map((key) => items.find((each) => each.key === key)!);
    if (op === "remove" && item.file) {
      const nextFiles = files.filter((file) => file !== item.file);
      putInInput(nextFiles);
      setFiles(nextFiles);
      URL.revokeObjectURL(item.thumb);
    }
    flushSync(() => setItems(next));
    if (op === "remove") {
      setStatus(`Photo removed. ${next.length} left.`);
      const rows = listRef.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("li") ?? [];
      const target = rows[Math.min(from, rows.length - 1)]?.querySelector("button") ?? inputRef.current;
      target?.focus();
      return;
    }
    const at = keys.indexOf(item.key);
    setStatus(at === 0 ? "That photo is the cover now." : `Moved to photo ${at + 1} of ${next.length}.`);
    // Focus stays with the photo: on the same button if it still has it, otherwise on its first.
    const row = listRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-key="${item.key}"]`);
    (row?.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(`[data-op="${op}"]`) ?? row?.querySelector("button"))?.focus();
  }

  return (
    <fieldset className="form-field sell-fieldset">
      <legend className="form-label">Photos</legend>
      <p id={`${prefix}-photos-hint`} className="form-hint">
        Up to {PHOTOS_PER_LISTING} JPG, PNG or WebP photos, {PHOTO_MAX_MB} MB each. The first is the cover. We make
        them smaller and remove hidden data such as your location. If someone else is in a photo, ask them first.
      </p>
      <FieldError id={`${prefix}-photos-error`} message={error} />
      {items.length > 0 && (
        <>
          <p id={`${prefix}-alt-hint`} className="form-hint">
            Describe each photo for people who can&apos;t see it. Leave it empty to use the title.
          </p>
          <ol ref={listRef} className="sell-photos">
            {items.map((item, index) => {
              const token = tokenOf(item);
              const altId = `${prefix}-alt-${index + 1}`;
              const shown = BUTTONS.filter(
                ({ op }) => (index > 0 || (op !== "cover" && op !== "earlier")) && (index < items.length - 1 || op !== "later"),
              );
              return (
                <li key={item.key} data-key={item.key} className="sell-photo">
                  <input type="hidden" name="photo" value={token} />
                  {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element -- a blob or /_next/image address (plan decision 13). */}
                  <img src={item.thumb} alt="" width={96} height={96} className="sell-thumb" />
                  <div className="sell-photo-body">
                    <p className="sell-photo-title">
                      Photo {index + 1}
                      {index === 0 && " (the cover)"}
                    </p>
                    <label htmlFor={altId} className="form-label">
                      Describe photo {index + 1}
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id={altId}
                      name={`alt:${token}`}
                      type="text"
                      defaultValue={item.alt}
                      autoComplete="off"
                      aria-describedby={`${prefix}-alt-hint`}
                      className="form-input"
                    />
                    <div className="sell-photo-buttons">
                      {shown.map(({ op, label }) => (
                        <button
                          key={op}
                          type="submit"
                          name="photoMove"
                          value={`${token}:${op}`}
                          data-op={op}
                          onClick={(event) => press(event, item, op)}
                          className={cx("sell-action", op === "remove" && "sell-action-danger")}
                        >
                          {label} <span className="sr-only">(photo {index + 1})</span>
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ol>
        </>
      )}
      <label htmlFor={inputId} className="form-label">
        Add photos
      </label>
      <input
        ref={inputRef}
        id={inputId}
        name="newPhotos"
        type="file"
        multiple
        accept={PHOTO_ACCEPT}
        onChange={pick}
        aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
        aria-describedby={describedBy(`${prefix}-photos-hint`, error && `${prefix}-photos-error`)}
        className="form-input sell-file"
      />
      <p role="status" className="sell-photo-status">
        {status}
      </p>
    </fieldset>
  );
}
```
