- [ ] **Step 5: Write the form**

Create `components/sell/ListingFormView.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { ErrorSummary } from "@/components/forms/ErrorSummary";
import { describedBy, FieldError } from "@/components/forms/FieldError";
import { errorFor, type FormError } from "@/lib/auth/messages";
import { listingFieldIds, listingFieldPrefix } from "@/lib/selling/errors";
import { saveListingForm } from "@/lib/selling/listing-actions";
import { myListingsPath, tabFor } from "@/lib/selling/paths";
import { CONDITION_CHOICES, HANDOVER_CHOICES, KIND_CHOICES, PAY_CHOICES } from "@/lib/selling/rules";
import type { Category, ListingState, PickupPoint } from "@/lib/types";
import { RadioGroup, SelectField, TextField } from "./fields";
import { PhotoField, type SavedPhoto } from "./PhotoField";

type ListingFormViewProps = {
  /** Null for a new listing. */
  listingId: string | null;
  /** The listing's state now, for the main button's words (plan decision 3). */
  state: ListingState | null;
  /** The shop is waiting for approval: drafts only (plan decision 4). */
  waiting: boolean;
  /** The fields' values: the listing's, a new one's defaults, or what was typed before a failed save. */
  values: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  photos: readonly SavedPhoto[];
  errors: readonly FormError[];
  categories: readonly Pick<Category, "slug" | "name">[];
  /** The shop's pickup spots. */
  spots: readonly PickupPoint[];
  campusName: string;
};

/**
 * The listing form (selling spec 2.10, plan decision 26): every field of the listing model, always shown (nothing can
 * hide them without JavaScript), with hints saying which apply; the errors at the top and by each field; the photos;
 * and Save and publish (Save changes on a paused or sold listing) and Save as draft.
 */
export function ListingFormView(props: ListingFormViewProps) {
  const { listingId, state, waiting, values, photos, errors, categories, spots, campusName } = props;
  const prefix = listingFieldPrefix(listingId);
  const ids = listingFieldIds(prefix);
  const errorOf = (field: keyof typeof ids) => errorFor(errors, ids[field]);
  const priceError = errorOf("price");
  const publishLabel = state === "paused" || state === "sold" ? "Save changes" : "Save and publish";
  return (
    <>
      <ErrorSummary errors={errors} />
      {waiting && (
        <p className="form-notice">
          Your shop is waiting for the Hub. Save your listing as a draft now, and publish it once your shop is
          approved.
        </p>
      )}
      <form action={saveListingForm} noValidate className="form-stack">
        {/* Enter in a field presses the form's first submit button, so a hidden copy of the save comes before any
            photo's buttons (which are submit buttons too). */}
        <button type="submit" name="intent" value={waiting ? "draft" : "publish"} tabIndex={-1} aria-hidden="true" className="sr-only">
          Save
        </button>
        {listingId && <input type="hidden" name="listing" value={listingId} />}
        <RadioGroup
          id={ids.kind}
          name="kind"
          legend="What are you listing?"
          options={KIND_CHOICES}
          defaultValue={values.kind ?? ""}
          error={errorOf("kind")}
          row
        />
        <TextField
          id={ids.title}
          name="title"
          label="Title"
          hint="Up to 70 characters, such as “Casio fx-991ZA Plus calculator”."
          defaultValue={values.title ?? ""}
          error={errorOf("title")}
          required
        />
        <SelectField
          id={ids.category}
          name="category"
          label="Category"
          options={categories.map((category) => ({ value: category.slug, label: category.name }))}
          placeholder="Choose a category"
          defaultValue={values.category ?? ""}
          error={errorOf("category")}
        />
        <SelectField
          id={ids.condition}
          name="condition"
          label="Condition (items only)"
          hint="Leave it for a service."
          options={CONDITION_CHOICES}
          placeholder="Choose a condition"
          defaultValue={values.condition ?? ""}
          error={errorOf("condition")}
          required={false}
        />
        <div className="form-field">
          <label htmlFor={ids.price} className="form-label">
            Price in rand
          </label>
          <p id={`${ids.price}-hint`} className="form-hint">
            Whole rand, such as 250. Leave it empty if you only swap.
          </p>
          <FieldError id={`${ids.price}-error`} message={priceError} />
          <div className="sell-price">
            <span aria-hidden="true">R</span>
            <input
              id={ids.price}
              name="price"
              type="text"
              inputMode="numeric"
              autoComplete="off"
              defaultValue={values.price ?? ""}
              aria-invalid={priceError ? true : undefined}
              aria-describedby={describedBy(`${ids.price}-hint`, priceError && `${ids.price}-error`)}
              className="form-input"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="form-check">
            <input
              id={`${prefix}-free`}
              name="free"
              type="checkbox"
              value="yes"
              defaultChecked={values.free === "yes"}
              className="form-checkbox"
            />
            <label htmlFor={`${prefix}-free`}>It&apos;s free</label>
          </div>
          <div className="form-check">
            <input
              id={`${prefix}-from`}
              name="from"
              type="checkbox"
              value="yes"
              defaultChecked={values.from === "yes"}
              className="form-checkbox"
            />
            <label htmlFor={`${prefix}-from`}>This is a starting price (services)</label>
          </div>
        </div>
        <RadioGroup
          id={ids.payMode}
          name="payMode"
          legend="How do you want to be paid?"
          options={PAY_CHOICES}
          defaultValue={values.payMode ?? ""}
          error={errorOf("payMode")}
          row
        />
        <TextField
          id={ids.swapFor}
          name="swapFor"
          label="What would you swap for?"
          hint="Only if you take swaps. Up to 200 characters, such as “a mechanical keyboard”."
          defaultValue={values.swapFor ?? ""}
          error={errorOf("swapFor")}
        />
        <TextField
          id={ids.description}
          name="description"
          label="Description"
          hint="What's included, sizes, how long it takes. Up to 1,000 characters. Line breaks are kept."
          defaultValue={values.description ?? ""}
          error={errorOf("description")}
          required
          multiline
        />
        <RadioGroup
          id={ids.handover}
          name="handover"
          legend="How does it change hands?"
          hint={`At ${campusName}, your shop's campus.`}
          options={spots.length > 0 ? HANDOVER_CHOICES : HANDOVER_CHOICES.slice(1)}
          defaultValue={values.handover ?? ""}
          error={errorOf("handover")}
        />
        {spots.length > 0 && (
          <SelectField
            id={ids.pickup}
            name="pickup"
            label="Pickup point"
            hint="For a pickup: where buyers collect it."
            options={spots.map((spot) => ({ value: spot.id, label: spot.name }))}
            placeholder="Choose a point"
            defaultValue={values.pickup ?? ""}
            error={errorOf("pickup")}
            required={false}
          />
        )}
        {/* Keyed by the server's photos: after a save that failed, the photos just uploaded come back as saved ones,
            and the field starts again from them rather than keeping files the form has already sent. */}
        <PhotoField
          key={photos.map((photo) => photo.src).join(" ")}
          prefix={prefix}
          photos={photos}
          error={errorOf("photos")}
        />
        <div className="sell-form-buttons">
          {!waiting && (
            <button type="submit" name="intent" value="publish" className="btn btn-primary form-submit">
              {publishLabel}
            </button>
          )}
          <button
            type="submit"
            name="intent"
            value="draft"
            className={waiting ? "btn btn-primary form-submit" : "btn btn-outline text-navy"}
          >
            Save as draft
          </button>
          <Link href={myListingsPath(state ? tabFor(state) : "available")} prefetch={false} className="link-button">
            Cancel
          </Link>
        </div>
      </form>
    </>
  );
}
```

Create `components/sell/ListingForm.tsx`:

```tsx
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { readFormState } from "@/lib/auth/form-state";
import { getCampuses, getCategories, getPickupPoints } from "@/lib/data";
import { getOwnListing } from "@/lib/data/own-listings";
import type { ListingRow } from "@/lib/data/rows";
import { isId } from "@/lib/messaging/paths";
import { listingFieldIds, listingFieldPrefix, sellingErrors } from "@/lib/selling/errors";
import { listingForm } from "@/lib/selling/form-state";
import { requireShopOwner } from "@/lib/selling/owner";
import { editListingPath, NEW_LISTING_PATH } from "@/lib/selling/paths";
import { thumbSrc } from "@/lib/selling/thumbs";
import { ListingFormView } from "./ListingFormView";

/** The form's values for a listing, or a new one's: an item, for cash, picked up at the shop's first pickup point. */
function valuesOf(listing: ListingRow | null, firstSpot: string | null): Record<string, string> {
  if (!listing) return { kind: "item", payMode: "cash", handover: firstSpot ? "pickup" : "online", pickup: firstSpot ?? "" };
  return {
    kind: listing.kind,
    title: listing.title,
    category: listing.categorySlug,
    condition: listing.condition ?? "",
    price: listing.priceZar ? String(listing.priceZar) : "",
    free: listing.priceZar === 0 ? "yes" : "",
    from: listing.priceIsFrom ? "yes" : "",
    payMode: listing.payMode,
    swapFor: listing.swapFor ?? "",
    description: listing.description,
    handover: listing.handover,
    pickup: listing.pickupPointId ?? "",
    photos: listing.photos.map((photo) => photo.src).join("\n"),
    ...Object.fromEntries(listing.photos.map((photo) => [`alt:${photo.src}`, photo.alt])),
  };
}

/**
 * The listing form for this seller (selling spec 2.10 and section 3): a new listing, or one of theirs to edit, with
 * what was typed before a failed save or a photo button (plan decision 9). Request-time; another shop's listing, or
 * none, is the 404.
 */
export async function ListingForm({ params }: { params?: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const id = params ? (await params).id : null;
  const owner = await requireShopOwner(id ? editListingPath(id) : NEW_LISTING_PATH);
  const listing = id && isId(id) ? await getOwnListing(owner.shop.slug, id) : null;
  if (id && !listing) notFound();
  const [state, categories, campuses, pickupPoints] = await Promise.all([
    readFormState(listingForm(listing?.id ?? null)),
    getCategories(),
    getCampuses(),
    getPickupPoints(),
  ]);
  const spots = pickupPoints.filter((point) => owner.shop.pickupPointIds.includes(point.id));
  const values = state ? state.values : valuesOf(listing, spots[0]?.id ?? null);
  const photos = (values.photos ?? "")
    .split("\n")
    .filter(Boolean)
    .map((src) => ({ src, alt: values[`alt:${src}`] ?? "", thumb: thumbSrc(src) }));
  return (
    <ListingFormView
      listingId={listing?.id ?? null}
      state={listing?.state ?? null}
      waiting={owner.status === "pending"}
      values={values}
      photos={photos}
      errors={sellingErrors(state?.errors ?? [], listingFieldIds(listingFieldPrefix(listing?.id ?? null)))}
      categories={categories}
      spots={spots}
      campusName={campuses.find((campus) => campus.slug === owner.shop.campusSlug)?.name ?? ""}
    />
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Measure the two pages, signed in**

In `tests/e2e/performance.spec.ts`, add at the end of the file (if D or E made a helper for the signed-in measure, use it with the sample seller instead of repeating the steps):

```ts

// The listing form's two pages (selling plan, Task 12): the only routes with new client code, measured signed in as
// the sample seller. Each signs in in a browser context of its own and copies only the session cookie across, so
// nothing is cached yet.
const LISTING_FORMS = [
  ["new listing form", "/sell/listings/new", "New listing"],
  ["edit listing form", "/sell/listings/l-001/edit", "Edit listing"],
] as const;

for (const [name, path, heading] of LISTING_FORMS) {
  test(`a first visit to the ${name}, signed in, stays inside the JavaScript budget`, async ({
    page,
    context,
    browser,
    baseURL,
  }) => {
    test.skip(test.info().project.name !== "phone-390", "Measured once, at phone size");
    const other = await browser.newContext();
    const signingIn = await other.newPage();
    await signingIn.goto(`${baseURL}/login`);
    await signingIn.locator("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Seller, Tech corner", exact: true }).click();
    await signingIn.waitForURL(/\/account$/);
    await context.addCookies((await other.cookies()).filter((cookie) => cookie.name === "vossie_session"));
    await other.close();

    let scriptBrotli = 0;
    const pending: Array<Promise<void>> = [];
    page.on("response", (response) => {
      if (response.request().resourceType() !== "script") return;
      pending.push(
        response.body().then((body) => {
          scriptBrotli += brotliCompressSync(body, { params: { [constants.BROTLI_PARAM_QUALITY]: 5 } }).length;
        }),
      );
    });
    await page.goto(path, { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(heading);
    await expect(page.getByLabel("Add photos")).toBeVisible();
    await Promise.all(pending);
    console.log(`${name} (signed in): ${JSON.stringify({ scriptBrotli })}`);
    expect(scriptBrotli, "JavaScript, brotli as a host serves it").toBeLessThanOrEqual(150 * 1024);
  });
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { campuses } from "@/data/fixtures/campuses";
import { categories } from "@/data/fixtures/categories";
import { pickupPoints } from "@/data/fixtures/pickup-points";
import { sampleSellerRows } from "@/data/fixtures/shops";
import { resetSampleStore } from "@/lib/data/store";
import { redirectOf } from "@/tests/helpers/next-request";
import { ShopProfile } from "./ShopProfile";
import { ShopProfileView } from "./ShopProfileView";

const viewer = vi.hoisted(() => ({ id: null as string | null }));

vi.mock("next/navigation", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextNavigation());
vi.mock("next/server", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextServer());
vi.mock("@/lib/auth/session", async () => {
  const { getAccount } = await import("@/lib/data/accounts");
  const { redirect } = await import("next/navigation");
  const { signInHref } = await import("@/lib/next-path");
  const getViewer = async () => (viewer.id ? getAccount(viewer.id) : null);
  return {
    getViewer,
    getSessionKey: async () => null,
    requireAccount: async (next: string) => (await getViewer()) ?? redirect(signInHref(next)),
  };
});
vi.mock("@/lib/selling/shop-actions", () => ({ saveShopProfile: async () => {} }));

const row = (slug: string) => structuredClone(sampleSellerRows.find((item) => item.slug === slug)!);

const show = (overrides: Partial<Parameters<typeof ShopProfileView>[0]> = {}) =>
  render(
    <ShopProfileView
      shop={row("tech-corner")}
      status="approved"
      typed={null}
      errors={[]}
      notice={null}
      campuses={campuses}
      categories={categories}
      pickupPoints={pickupPoints}
      host="vossie.test"
      {...overrides}
    />,
  );

beforeEach(() => {
  resetSampleStore();
  viewer.id = null;
});

describe("Edit shop (selling spec 2.11)", () => {
  it("fills the wizard's fields from the shop, all on one page", () => {
    show();
    expect(screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Shop name" })).toHaveValue("Tech corner");
    expect(screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Main category" })).toHaveValue("tech");
    expect((screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "About your shop" }) as HTMLTextAreaElement).value).toMatch(
      /^I test every calculator/,
    );
    const spots = screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Pickup points at Midrand" });
    expect(within(spots).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Library entrance" })).toBeChecked();
    expect(within(spots).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Main reception" })).toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Remove the photo and use my category's picture" })).not.toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save changes" })).toHaveAttribute("type", "submit");
  });

  it("lets an approved shop change its link once, warning that old links stop working", () => {
    const { unmount } = show();
    const slug = screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Your shop's link" });
    expect(slug).toHaveValue("tech-corner");
    expect(slug).toHaveAccessibleDescription(/Links to the old address stop working/);
    unmount();
    show({ shop: { ...row("tech-corner"), slugChanged: true } });
    expect(screen.queryByRole("textbox", { name: "Your shop's link" })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByText("vossie.test/sellers/tech-corner")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("You've already changed your link once.")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("tells a shop waiting for approval that its link comes with approval", () => {
    show({ shop: row("crochet-corner"), status: "pending" });
    expect(screen.queryByRole("textbox", { name: "Your shop's link" })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Your shop's link is set when the Hub approves it/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows what was typed and the errors after a save that went wrong, and the notice after one that worked", () => {
    const failed = show({
      typed: { name: "", tagline: "", category: "tech", about: "Hi", campus: "pretoria", points: "", slug: "tech-corner" },
      errors: [{ message: "Your campus changed, so choose your pickup points there.", fieldId: "profile-points" }],
    });
    expect(screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Shop name" })).toHaveValue("");
    expect(screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Pickup points at Pretoria" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("alert", { name: "There's a problem" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    failed.unmount();
    show({ notice: "Your shop is saved." });
    expect(screen.getByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("Your shop is saved.");
  });

  it("shows the signed-in seller's own shop, and sends anyone without one to /sell", async () => {
    viewer.id = "acc-seller";
    render(await ShopProfile());
    expect(screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Shop name" })).toHaveValue("Tech corner");
    viewer.id = "acc-student";
    expect(await redirectOf(() => ShopProfile())).toBe("/sell");
  });
});
```

Create `components/sell/ShopProfileView.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { ErrorSummary } from "@/components/forms/ErrorSummary";
import { describedBy, FieldError } from "@/components/forms/FieldError";
import { errorFor, type FormError } from "@/lib/auth/messages";
import type { SellerRow } from "@/lib/data/rows";
import { shopFieldIds } from "@/lib/selling/errors";
import { SELL_PATH } from "@/lib/selling/paths";
import { saveShopProfile } from "@/lib/selling/shop-actions";
import type { Campus, Category, PickupPoint, SellerStatus } from "@/lib/types";
import { AboutField, CampusSpotsFields, NameFields, ShopPhotoField } from "./ShopFields";

type ShopProfileViewProps = {
  shop: SellerRow;
  status: SellerStatus;
  /** What was typed before a save that went wrong, or null to show the shop as it is. */
  typed: Readonly<Record<string, string>> | null;
  errors: readonly FormError[];
  notice: string | null;
  campuses: readonly Pick<Campus, "slug" | "name">[];
  categories: readonly Pick<Category, "slug" | "name">[];
  pickupPoints: readonly PickupPoint[];
  /** The site's host, from siteUrl(), for the link's beginning. */
  host: string;
};

const IDS = shopFieldIds("profile");

/**
 * Edit shop (selling spec 2.11): the wizard's fields on one page, then the shop's link, which an approved or paused
 * shop may change once (plan decision 16). Save changes comes first among the form's submit buttons, so Enter saves.
 */
export function ShopProfileView(props: ShopProfileViewProps) {
  const { shop, status, typed, errors, notice, campuses, categories, pickupPoints, host } = props;
  const value = (key: string, fallback: string) => typed?.[key] ?? fallback;
  const chosen = typed ? (typed.points ?? "").split(" ").filter(Boolean) : shop.pickupPointIds;
  return (
    <>
      {notice && (
        <p role="status" className="form-notice">
          {notice}
        </p>
      )}
      <ErrorSummary errors={errors} />
      <form action={saveShopProfile} noValidate className="form-stack">
        <NameFields
          ids={IDS}
          errors={errors}
          name={value("name", shop.name)}
          tagline={value("tagline", shop.tagline)}
          categorySlug={value("category", shop.categorySlug)}
          categories={categories}
        />
        <AboutField ids={IDS} errors={errors} about={value("about", shop.about)} />
        <ShopPhotoField
          ids={IDS}
          errors={errors}
          current={shop.image?.src ?? null}
          categorySlug={shop.categorySlug}
          remove="checkbox"
        />
        <CampusSpotsFields
          ids={IDS}
          errors={errors}
          campusSlug={value("campus", shop.campusSlug)}
          chosen={chosen}
          campuses={campuses}
          pickupPoints={pickupPoints}
        />
        <LinkField shop={shop} status={status} typed={value("slug", shop.slug)} host={host} errors={errors} />
        <div className="sell-form-buttons">
          <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary form-submit">
            Save changes
          </button>
          <Link href={SELL_PATH} prefetch={false} className="link-button">
            Cancel
          </Link>
        </div>
      </form>
    </>
  );
}

/** The shop's link (selling spec 2.11): set on approval, then changeable once, with the warning. */
function LinkField({
  shop,
  status,
  typed,
  host,
  errors,
}: {
  shop: SellerRow;
  status: SellerStatus;
  typed: string;
  host: string;
  errors: readonly FormError[];
}) {
  if (status === "pending") {
    return (
      <div className="form-field">
        <p className="form-label">Your shop&apos;s link</p>
        <p className="form-hint">Your shop&apos;s link is set when the Hub approves it.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (shop.slugChanged) {
    return (
      <div className="form-field">
        <p className="form-label">Your shop&apos;s link</p>
        <p className="sell-slug-now">{`${host}/sellers/${shop.slug}`}</p>
        <p className="form-hint">You&apos;ve already changed your link once.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }
  const error = errorFor(errors, IDS.slug);
  return (
    <div className="form-field">
      <label htmlFor={IDS.slug} className="form-label">
        Your shop&apos;s link
      </label>
      <p id={`${IDS.slug}-hint`} className="form-hint">
        The end of your shop&apos;s address. You can change it once. Links to the old address stop working, so anyone
        who saved one will need the new one.
      </p>
      <FieldError id={`${IDS.slug}-error`} message={error} />
      <div className="sell-slug">
        <span aria-hidden="true">{`${host}/sellers/`}</span>
        <input
          id={IDS.slug}
          name="slug"
          type="text"
          defaultValue={typed}
          autoComplete="off"
          spellCheck={false}
          aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
          aria-describedby={describedBy(`${IDS.slug}-hint`, error && `${IDS.slug}-error`)}
          className="form-input"
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `components/sell/ShopProfile.tsx`:

```tsx
import { readFormState } from "@/lib/auth/form-state";
import { getCampuses, getCategories, getPickupPoints } from "@/lib/data";
import { sellingErrors, shopFieldIds } from "@/lib/selling/errors";
import { PROFILE_FORM } from "@/lib/selling/form-state";
import { noticeText } from "@/lib/selling/notices";
import { requireShopOwner } from "@/lib/selling/owner";
import { PROFILE_PATH } from "@/lib/selling/paths";
import { siteUrl } from "@/lib/site-url";
import { ShopProfileView } from "./ShopProfileView";

/** Edit shop for this seller (selling spec 2.11 and section 3): request-time, as it reads the session. */
export async function ShopProfile() {
  const owner = await requireShopOwner(PROFILE_PATH);
  const [state, campuses, categories, pickupPoints] = await Promise.all([
    readFormState(PROFILE_FORM),
    getCampuses(),
    getCategories(),
    getPickupPoints(),
  ]);
  // After a save that went wrong, the form shows what was typed; otherwise the shop as it is.
  const typed = state && state.errors.length > 0 ? state.values : null;
  return (
    <ShopProfileView
      shop={owner.shop}
      status={owner.status}
      typed={typed}
      errors={sellingErrors(state?.errors ?? [], shopFieldIds("profile"))}
      notice={noticeText(state?.notice)}
      campuses={campuses}
      categories={categories}
      pickupPoints={pickupPoints}
      host={new URL(siteUrl()).host}
    />
  );
}
```

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Edit your shop", robots: { index: false } };

/** Edit shop (selling spec 2.11): the heading is prerendered; the form streams in. */
export default function ShopProfilePage() {

    <div className="page sell-page">

        </header>

}

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the owner's links**

Create `lib/selling/links.ts`:

```ts
import { getViewer } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { getListingDetail } from "@/lib/data";
import type { MessageTarget } from "@/lib/messaging/paths";
import { editListingPath, PROFILE_PATH } from "./paths";

/**
 * The owner's link in Message seller's place (selling spec 2.12, plan decision 19): Edit listing on their own listing,
 * Edit shop on their own storefront. Null for everyone else. Request-time: it reads the session, so the component
 * that calls it sits inside <Suspense>.
 */
export async function ownerLinkFor(target: MessageTarget): Promise<{ href: string; label: string } | null> {
  const shopSlug = (await getViewer())?.seller?.sellerSlug ?? null;
  if (!shopSlug) return null;
  if (target.kind === "shop") return target.sellerSlug === shopSlug ? { href: PROFILE_PATH, label: "Edit shop" } : null;
  const detail = await getListingDetail(target.listingId);
  return detail?.listing.sellerSlug === shopSlug
    ? { href: editListingPath(target.listingId), label: "Edit listing" }
    : null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the helpers**

Create `tests/e2e/helpers/selling.ts` (Playwright only runs `*.spec.ts`, so this file is never run on its own):

```ts
import { expect, type Page } from "@playwright/test";
import { pngBytes } from "../../helpers/image-bytes";
import { button, field, main, project } from "./messaging";

// Shared by the selling tests (selling plan, Task 16). Every test shares one server and one sample store, so each
// names what it makes with a word no other test uses (plan decision 25).

/** A word no other test uses, for a shop's, a student's or a listing's name. */
export function uniqueWord(): string {
  return `${project()}${Date.now().toString(36)}${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");
}

/** A new student who'll apply to sell. */
export function newSeller(): { email: string; firstName: string } {
  const word = uniqueWord();
  return { email: `sell-${word}@eduvos.com`, firstName: `Zola ${word.slice(-6)}` };
}

/** A real PNG whose metadata holds a stand-in location (tests/helpers/image-bytes.ts), as a file to pick. */
export function photoWithLocation(name = "photo.png") {
  return { name, mimeType: "image/png", buffer: Buffer.from(pngBytes({ width: 64, height: 48, exif: true, text: true })) };
}

/** Fills the listing form that's showing with a R150 item in good condition, kept for pickup at the first point. */
export async function fillListing(page: Page, title: string) {
  await field(page, "Title").fill(title);
  await field(page, "Category").selectOption({ label: "Res & living" });
  await field(page, "Condition (items only)").selectOption({ label: "Good" });
  await field(page, "Price in rand").fill("150");
  await field(page, "Description").fill("Quiet and strong.\nCollect it at the library.");
}

/**
 * Publishes a new listing as the signed-in seller, with these photos, and gives back its id. With the app's
 * JavaScript, it waits for the photo field to have the photos ready first (plan decision 10).
 */
export async function publishListing(
  page: Page,
  title: string,
  { photos = [] as ReturnType<typeof photoWithLocation>[], js = true } = {},
): Promise<string> {
  await page.goto("/sell/listings/new");
  await fillListing(page, title);
  if (photos.length > 0) {
    await field(page, "Add photos").setInputFiles(photos);
    if (js) await expect(main(page).getByText("Photo 1 (the cover)").filter({ visible: true })).toBeVisible();
  }
  await button(page, "Save and publish").click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sell\/listings$/);
  const href = await main(page).getByRole("link", { name: `Edit ${title}` }).getAttribute("href");
  return /\/sell\/listings\/(l-[0-9a-f-]{36})\/edit$/.exec(href ?? "")![1];
}

/** The listing cards on the page that's showing with this title. */
export const cardsFor = (page: Page, title: string) =>
  page.getByRole("article").filter({ hasText: title }).filter({ visible: true });

/** The 404 page's heading. */
export const notFoundHeading = (page: Page) =>
  page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "We couldn't find that page" });
```

import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";

import { fillListing, newSeller, notFoundHeading, photoWithLocation, uniqueWord } from "./helpers/selling";

const BOTH = ["phone-390", "desktop-1440"];

test.describe("selling (selling spec section 3)", () => {
  test("/sell pitches to a visitor, and Start selling goes through sign-in to the wizard", async ({ page }) => {
    onlyAt(...BOTH);

    await expect(page).toHaveTitle("Sell on Vossie · Vossie");

  });

  }) => {

    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sell\/onboarding$/);

    await expect(main(page).getByText("Step 1 of 5: Basics")).toBeVisible();

    await expect(page.getByRole("alert", { name: "There's a problem" })).toBeVisible();

    await button(page, "Continue").click();

    await field(page, "About your shop").fill("I bake between lectures.\nMuffins on Mondays.");
    await button(page, "Continue").click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/step=3$/);
    await field(page, "Shop photo (optional)").setInputFiles(photoWithLocation("shop.png"));
    await button(page, "Continue").click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/step=4$/);
    await main(page).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Library entrance" }).check();
    await button(page, "Continue").click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/step=5$/);

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the tests that change what buyers see**

Create `tests/e2e/selling-writes.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { containsText, GPS } from "../helpers/image-bytes";
import { button, field, main, signInAsSample } from "./helpers/messaging";
import { cardsFor, fillListing, notFoundHeading, photoWithLocation, publishListing, uniqueWord } from "./helpers/selling";

// These run in the selling-writes project, after the five device projects (selling plan decision 25). Each uses the
// sample seller and touches only its own new listing, found by a title no other test uses.

const browseFor = (title: string) => `/browse?q=${encodeURIComponent(title)}&campus=all`;
const photoAddresses = (page: import("@playwright/test").Page) =>
  page.locator('main input[name="photo"]').evaluateAll((inputs) => inputs.map((input) => (input as HTMLInputElement).value));

test("a new listing with a photo shows in browse, on its page and on the storefront straight away", async ({
  page,
  request,
}) => {
  const title = `Desk fan ${uniqueWord()}`;
  await signInAsSample(page, "Seller, Tech corner", "/sell");
  const id = await publishListing(page, title, { photos: [photoWithLocation("fan.png")] });
  await expect(main(page).getByRole("status")).toContainText("Published. Buyers can find it now.");

  await page.goto(browseFor(title));
  await expect(cardsFor(page, title)).toHaveCount(1);

  await page.goto(`/listings/${id}`);
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(title);
  const cover = page.getByRole("region", { name: "Photos" }).getByRole("img").first();
  await expect(cover).toHaveAttribute("src", /\/_next\/image\?url=%2Fphotos%2Fph-/);
  await expect.poll(() => cover.evaluate((image: HTMLImageElement) => image.naturalWidth)).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  // The browser re-encoded it as WebP, which left its hidden data behind (spec 2.3).
  const src = decodeURIComponent(/url=([^&]+)/.exec((await cover.getAttribute("src")) ?? "")![1]);
  const photo = await request.get(src);
  expect(photo.headers()["content-type"]).toBe("image/webp");
  expect(containsText(await photo.body(), GPS)).toBe(false);

  await page.goto("/sellers/tech-corner");
  await expect(cardsFor(page, title)).toHaveCount(1);
});

test("edit, pause, resume, mark sold and delete, each seen by a buyer", async ({ page, browser, baseURL }) => {
  const title = `Kettle ${uniqueWord()}`;
  const renamed = `${title} white`;
  await signInAsSample(page, "Seller, Tech corner", "/sell");
  const id = await publishListing(page, title);
  const buyer = await (await browser.newContext({ baseURL })).newPage();

  await page.goto(`/sell/listings/${id}/edit`);
  await field(page, "Title").fill(renamed);
  await button(page, "Save and publish").click();
  await expect(main(page).getByRole("status")).toContainText("Your changes are saved.");
  await buyer.goto(`/listings/${id}`);
  await expect(buyer.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(renamed);

  await main(page).getByRole("button", { name: `Pause ${renamed}` }).click();
  await expect(main(page).getByRole("status")).toContainText("Paused.");
  await buyer.goto(`/listings/${id}`);
  await expect(buyer.getByText("Not taking orders right now").filter({ visible: true })).toBeVisible();
  await buyer.goto(browseFor(renamed));
  await expect(cardsFor(buyer, renamed)).toHaveCount(0);

  await page.goto("/sell/listings?tab=paused");
  await main(page).getByRole("button", { name: `Resume ${renamed}` }).click();
  await expect(main(page).getByRole("status")).toContainText("It's available again.");
  await buyer.goto(browseFor(renamed));
  await expect(cardsFor(buyer, renamed)).toHaveCount(1);

  await page.goto("/sell/listings");
  await main(page).getByRole("button", { name: `Mark sold ${renamed}` }).click();
  await buyer.goto(`/listings/${id}`);
  await expect(buyer.getByText("Sold", { exact: true }).filter({ visible: true })).toBeVisible();

  await page.goto("/sell/listings?tab=sold");
  await main(page).getByRole("link", { name: `Delete ${renamed}` }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText("Delete this listing?");
  await button(page, "Delete listing").click();
  await expect(main(page).getByRole("status")).toHaveText("Deleted.");
  await buyer.goto(`/listings/${id}`);
  await expect(notFoundHeading(buyer)).toBeVisible();
  await buyer.context().close();
});

test("without the app's JavaScript, photos upload, move in a round trip, and lose their hidden data", async ({
  page,
  request,
}) => {
  await page.route("**/_next/static/**/*.js", (route) => route.abort());
  const title = `Desk lamp ${uniqueWord()}`;
  await signInAsSample(page, "Seller, Tech corner", "/sell/listings/new");
  await fillListing(page, title);
  await field(page, "Add photos").setInputFiles([photoWithLocation("front.png"), photoWithLocation("back.png")]);
  await button(page, "Save as draft").click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sell\/listings\?tab=drafts$/);

  await main(page).getByRole("link", { name: `Edit ${title}` }).click();
  const before = await photoAddresses(page);
  expect(before).toHaveLength(2);
  await field(page, "Description").fill("Typed before the move.");
  await main(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Make cover (photo 2)" }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/edit#edit-l-[0-9a-f-]{36}-photos$/);
  expect(await photoAddresses(page)).toEqual([before[1], before[0]]);
  await expect(field(page, "Description")).toHaveValue("Typed before the move.");

  await button(page, "Save and publish").click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sell\/listings$/);
  // Nothing in the browser re-encoded them: they're PNGs, stripped by the server (spec 2.3).
  for (const src of before) {
    const photo = await request.get(src);
    expect(photo.headers()["content-type"]).toBe("image/png");
    expect(containsText(await photo.body(), GPS)).toBe(false);
  }
});

test("Back after publishing shows the storefront with the new listing, not a kept copy", async ({ page }) => {
  const title = `Desk clock ${uniqueWord()}`;
  await signInAsSample(page, "Seller, Tech corner", "/sellers/tech-corner");
  // Client-side navigations from here, so Next keeps the storefront for Back (messaging plan decision 6).
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Edit shop" }).filter({ visible: true }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sell\/profile$/);
  await main(page).getByRole("link", { name: "Your shop" }).filter({ visible: true }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sell$/);
  await main(page).getByRole("link", { name: "New listing" }).filter({ visible: true }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sell\/listings\/new$/);
  await fillListing(page, title);
  await button(page, "Save and publish").click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sell\/listings$/);
  await page.goBack();
  await page.goBack();
  await page.goBack();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sellers\/tech-corner$/);
  await expect(cardsFor(page, title)).toHaveCount(1);
});
```

test("selling review screenshots", async ({ page, browser, baseURL }) => {
  const project = test.info().project.name;
  test.skip(!["phone-390", "desktop-1440"].includes(project), "Saved at 390 and 1440 only");
  const file = (name: string) => `docs/progress/selling/${project}-${name}.png`;
  const main = page.locator("main");
  const shot = (name: string) => page.screenshot({ path: file(name), fullPage: true });
  await page.emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: "reduce" });

  await page.goto("/sell");
  await expect(main.getByRole("link", { name: "Start selling" })).toBeVisible();
  await shot("01-pitch");

  // A new student through the wizard: nothing of the sample accounts changes.
  await page.goto("/login?next=%2Fsell%2Fonboarding");
  await signUpHere(page, newSeller());
  await main.getByRole("button", { name: "Continue", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("alert", { name: "There's a problem" })).toBeVisible();
  await shot("02-wizard-step-1-error");
  await page.getByLabel("Shop name", { exact: true }).fill(`Bakes ${uniqueWord()}`.slice(0, 60));
  await page.getByLabel("Main category", { exact: true }).selectOption({ label: "Food" });
  await main.getByRole("button", { name: "Continue", exact: true }).click();
  await page.getByLabel("About your shop", { exact: true }).fill("I bake between lectures.\nMuffins on Mondays.");
  await main.getByRole("button", { name: "Continue", exact: true }).click();
  await page.getByLabel("Shop photo (optional)", { exact: true }).setInputFiles(photoWithLocation("shop.png"));
  await main.getByRole("button", { name: "Continue", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(main.getByText("Step 4 of 5", { exact: false })).toBeVisible();
  await shot("03-wizard-step-4");
  await main.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Library entrance" }).check();
  await main.getByRole("button", { name: "Continue", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(main.getByRole("region", { name: "Your answers" })).toBeVisible();
  await shot("04-wizard-review");
  await main.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "I've read the seller guidelines" }).check();
  await main.getByRole("button", { name: "Send for approval", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "The Hub is checking your shop" })).toBeVisible();
  await shot("05-waiting");

  // The sample seller in a browser of their own: only read, so the sample stays as it is.
  const context = await browser.newContext({ baseURL, viewport: page.viewportSize()!, reducedMotion: "reduce" });
  const seller = await context.newPage();
  const sellerShot = (name: string) => seller.screenshot({ path: file(name), fullPage: true });
  await signInAsSample(seller, "Seller, Tech corner", "/sell");
  await expect(seller.getByRole("region", { name: "In the last 7 days" })).toBeVisible();
  await sellerShot("06-dashboard");
  await seller.goto("/sell/listings");
  await expect(seller.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Listings by state" })).toBeVisible();
  await sellerShot("07-my-listings");
  await seller.goto("/sell/listings/l-001/edit");
  await expect(seller.locator("main").getByText("Photo 3")).toBeVisible();
  await sellerShot("08-listing-form");
  await seller.goto("/sell/listings/new");
  await seller.locator("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Save and publish", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(seller.getByRole("alert", { name: "There's a problem" })).toBeVisible();
  await sellerShot("09-listing-form-errors");
  await seller.goto("/sell/listings/l-001/delete");
  await sellerShot("10-delete");
  await seller.goto("/sell/profile");
  await sellerShot("11-edit-shop");
  await seller.goto("/listings/l-001");
  await expect(seller.getByRole("link", { name: "Edit listing" }).filter({ visible: true })).toBeVisible();
  await seller.screenshot({ path: file("12-listing-owner") });
  await context.close();
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Bring the spec in line with the build**

In `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-selling-design.md`, set the status to "Built (2026-10-<day>), in sample mode." and add a section at the end, "## 8. Changes on plan review and build", listing where the plan and the build refined the spec (wins over the decisions above):
- A shop's status stays on the owner's seller profile; a waiting shop has a link but no storefront until approval; sample shops with no owner are always open (plan decision 1).
- A paused shop keeps its storefront ("Nothing listed right now"), and its listings' pages show them as paused; they leave every list (plan decision 2).
- Listings have one state: draft, available, paused or sold; Save and publish says Save changes on a paused or sold listing and keeps its state; a sold listing can be relisted; a draft's date is its first publish; drafts pass the same checks (plan decisions 3 and 5).
- The stripper also drops JPEG's APP13 (IPTC) and comments, PNG's zTXt and iTXt, and anything after the image's end (plan decision 11); resized photos aren't kept on the server's disk (plan decision 12).
- The photo field is one list of saved and new photos; without the app's JavaScript its buttons post the form; Safari's photos are re-encoded as JPEG (plan decisions 9 and 10).
- The wizard's Back is a link; step 4 comes back with the new campus's pickup points; Send for approval goes to the first step with a problem (plan decision 15).
- A waiting shop's link follows its name; the one change of link carries the shop's listings, conversations and reports with it; a new campus moves the shop's listings (plan decisions 16 and 17).
- The dashboard counts the last 7 days, enquiries about listings only, and "replied within 48 hours" as "3 of 4" (plan decision 18).
- The end-to-end tests that change listings run after all others; no test approves a shop (approval is stage G's), so listing flows use the sample seller (plan decision 25).
- Anything else from this plan's "Changes made during the build".
